Result: 在 GRAB 和 ARCTIC 两个基准数据集上，JointHOI 在文本遵循性（text adherence）和物理合理性（physical plausibility）方面均一致优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过将接触建模为与运动联合生成的内模态并引入接触引导采样，JointHOI 有效解决了手物交互生成中的接触不一致问题，为文本驱动的HOI生成提供了一种更简洁且物理上更可信的单阶段方案。

Abstract: Text driven hand object interaction (HOI) generation is gaining attention for immersive applications and robotics, yet producing physically plausible interactions remains challenging. Even when individual motions appear natural, small contact errors can cause conspicuous artifacts such as floating and interpenetration. Prior methods mitigate these issues using explicit contact cues or implicit grasp priors, but typically rely on multi stage pipelines and fail to model temporally evolving contact. We present JointHOI, a single stage diffusion framework that jointly generates 3D hand object motion and dynamic, distance based contact maps from text. By treating contact as an auxiliary inner modality, joint generation enables the model to learn contact motion coupling during training. At inference, contact guided sampling enforces consistency between generated contact maps and motion implied geometry, improving temporal stability and reducing penetration and floating. Experiments on GRAB and ARCTIC demonstrate consistent improvements in text adherence and physical plausibility over prior methods.

</details>


### [35] [LLM-Empowered Multimodal Fusion Framework for Autonomous Driving: Semantic Enhancement and Channel-Adaptive Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01772)
*Wen Wang,Yaping Sun,Yejun He,Hao Chen,Zhiyong Chen,Xiaodong Xu,Nan Ma,Shuguang Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LM-SCIP框架，以大语言模型为核心推理引擎，通过通道自适应语义模块动态融合视觉与雷达数据，在低信噪比下实现鲁棒的视觉主导回退，在高信噪比下实现协同融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中，视觉-雷达融合质量受遮挡、恶劣天气和通道噪声等动态变化的输入质量影响，传统静态数据融合方法难以应对，需要一种通道感知的语义推理方法。

Method: 提出LM-SCIP框架：(1) 层级式雷达-视觉编码器；(2) 通道自适应语义模块(CASM)，将链路指标映射为"通道提示"以动态门控外部雷达特征；(3) 参数高效的LoRA微调LLM结合异构混合专家(H-MoE)，在局部视觉线索与通道条件化的雷达上下文之间进行仲裁；(4) 解耦多任务解码器输出定位、轨迹预测和图像重建。

Result: 在nuScenes上，控制雷达输入开关条件下，LM-SCIP相比纯视觉基线定位RMSE降低40.0%；在VIRAT上，达到0.214m定位RMSE和0.179m minFDE(k=1)。

Conclusion: LM-SCIP成功实现了低SNR下的鲁棒视觉主导回退和高SNR下的协同融合，验证了以LLM为核心进行通道感知语义推理进行多模态融合的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-radar fusion is central to robust autonomous driving, combining dense visual semantics with precise range and velocity measurements from radar. However, real-world fusion quality is fundamentally challenged by dynamically varying input quality, stemming from occlusion, adverse weather, and channel noise. To address this, we re-frame the problem from static data fusion to channel-aware semantic reasoning and propose a Large Language Model-centric Semantic-layer Channel-aware Integrated Perception (LM-SCIP) framework. It places a Large Language Model (LLM) as a central reasoning core to fuse a local visual stream with a quality-varying external radar stream used to cover perception-blind spots. Concretely, LM-SCIP couples a hierarchical radar-vision encoder with a Channel-Adaptive Semantic Module (CASM) that maps link indicators into a "Channel Prompt" to dynamically gate external radar features. A parameter-efficient, LoRA-tuned LLM, in conjunction with a heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts (H-MoE), then arbitrates between local visual cues and the channel-conditioned radar context. Finally, a decoupled multi-task decoder outputs localization, trajectory forecasting, and image reconstruction. Experiments on nuScenes and VIRAT validate our approach. On nuScenes, under a controlled toggle of radar input, LM-SCIP reduces localization RMSE by 40.0% versus a vision-only baseline. On VIRAT, the model attains a 0.214m localization RMSE and 0.179m minFDE (k=1). These results reveal that the proposed LM-SCIP enables a robust vision-dominant fallback at low SNR and synergistic fusion at high SNR.

</details>


### [36] [SpaceEra++: A Unified Framework Towards 3D Spatial Reasoning in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01784)
*Weili Guan,Haoyu Zhang,Meng Liu,Qianlong Xiang,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceEra++ 是对 SpaceEra 框架的全面扩展，通过引入 ScenePick（帧采样策略）缓解扫描视频输入不足，以及 SpaceAlign（空间对齐）强化成对物体约束，从数据构建、模型设计、训练优化到提示推理全链路提升了视觉语言模型的 3D 空间理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型受限于 2D 观测带来的空间不确定性和 3D 空间理解数据稀缺。原 SpaceEra 虽取得显著提升，但仍存在扫描视频输入信息不足和推理约束薄弱两大瓶颈，亟需系统性改进。

Method: 提出 SpaceEra++ 综合框架，涵盖四大环节：(1) ScenePick：一种平衡空间覆盖与物体语义的帧采样策略，生成紧凑且全面的场景表征；(2) SpaceAlign：联合利用绝对坐标与相对空间关系，施加成对物体约束，使优化目标与空间精度对齐；(3) 完整的训练优化与提示推理管线。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SpaceEra++ 相较强基线取得一致性的性能提升；消融实验验证了 ScenePick 和 SpaceAlign 各自及联合的贡献；进一步分析为未来研究提供了指导意见。

Conclusion: SpaceEra++ 通过数据层面和推理层面的双重改进，有效弥补了原框架的不足，显著提升了 VLM 的视觉空间理解能力，并为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Visual-spatial understanding, defined as the ability to infer object relationships and scene layouts from visual inputs, is fundamental to downstream tasks such as robotic navigation and embodied interaction. However, pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) remain constrained by spatial uncertainty stemming from inherently 2D observations and by the scarcity of data for 3D spatial understanding. To address these limitations, we proposed a novel framework, SpaceEra, in the NeurIPS 2025 Spotlight paper. Although it achieved significant performance gains, we further observed that its effectiveness is hindered by insufficient input from scanning videos and weak reasoning constraints. To tackle these newly emerged challenges, we extend the original framework into a comprehensive system, termed SpaceEra++, which spans data construction, model design, training optimization, and prompting inference. Specifically, to alleviate input insufficiency, we introduce ScenePick, a frame sampling strategy that balances spatial coverage with object semantics to produce compact yet comprehensive scene representations. In addition, to enhance spatial reasoning, we develop SpaceAlign, which enforces pairwise object constraints by jointly exploiting absolute coordinates and relative spatial relations, thereby aligning optimization with spatial accuracy. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, while ablation studies validate both the individual and joint contributions of each component, and further analyses provide guidance for future research.

</details>


### [37] [PixGS: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Direct 3D Gaussian Splat Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01803)
*Duy Cao,Phong Nguyen-Ha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixGS 提出一种单阶段、基于像素空间扩散的直接3DGS生成方法，解决了现有方法依赖多阶段级联管线导致的解码伪影和误差累积问题，通过直接去噪3D高斯属性并结合全面的监督策略（法线、深度、高频结构），在保持快速推理的同时超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D内容生成方法存在两个核心瓶颈：一是2D生成器带来的视图不一致性，二是高质量3D数据稀缺。当前主流方案将预训练的文生图潜空间扩散模型适配用于3DGS生成，但这类方法依赖复杂的多阶段级联管线，计算开销大且可扩展性受限。更关键的是，生成质量受限于各组件容量和有损压缩的潜空间，导致解码伪影和误差累积。因此，需要一种能绕过有损潜空间压缩、直接生成高质量3DGS的单阶段方法。

Method: PixGS 的核心思路是利用像素空间扩散模型替代潜空间扩散模型，直接在像素空间对3D高斯属性（位置、颜色、协方差等）进行逐时间步去噪，绕过潜空间的有损压缩。具体而言：（1）采用单阶段管线直接生成3DGS，无需级联多个模型；（2）在像素空间而非潜空间进行去噪，实现对 appearance 和 geometry 的精确 splat 级正则化；（3）引入全面的监督策略，整合表面法线、深度图和高频结构信息，弥补以往方法在这些几何与细节监督信号上的缺失。

Result: 实验表明，PixGS 在生成质量上超越了当前最先进的方法，同时保持了极快的推理速度——在单张A100 GPU上仅需1秒，为多阶段生成管线提供了一种鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: PixGS 验证了像素空间扩散在3DGS直接生成中的可行性与优越性：通过绕过潜空间压缩、实施 splat 级正则化以及引入多维度几何监督，单阶段管线即可在质量和效率上全面超越复杂的多阶段方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D content generation from text or images have achieved impressive results, yet view inconsistency from 2D generators and the scarcity of high-quality 3D data remain significant bottlenecks. Existing solutions typically adapt large-scale pre-trained text-to-image latent diffusion models to generate 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS). However, these approaches often rely on training complex cascade pipelines that are computationally expensive and scalability-limited. Most critically, the quality of generated 3D assets is inherently constrained by each component capacity and compressed latent space, leading to decoding artifacts and accumulated errors. To address these limitations, we propose PixGS, a single-stage pipeline for direct high-quality 3DGS generation, which leverages recent advances in pixel-space diffusion to bypass lossy latent compression while still benefiting from the vast 2D generative priors. By directly denoising 3D Gaussian attributes at each timestep, our method enables precise, splat-level regularization of both appearance and geometry. Furthermore, we introduce a comprehensive supervision strategy that incorporates surface normals, depth, and high-frequency structural information, which is often overlooked in prior works. Experiments demonstrate that PixGS outperforms current state-of-the-art methods while maintaining a fast inference speed (1s on a single A100 GPU), offering a robust and efficient alternative to multi-stage generation pipelines.

</details>


### [38] [MMBench-Live: A Continuously Evolving Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01813)
*Yuanzhi Liu,Shousheng Zhao,Bo Zhou,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMBench-Live 是一个基于多智能体自动化流水线的持续演进多模态评测基准，通过任务引导的数据构建框架，结合分布一致性更新策略，实现低成本、高效率的基准更新，同时保持跨版本排名稳定性和语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多模态评测基准是静态的，面临时效性退化、数据污染和高昂维护成本等问题，亟需一种可持续演进的基准构建范式。

Method: 提出多智能体驱动的自动化流水线，将基准演进视为任务引导的数据集构建过程，包含：① 结构化基准规格定义；② 反馈控制的实时数据采集；③ 可执行推理验证的QA生成。同时引入分布一致性更新策略，从原始基准中提取任务相关视觉模式以指导数据采集与过滤，确保跨版本可比性。

Result: 从MMBench实例化生成了5.9K新评测样本，答案正确率高，每次更新仅需约30美元和1-2小时。评测显示MMBench-Live保持了稳定的模型排名、与原始基准的语义对齐，且表现出更弱的数据污染记忆信号。

Conclusion: MMBench-Live为可持续多模态基准演进提供了一种实用且可扩展的范式，有效缓解了静态基准的固有问题。

Abstract: Evaluation benchmarks are essential for assessing vision-language models (VLMs), but most multimodal benchmarks are static, making them vulnerable to temporal staleness, data contamination, and costly maintenance. We present MMBench-Live, a continuously evolving multimodal benchmark built by a multi-agent-driven automated pipeline. Our framework treats benchmark evolution as task-guided dataset construction, integrating structured benchmark specification, feedback-controlled real-time data acquisition, and verifiable QA generation with executable reasoning. To maintain cross-version comparability, we introduce a distribution-consistent update strategy that extracts task-related visual patterns from the original benchmark to guide data collection and filtering. Instantiated from MMBench, MMBench-Live contains 5.9K newly generated evaluation instances with a high answer correctness rate, while each update costs about USD 30 and takes 1-2 hours. Extensive evaluations show that MMBench-Live preserves stable model rankings, maintains semantic alignment with the original benchmark, and exhibits weaker contamination-related memorization signals, suggesting a practical and scalable paradigm for sustainable multimodal benchmark evolution. The project is available at https://github.com/PRIS-CV/MMBench-Live.

</details>


### [39] [Rethinking Conditional Generation for Underwater Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01825)
*Hua Li,Yongjie Weng,Yutong Li,Zhiyuan Li,Runmin Cong,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCGNet提出用于水下显著目标检测，通过动态多粒度模块、水下物理先验模块和水下空间高斯模块构建退化感知的条件生成网络，结合扩散Transformer瓶颈，在多个数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像因散射和吸收效应导致低对比度、不均匀照明和颜色失真，传统显著目标检测方法在水下环境中效果有限，需要专门针对水下退化特性设计检测方法。

Method: 提出DCGNet，包含四个核心组件：(1)动态多粒度模块(DMG)，模拟人类视觉系统检测不同尺度及模糊边界的显著目标；(2)水下物理先验模块(UPP)，利用伪深度引导估计水下光衰减和反向散射，恢复退化感知的RGB特征；(3)水下空间高斯模块(USG)，从最强引导响应构建空间高斯显著先验，增强目标中心区域并抑制杂乱背景；(4)轻量级时间步自适应扩散Transformer瓶颈(DiT)，在去噪解码器中精炼不同扩散时间步的融合特征。

Result: 在USOD10K、USOD、CSOD10K、MAS3K和RMAS五个数据集上，DCGNet显著超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DCGNet通过退化感知的条件生成策略有效解决了水下显著目标检测的核心挑战，验证了其在复杂水下视觉应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Salient Object Detection in underwater images remains challenging due to low contrast, uneven illumination, and color distortion caused by scattering and absorption effects, which limit the effectiveness of conventional SOD methods in underwater environments. To address these challenges, we propose a Degradation-aware Conditional Generation Network (DCGNet), specifically designed to construct reliable conditional features for underwater saliency generation. First, we design a Dynamic Multi-Granularity module (DMG) grounded in the human visual system to robustly detect salient objects of varying scales with blurred boundaries. Then, we develop an Underwater Physics-Prior module (UPP), which utilizes pseudo-depth guidance to estimate underwater light attenuation and backscatter, thereby restoring degradation-aware RGB features and mitigating color distortion and boundary ambiguity. Based on the physics-guided representation, we introduce an Underwater Spatial Gaussian module (USG), which constructs a spatial Gaussian saliency prior from the strongest guided response to enhance object-centered salient regions and suppress cluttered underwater backgrounds. In addition, a lightweight timestep-adaptive Diffusion Transformer (DiT) bottleneck is inserted into the denoising decoder to refine fused features at different diffusion timesteps. Comprehensive experiments on USOD10K, USOD, CSOD10K, MAS3K, and RMAS demonstrate that DCGNet significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, verifying its potential for complex underwater visual applications.

</details>


### [40] [C2E: Boosting Ego-Only 3D Object Detection via Multi-Teacher Contrastive Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01827)
*Jinlong Wang,Xun Huang,Qiming Xia,Shijia Zhao,Chenglu Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的C2E（协同感知到自感知）范式，通过多对单（M2S）智能体对比知识蒸馏框架，使单智能体检测器在推理时无需通信即可获得协同感知级别的性能，解决了通信成本高和位姿累积误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单车感知（Eo-Perception）受限于视角和遮挡，而多智能体协同感知（Co-Perception）虽然性能优异，但面临通信成本高和累积位姿误差两大瓶颈。作者希望探索一种C2E范式，在推理阶段保留协同感知的优异性能同时避免其固有缺陷。

Method: 提出M2S（Multi-to-Single）对比知识蒸馏框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 多层级特征增强模块（Multi-Level Feature Enhancement），提供更稳定的特征；(2) 辅助点云重建机制（Auxiliary Point Cloud Reconstruction），缓解点云域差异；(3) 多教师对比蒸馏机制（Multi-Teacher Contrastive Distillation），缓解特征分布域差异。

Result: 在V2XSet、V2V4Real和DAIR-V2X三个数据集上验证，与SOTA协同感知模型CoSDH及其他优秀3D检测器结合时，M2S框架在无任何通信成本的情况下，3D mAP最高提升8.64%。

Conclusion: M2S框架成功实现了C2E范式，使单智能体检测器在推理时无需通信即可达到协同感知的性能水平，兼顾了高性能与低延迟，具有良好的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D object detection is essential for autonomous driving systems. However, traditional Ego-only Perception (Eo-Perception) suffers from limited perspective and occlusions in a complex outdoor environment, leading to performance bottlenecks. Recently, research on multi-agent Collaborative Perception (Co-Perception) has demonstrated excellent performance, but high communication costs and accumulated pose error hinder its application. To address this, we explore a novel C2E (Co-Perception to Eo-Perception) paradigm through the Multi-to-Single (M2S) agent contrastive knowledge distillation framework. Our M2S framework first designs Multi-Level Feature Enhancement module to provide more stable features, and introduces Auxiliary Point Cloud Reconstruction and Multi-Teacher Contrastive Distillation mechanisms to mitigate domain gaps in point cloud and feature distributions within the C2E paradigm. Benefiting from this, our M2S can retain the excellent performance of collaborative perception while effectively avoiding the drawbacks, such as communication delays and positioning errors. Extensive experiments on the V2XSet, V2V4Real and DAIR-V2X datasets show the effectiveness and generalizability of our M2S framework when combined with the state-of-the-art CoSDH model and other excellent 3D detectors. Our M2S framework can deliver up to a 8.64% improvement in 3D mAP performance without introducing any communication costs.

</details>


### [41] [Geometric Foundation Model Distillation for Efficient Lunar 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01851)
*Clémentine Grethen,Florient Chouteau,Géraldine Morin,Simone Gasparini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过知识蒸馏将大型3D基础模型（如MASt3R）压缩为轻量级模型，以月球立体重建为案例，提出基于SVD的结构化初始化方法，实现了7倍模型压缩且性能基本保持，并总结出蒸馏几何基础模型的多项实用原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型3D基础模型（如MASt3R）虽在立体重建上性能卓越，但计算需求高，难以部署在资源严格受限的场景（如行星探测器的星载计算）。因此亟需探索在保持重建精度的前提下对这些模型进行有效压缩的方法。

Method: 以月球影像上微调的688M参数MASt3R为教师模型，通过知识蒸馏将其密集几何预测迁移到轻量级学生模型。学生模型覆盖不同编码器类型（CNN vs ViT）、解码器宽度与深度及训练策略。为弥合师生模型间的维度差异，提出基于结构化SVD的初始化方法，将教师解码器权重投影到学生更小的隐空间，提供有效热启动以改善收敛与最终性能。

Result: 在月球数据上，蒸馏后的学生模型在保留教师大部分重建精度的同时，模型尺寸缩小达7倍，且优于直接用稀疏真值标注训练的基线模型。

Conclusion: 研究得出多项蒸馏几何基础模型的关键启示：卷积编码器性能不及Transformer（但预训练可用性仍是混淆因素）；保留编码器容量比维持大解码器更关键；特征级蒸馏一致优于仅输出监督；SVD初始化可提升优化稳定性。这些发现为在资源受限环境中部署3D重建模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Large 3D foundation models such as MASt3R achieve state-of-the-art stereo reconstruction but are computationally demanding for deployment under strict hardware constraints -- a critical limitation in domains such as planetary exploration, where onboard computing is severely restricted. We study how far such models can be compressed through knowledge distillation, using lunar stereo reconstruction as a challenging and practically relevant case study. Starting from a 688M-parameter MASt3R teacher fine-tuned on lunar imagery, we distill its dense geometric predictions into a family of lightweight students spanning different encoder types (CNN vs ViT), decoder widths and depths, and training strategies. To bridge the dimensional mismatch between teacher and student, we propose a structured SVD-based initialization that projects the teacher's decoder weights into the student's smaller latent space, yielding a warm start that significantly improves convergence and final performance. Based on our results on lunar data, we can obtain a distilled student that retains most of teacher's reconstruction accuracy while reducing the model size up to 7 times, and even outperforms a baseline trained directly with sparse ground-truth annotations. Beyond compression, our study highlights both principles and practical insights for distilling geometric foundation models: a convolutional encoder underperforms transformer-based alternatives (though pretraining availability remains a confounding factor), preserving encoder capacity is more critical than maintaining a large decoder, feature-level distillation consistently outperforms output-only supervision, and SVD-based initialization improves optimisation stability. These findings provide practical guidelines for deploying 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [42] [QWERTY: Training-Free Motion Control via Query-Warped Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01869)
*Kyobin Choo,Youngmin Kim,Hyunkyung Han,Geunrip Park,Chanyoung Kim,Sunyoung Jung,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QWERTY 是一种无需训练的运动控制框架，通过对预训练图像到视频 DiT 中的 query 进行 warping 操作，利用用户定义的目标变形和光流来实现灵活的运动控制，并提出基于自引导的潜变量优化来提升控制稳定性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 DiT 的运动控制主要依赖文本提示，隐式且效率低；基于微调的空间提示方法虽能显式控制，但数据与计算成本高且可能损害生成能力；而针对 U-Net 的无训练运动控制方法在 DiT 上尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 QWERTY 框架：(1) 通过用户定义的物体变形和光流，对 DiT 中 3D 全注意力的 query 帧不变语义子空间进行 warping 操作；(2) 发现 query-warped DiT 预测的噪声可自然引导扩散轨迹朝向期望运动；(3) 将该噪声作为自引导信号进行潜变量优化，提升控制稳定性与视觉质量。

Result: 在最新的图像到视频 DiT 上，QWERTY 在现有无训练方法中实现了最有效的运动控制，性能与基于微调的方法相当。

Conclusion: QWERTY 首次在 DiT 架构上实现了无需训练的运动控制，其 query-warping 与自引导优化策略证明了通过操纵注意力机制即可有效引导预训练 DiT 的运动生成，性能媲美微调方法。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (DiTs) generate high-fidelity and temporally coherent videos, yet motion control remains implicit, primarily relying on text prompts. As a result, achieving desired motion often requires extensive prompt engineering and repeated resampling. While fine-tuning models with additional spatial prompts (e.g., bounding boxes or point trajectories) enables explicit control, it demands substantial data curation and computation, and may compromise the generative capabilities of pretrained models. Consequently, training-free motion control using such spatial prompts has been explored in U-Net-based video diffusion models, but remains largely unexplored for DiTs. We introduce QWERTY, a training-free framework that enables flexible motion control in pretrained image-to-video DiTs via user-defined object warping and optical flow. We carefully manipulate the 3D full attention of DiTs by warping the frame-invariant semantic subspace of queries. We find that the noise predicted by the query-warped DiT naturally guides the diffusion trajectory toward the desired motion, and further show that leveraging this noise as self-guidance for latent optimization improves control stability and visual quality. Experiments show that QWERTY achieves the most effective motion control among existing training-free approaches on a recent image-to-video DiT, with performance comparable to fine-tuning-based methods.

</details>


### [43] [Descriptor: LYNRED Mobility Dataset Multimodal Detection Subset (LYNRED-MDS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01871)
*Loïc Arbez,Jessy Matias,Xavier Brenière,Jocelyn Chanussot,Ronald Phlypo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LYNRED-MDS数据集，一个包含4000对RGB-红外图像的多模态检测子集，覆盖多样化天气、光照和道路条件，旨在推进低能见度条件下早期碰撞预测研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道路安全系统侧重碰撞后损害最小化，而早期碰撞预测（尤其在夜间、雾天等低能见度条件下）依赖热红外感知。但现有RGB-红外数据集（如FLIR ADAS、LLVIP）场景过于简单且多为晴好天气，缺乏对边缘案例的覆盖，限制了可靠驾驶视觉系统的开发。

Method: 作者构建LYNRED-MDS数据集，从LYNRED Mobility Dataset中抽取4000对RGB-红外图像，数据采集自法国格勒诺布尔周边，涵盖城市、乡村、山区等多种驾驶场景及多样化天气和光照条件。使用YOLOv8n基线模型进行跨数据集热红外评估，验证数据集的泛化潜力。

Result: 跨数据集评估表明，LYNRED-MDS在驾驶场景下的行人检测任务中展现出较强的泛化潜力，尤其在边缘案例覆盖方面优于现有基准数据集。

Conclusion: LYNRED-MDS通过覆盖关键边缘案例，能够支持开发更可靠、可部署的高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS）视觉系统，推动低能见度条件下早期碰撞预测技术的发展。

Abstract: Current road safety systems primarily focus on minimizing post-collision damage. However, advances in algorithmic perception are shifting focus toward early collision prediction, especially in lowvisibility conditions like nighttime or fog, where thermal infrared sensing outperforms both human vision and RGB imaging. While available RGB-infrared datasets such as FLIR ADAS and LLVIP are good benchmarks, they mostly consist of clear weather and overly simple scenarios. In this article, we introduce the LYNRED-MDS: Multimodal Detection Subset, a subset of the LYNRED Mobility Dataset, comprised of 4000 RGB-infrared image pairs captured under diverse weather, lighting, and road conditions around Grenoble, France. Our dataset spans varied driving contexts (urban, rural, mountainous, etc.) and a vehicle fleet compliant with Western European standards. Thermal cross-dataset evaluation using a YOLOv8n baseline suggests that our dataset offers strong generalization potential for pedestrian detection in driving scenarios. By covering critical edge cases, our dataset supports the development of more reliable and deployable vision systems for advanced driver-assistance systems.

</details>


### [44] [SAB-LVLM: Significance-Aware Binarization for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01876)
*Qi Lyu,Jiahua Dong,Baichen Liu,Xudong Wang,Mingfei Han,Yulun Zhang,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出显著性感知二值化方法SAB-LVLM，通过构建跨层和跨模态的显著性二值化映射图，以交替显著性加权更新方案对大型视觉语言模型进行约1-bit压缩，在保持性能的同时大幅降低存储和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二值化方法忽略了不同层和不同模态间权重的差异性，导致与下游任务无关的参数被保留、而模态关键权重未被充分优化，造成显著性能退化。因此需要一种能够感知跨层、跨模态权重显著性的二值化策略。

Method: （1）为文本和视觉输入分别构建Hessian矩阵，提出空间显著性图来区分单模态激活和跨模态激活的全精度权重；（2）设计模态引导的整合策略，生成显著性感知的二值化映射图，度量权重在层和模态间的显著性；（3）将该映射图作为误差重加权项引入二值化目标函数，并通过交替显著性加权更新方案进行二值化拟合。

Result: 在约1-bit压缩约束下，SAB-LVLM在多项实验中显著优于现有的二值化后训练量化（PTQ）方法。

Conclusion: SAB-LVLM通过显著性感知的二值化策略，有效解决了LVLM跨模态压缩中权重重要性差异问题，在极低比特压缩下实现了优越的性能保持，为资源受限设备上的LVLM部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in multimodal understanding, yet their enormous parameter scale and cross-modal computation incur substantial memory and latency overhead, severely limiting real-world deployment on resource-constrained devices. Binarization offers an attractive solution by drastically reducing storage and computational costs. However, existing binarization methods neglect the varying importance of weights across different layers and modalities. This causes parameters irrelevant to downstream tasks to be unnecessarily retained, whereas modality-critical weights may not be adequately optimized, resulting in significant performance degradation. To address these challenges, we develop a novel \underline{S}ignificance-\underline{A}ware \underline{B}inarization for \underline{L}arge \underline{V}ision-\underline{L}anguage \underline{M}odels (SAB-LVLM). Specifically, after constructing Hessian matrices for textual and visual inputs, we propose a spatial significance map to distinguish full-precision weights activated under a single modality from those activated across modalities. We then devise a modality-guided integration strategy to obtain the significance-aware binarization map, which measures weight significance across layers and modalities. Subsequently, this binarization map is incorporated into the binarization objective as an error reweighting term, and binarization fitting is performed through an alternating significance-weighted update scheme. Extensive experiments illustrate the superiority of our SAB-LVLM over existing binary PTQ methods under an approximately 1-bit compression constraint. Our code is accessible at https://github.com/LyuQi127/SAB_LVLM.

</details>


### [45] [Diversity-aware View Partitioning for Scalable VGGT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01885)
*Jinsoo Park,Donggyu Choi,Ahyun Seo,Minsu cho,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、即插即用的VGGT推理框架，通过基于视觉差异性和空间分散度的图划分将视图组织为多样性感知的平衡块，从而在不牺牲几何精度的情况下实现可扩展的多视图重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT等几何Transformer因全局注意力的二次复杂度难以扩展至大规模视图集合；同时，实验发现仅增加视图数量而缺乏足够的视角多样性反而会降低重建质量，因为冗余视图引入高度相似的token，稀释了注意力机制中的几何信息信号。

Method: 提出一种训练无关、即插即用的推理框架：（1）通过组合图划分（combinatorial graph partitioning），同时考虑视觉差异性和空间分散度，将视图组织为多样性感知的平衡块；（2）为在不进行完整位姿估计的情况下近似空间关系，提出基于少量种子帧视觉相似性的软位姿传播策略来估计空间分散度。

Result: 在相机位姿估计、多视图深度预测和3D重建任务上均取得性能提升，同时降低了内存使用和推理延迟；该框架可与现有VGGT变体互补，实现可扩展的多视图重建并保持几何保真度。

Conclusion: 通过多样性感知的视图分块策略，有效缓解了VGGT在大规模视图场景下注意力稀释和计算开销问题，为可扩展的多视图几何重建提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Geometry transformers such as VGGT achieve strong performance by jointly reasoning over multiple views with global attention. However, scaling them to large view collections remains challenging due to the quadratic cost of attention. Moreover, our empirical analysis reveals that the reconstruction quality in VGGT is sensitive to the distribution of viewpoints. Simply increasing the number of views without sufficient viewpoint diversity can even degrade performance, as redundant views introduce highly similar tokens that dilute informative geometric signals in the attention mechanism. Motivated by this observation, we propose a training-free and plug-and-play VGGT inference framework that organizes views into diversity-aware balanced chunks. The chunks are constructed through combinatorial graph partitioning over visual dissimilarity and spatial dispersion. This view organization allows the transformer to focus attention on geometrically informative views while reducing redundant attention interactions. To estimate spatial dispersion without full pose estimation, we approximate spatial relationships via a soft pose propagation strategy based on visual similarity from a small set of seed frames. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved performance in camera pose estimation, multi-view depth prediction, and 3D reconstruction while reducing memory usage and inference latency. Our framework also complements existing VGGT variants, enabling scalable multi-view reconstruction without sacrificing geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [46] [FoundDP: Revisiting Weak Disparity Observability in Dual-Pixel Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01900)
*Fengchen He,Hao Xu,Dayang Zhao,Tingwei Quan,Shaoqun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoundDP提出一个统一框架，将双像素(DP)的度量深度信息与单目深度基础模型的全局结构先验相结合，通过视觉Transformer特征对齐解决弱视差区域的结构退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP成像的基线极小，导致在无纹理、低对比度或降采样区域视差可观测性不足，现有方法仅依赖局部视差线索在此类场景下不可靠。需要一个既能保持度量尺度又能恢复结构一致性的方法。

Method: 提出统一框架：(1)利用DP导出的深度保持度量尺度；(2)借助Vision Transformer(ViT)特征恢复弱视差区域的结构一致性；(3)识别并缓解DP散焦模糊导致的ViT表征退化，通过ViT特征对齐实现稳定的度量引导深度估计。

Result: 在合成和真实DP基准数据集上，FoundDP在结构保真度和度量精度方面均取得一致提升，尤其在弱视差可观测条件下表现优越。

Conclusion: FoundDP通过融合DP度量深度与单目深度基础模型的结构先验，并辅以ViT特征对齐解决散焦模糊带来的表征退化，有效克服了DP在弱视差区域的结构退化问题，实现了鲁棒且准确的度量深度估计。

Abstract: Dual-pixel (DP) imaging enables metric depth estimation from a single camera using sub-aperture disparity. However, the extremely small effective baseline limits disparity observability, leading to structural degradation and depth failure in textureless, low-contrast, or downsampled regions. Existing DP-based methods rely primarily on local disparity cues and therefore become unreliable when disparity signals are weak or ambiguous. To address this limitation, we propose \emph{FoundDP}, a unified framework that integrates metric DP depth with global structural priors from a monocular depth foundation model. Our method preserves metric scale through DP-derived depth and leverages Vision Transformer (ViT) features to restore structural consistency in weak-disparity regions. To ensure reliable metric guidance under DP imaging conditions, we identify and mitigate ViT representation degradation induced by DP defocus blur via ViT feature alignment, enabling stable metric-guided depth estimation. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world DP benchmarks show that FoundDP delivers superior performance, with consistent gains in structural fidelity and metric accuracy, especially under reduced disparity observability. Code will be available at: https://github.com/EchoLighting/FoundDP

</details>


### [47] [Rethinking Post-Hoc Calibration in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01902)
*Tristan Kirscher,Kim-Celine Kahl,Balint Kovacs,Maximilian R. Rokuss,Klaus Maier-Hein,Xavier Coubez,Philippe Meyer,Sylvain Faisan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了语义分割中后处理校准的两个结构性问题：(1) logit平移不变性——校准器应对logit的任意常数偏移不敏感；(2) 决策保持校准——校准不应改变argmax或排序结果以避免损害分割性能。作者提出平移不变(TI)校准器设计和类别条件仿射校准器，在保持决策的同时提升校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割模型普遍存在校准不良问题，后处理校准虽实用，但在密集预测中存在两个被忽视的结构性问题：一是标准校准器对logit的常数偏移敏感，导致同一预测分布产生不同校准结果；二是基于似然的校准目标与分割任务的Dice等指标不匹配，校准可能改变类别排序，反而降低分割质量。

Method: 1. 定义平移不变(TI)校准器：要求输出在logit加常数偏移下不变，分析常见校准器(温度缩放、矩阵/向量缩放、Dirichlet等)的TI性质，为位移敏感校准器构造TI对应版本。2. 引入决策保持约束：argmax保持和顺序保持，证明仿射softmax校准器在此约束下退化为温度缩放；进而提出类别条件仿射校准器(class-conditional affine calibrators)，在满足决策保持的同时保留更强的表达能力，用于量化校准-分割的权衡。

Result: 在自然图像和医学分割基准上以及分布偏移条件下，匹配对比实验表明：平移不变变体普遍提升校准指标(如ECE)；决策保持变体防止了分割性能退化，同时保持强校准性能。类别条件仿射校准器在argmax保持下可超越温度缩放。

Conclusion: 良好的后处理校准应满足两个设计原则：平移不变性以避免对logit表示的任意依赖，以及决策保持以避免损害分割性能。类别条件仿射校准器是实现决策保持校准的有效方案，兼顾了表达能力和分割质量。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimates are essential in semantic segmentation, especially in safety-critical settings where overconfident errors can mislead downstream decisions. Yet modern segmentation models often remain miscalibrated. Post-hoc calibration offers a practical way to correct confidence estimates without retraining the segmentation model, but its use in dense prediction raises structural issues that are often overlooked. We study two such issues. First, adding a constant to all logits leaves the softmax probabilities unchanged, but several standard calibrators can still depend on this arbitrary offset. As a result, two logit representations encoding the same predictive distribution may yield different calibrated probabilities. We define translation-invariant (TI) calibrators as those whose outputs are unchanged under such shifts, characterize which common calibrators satisfy this property, and construct TI counterparts of shift-sensitive calibrators to isolate the effect of removing representation dependence. Second, post-hoc calibration is typically fitted by minimizing a likelihood-based objective, whereas segmentation models are trained with task-specific metrics such as Dice. This mismatch can cause calibration to alter class orderings and degrade the deployed segmentation map. We study decision-preserving calibration under argmax- and order-preservation constraints. Since enforcing these constraints collapses affine softmax calibrators to temperature scaling, we introduce class-conditional affine calibrators that can be made argmax- or order-preserving while retaining greater expressivity, allowing us to quantify the calibration-segmentation trade-off induced by decision preservation. Across natural-image and medical segmentation benchmarks, and under corruption-based covariate shift, matched comparisons show that TI variants generally improve calibration metrics, while decision-preserving variants prevent segmentation degradation and retain strong calibration performance. These results provide practical design principles for well-defined post-hoc calibration pipelines in semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [48] [SFKD: Spatial--Frequency Joint-Aware Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation via Multi-Level Wavelet Spectral Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01906)
*Cuipeng Wang,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SFKD框架，利用小波变换与傅里叶表示的互补特性，通过多级离散小波变换显式解耦空间信息，结合双流双阶段精炼模块和高斯滤波频率损失，在保持空间信息的同时实现异构模型间的知识蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构知识蒸馏方法因异构模型间的归纳偏置差异导致空间分布不一致，往往弱化或丢弃空间信息。但空间信息同时编码了可迁移的全局结构语义和架构特定的局部细节，不应被直接忽略。因此需要一种能有效利用异构表示中空间信息的方法。

Method: 提出SFKD框架：(1)利用小波变换的空间局部性与傅里叶表示的全局能量分布互补特性；(2)应用多级离散小波变换显式解耦空间信息，生成小波子带；(3)通过双流双阶段精炼模块进一步精炼子带特征；(4)结合高斯滤波的频率损失选择性捕获有信息量的全局信息。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，在同构和异构模型设定下均进行了广泛实验，结果表明SFKD方法优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SFKD通过空间-频率联合感知的方式，有效保留了异构表示中的空间信息，既捕捉了全局结构语义又保留了局部细节，显著提升了异构知识蒸馏的性能。

Abstract: Most existing knowledge distillation methods focus on homogeneous models (e.g., CNN-to-CNN), thereby overlooking the flexibility and potential of knowledge transfer across heterogeneous models. Due to intrinsic inductive bias discrepancies between heterogeneous models that cause spatial distribution inconsistencies, prior heterogeneous distillation methods often weaken or discard spatial information in heterogeneous representations. However, the spatial information in representations often encodes transferable global structural semantics as well as architecture-specific local details, and therefore should not be directly ignored. To better leverage the spatial information encoded in heterogeneous representations, we propose a Spatial-Frequency Joint-Aware Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation framework (SFKD). By leveraging the complementary properties of wavelet transform spatial locality and Fourier representations in characterizing global energy distributions, we first apply multi-level discrete wavelet transform to explicitly decouple spatial information. The resulting wavelet sub-bands are further refined by a dual-stream dual-stage refinement module, and finally combined with a Gaussian-filtered frequency loss to selectively capture informative global information. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets under both homogeneous and heterogeneous models demonstrate the superiority of our method.

</details>


### [49] [Towards Real-World Ultrasound Understanding: Large Vision-Language Models from Multi-Image Examinations with Long-Form Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01908)
*Bingcong Yan,Chunlei Li,Jingliang Hu,Yilei Shi,Xiao Xiang Zhu,Lichao Mou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出，大规模真实超声检查数据（150万次检查、1770万张图像）与检查级别的临床数据对齐（多图-报告配对），配合标准LVLM的LoRA微调，即可在超声理解任务上超越此前更复杂的方法，无需特殊架构或训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在医学影像任务中表现强劲，但在超声领域的应用仍然有限，原因在于超声成像固有的复杂性和变异性。现有方法多依赖复杂架构或精心设计的训练策略，作者希望探究：真正实现真实世界超声理解的关键因素究竟是什么。

Method: (1) 构建大规模数据集：包含150万次真实超声检查、1770万张图像，覆盖多器官，并配对未经整理的临床报告；(2) 以检查（examination）为粒度组织数据，将多张图像与对应报告对齐，真实反映临床工作流程；(3) 使用低秩适应（LoRA）对标准LVLM进行微调，不引入任何任务特定的架构修改或训练策略。

Result: 该简单方案在多种超声理解任务上取得了优异表现，超越了此前采用更复杂流水线设计的方法。此外，模型和数据的缩放分析揭示了规模在超声LVLM中的作用。

Conclusion: 实现真实世界超声理解的关键不在于复杂的架构设计或训练策略，而在于数据规模（scale）和临床忠实的数据对齐方式。以检查级别组织多图-报告配对，是构建超声LVLM的有效路径。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved strong performance across many medical imaging tasks, yet their application to ultrasound remains limited due to its inherent complexity and variability. In this work, we revisit what is truly needed to enable real-world ultrasound understanding. Instead of introducing complex architectures or elaborate training strategies, we show that data scale and clinically faithful data alignment are the key factors. We construct a large-scale dataset of 1.5M real-world ultrasound examinations, containing 17.7M images, multi-organ coverage, and paired uncurated clinical reports. Crucially, we organize the data at the examination level, aligning multiple images with their corresponding reports to reflect real clinical workflows. We then fine-tune a standard LVLM using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) on this dataset without task-specific modifications. Surprisingly, this simple recipe already leads to strong performance across diverse ultrasound understanding tasks, outperforming prior methods designed with more complex pipelines. Beyond these results, we present model and data scaling analyses that provide insights into the role of scale in ultrasound LVLMs.

</details>


### [50] [Robust Image Processing Techniques for Construction Environment Monitoring Using Underwater Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01915)
*Seunghee Yun,Geonmo Yang,Juhui Lee,Changbeom Park,Jeahyung Choi,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向水下机器人施工环境监测的鲁棒图像处理框架，通过分阶段建模深度感知的前向散射模糊和真实海洋雪花粒子退化，生成高保真合成数据来重训练现有网络，有效缩小合成-真实域差距，在韩国沿海数据集上取得一致的视觉质量与UIQM提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水下图像处理方法主要关注吸收和后向散射，但真实水下图像还受到深度相关的前向散射模糊和海洋雪花等粒子退化的严重影响。现有合成数据与真实场景之间存在显著的域差距，限制了水下机器人视觉系统的实际应用效果。

Method: 提出分阶段处理流水线：1) 背景退化建模——利用深度感知的前向散射模拟；2) 前景退化建模——从真实图像中提取真实海洋雪花模式进行合成。生成的合成数据用于重训练现有Joint-ID网络（不修改架构，以隔离评估数据集真实性），并附加轻量级后处理方案增强对比度和结构清晰度。

Result: 在韩国沿海环境采集的真实水下数据集上进行实验，视觉质量和UIQM评分均获得一致提升，验证了显式建模前向散射和真实粒子效应对缩小合成-真实域差距的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模前向散射和真实粒子效应能够有效缩小合成数据与真实水下场景之间的域差距，提升水下机器人实际部署中的图像处理鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a robust image processing framework for underwater robot-based construction environment monitoring, targeting complex degradations observed in real marine environments. Unlike conventional approaches that mainly consider absorption and backscattering, real underwater imagery is strongly affected by depth-dependent forward scattering blur and particle-induced degradations such as marine snow. To address this, we introduce a staged processing pipeline that sequentially models background degradation via depth-aware forward scattering and foreground degradation using realistic marine snow patterns extracted from real images. The resulting synthetic data are used to retrain an existing Joint-ID network without modifying its architecture, enabling an isolated evaluation of dataset realism. In addition, a lightweight post-processing scheme is applied to enhance contrast and structural clarity. Experiments on real underwater datasets collected in Korean coastal environments demonstrate consistent improvements in visual quality and UIQM scores. The results indicate that explicitly modeling forward scattering and realistic particle effects effectively reduces the synthetic-to-real gap and improves practical applicability in real-world underwater robotic operations.

</details>


### [51] [Sparse-Aware Vector Quantization for Bandwidth-Efficient Collaborative 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01928)
*Feng Li,Chaokun Zhang,Gong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VQSOP框架，通过稀疏感知向量量化（SAVQ）和双分支自适应空间细化（ASR）模块，在3D语义占用预测的协同感知中实现通信量减少高达82倍，同时保持最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知中感知增益与通信开销之间存在固有权衡，3D语义占用预测依赖细粒度空间结构，现有方法要么将3D特征压缩为2D导致严重空间信息丢失，要么传输密集3D表示难以实际部署。需要一种既能保持完整几何上下文又能大幅降低通信开销的解决方案。

Method: 提出VQSOP框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）SAVQ（Sparse-Aware Vector Quantization）机制，利用3D场景稀疏性对信息丰富区域进行紧凑编码，大幅降低通信量同时保留完整几何上下文；（2）双分支ASR（Adaptive Spatial Refinement）模块，动态融合局部高频细节与广泛上下文语义，增强结构一致性和特征连续性。

Result: 在保持最先进性能水平的同时，通信量降低高达82倍。

Conclusion: VQSOP框架有效解决了协同3D语义占用预测中感知性能与通信效率的矛盾，通过稀疏向量量化和自适应空间细化实现了高效、高质量的协同感知。

Abstract: Collaborative perception extends single-agent perception by enabling multiple vehicles to exchange complementary perceptual information. However, it introduces an inherent trade-off between perception gain and communication overhead, which is particularly severe for 3D semantic occupancy prediction that relies on fine-grained spatial structures. Existing methods typically compress 3D features into 2D, causing severe spatial information loss, or transmit dense 3D representations, hindering real-world deployment. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bandwidth-efficient collaborative Vector Quantization Semantic Occupancy Prediction (VQSOP) framework. VQSOP employs a Sparse-Aware Vector Quantization (SAVQ) mechanism that exploits 3D scene sparsity to compactly encode informative regions, drastically reducing communication overhead while preserving complete geometric context. Furthermore, to enhance structural consistency and feature continuity, we design a Dual-Branch Adaptive Spatial Refinement (ASR) module that dynamically fuses local high-frequency details with broad contextual semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing communication volume by up to 82x.

</details>


### [52] [LiZAD: A Lightweight Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection Framework for Industrial Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01949)
*Uzair Khan,Luigi Capogrosso,Muhammad Aqeel,Francesco Setti,Michele Magno,Marco Cristani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiZAD 是一个专为边缘设备实时零样本异常检测（ZSAD）设计的轻量级框架，通过结合 DINOv3 的密集视觉特征与 MobileCLIP2 的高效文本嵌入，在显著降低计算和内存开销（内存减少61.5%、参数减少74.6%、延迟加速3.02倍）的同时，保持了有竞争力的检测性能（平均 P-AUROC 仅下降6.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高通量工业生产线上产品配置和视觉特征频繁变化，无法为每种新场景收集标注数据；现有 ZSAD 方法虽性能良好但计算量过大，无法部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，因此需要一种轻量级、可实时运行的 ZSAD 方案。

Method: 提出 LiZAD 框架：将 DINOv3 的密集、空间感知的视觉特征（用于精确像素级定位）与 MobileCLIP2 的高计算效率文本嵌入配对，通过低内存的可训练投影头（projection heads）将两者映射到共享潜在空间，实现轻量化零样本异常检测。

Result: 在 VisA、BTAD、MPDD、MVTec-AD 四个数据集上，相比六种最先进的 ZSAD 模型，LiZAD 平均内存占用降低 61.5%，参数量减少 74.6%，延迟加速 3.02 倍；平均 P-AUROC 仅比最佳模型下降 6.4%。成功部署在 NVIDIA Jetson NX 和 Jetson AGX 边缘设备上，并在维罗纳大学 ICE 实验室的真实产线上完成测试验证。

Conclusion: LiZAD 成功在异常检测性能与计算效率之间取得良好平衡，证明了将强视觉特征与高效文本嵌入相结合用于边缘端零样本异常检测的可行性，为资源受限工业场景下的实时缺陷检测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: In modern high-throughput industrial production lines, product configurations and visual characteristics frequently change, making it impractical to collect and annotate data for every new scenario. This dynamic setting makes Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection (ZSAD) particularly suitable, as it enables defect detection without requiring training on target-specific samples. Although recent ZSAD approaches show promising results, they are computationally intensive and thus unsuitable for deployment on resource-constrained devices. We propose LiZAD: a lightweight framework designed for real-time ZSAD specifically tailored for use on edge devices. The proposed approach pairs the dense and spatially aware visual features of DINOv3, crucial for precise pixel-level localization, with the highly computationally efficient text embeddings of MobileCLIP2. These features are then mapped into a shared latent space via low-memory trainable projection heads. Compared to six state-of-the-art ZSAD models, LiZAD achieves an average memory reduction of 61.5%, a parameter reduction of 74.6%, and a speedup of 3.02x in terms of latency. Despite substantial reductions in computational and memory costs, our approach maintains competitive anomaly detection performance, dropping the average P-AUROC by just 6.4% relative to the best state-of-the-art model across the VisA, BTAD, MPDD, and MVTec-AD datasets. Finally, it is successfully deployed on the NVIDIA Jetson NX and Jetson AGX edge devices and tested on the real production line of the Industrial Computer Engineering Laboratory (ICE Lab) at the University of Verona. The code is available at https://github.com/intelligolabs/LiZAD.

</details>


### [53] [Personalized 4D Whole-Heart Mesh Reconstruction from Cine MRI via Multi-Scale Temporal Modeling and Differentiable Contour Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01952)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Dongcheng Cang,Xiaohan Yuan,Mark YY Chan,Ching-Hui Sia,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端框架，从稀疏多视角cine MRI序列直接重建4D全心网格，通过可微分轮廓渲染器和多尺度时间建模模块实现解剖感知监督与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏cine MRI由于2D切片覆盖有限，且心脏形状与运动之间存在复杂耦合，精确重建4D全心网格极具挑战。现有方法多依赖中间轮廓拟合，通常只能重建静态、单相位或局部心脏几何，无法捕捉全腔室动态。

Method: 提出端到端图像到网格映射框架：(1) 基于Beer-Lambert衰减原理的可微分轮廓渲染器，通过轮廓投影损失实现解剖感知的3D+t网格变形监督；(2) 多尺度时间建模模块，融合全局心动周期级动态与局部帧间一致性，生成平滑且生理合理的网格轨迹。

Result: 全心平均绝对误差为1.68±0.31 mm，运动抖动为0.77±0.17 mm/frame³，在重建精度和运动平滑性上均优于现有方法；多视角2D轮廓对齐改善，并支持下游电生理模拟概念验证。

Conclusion: 该方法可从稀疏cine MRI实现高精度、时间平滑的4D全心重建，为心脏数字孪生建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate 4D whole-heart mesh reconstruction from sparse cine MRI is critical for creating cardiac digital twins, but remains challenging due to limited 2D slice coverage and the complex coupling between cardiac shape and motion. Existing methods often rely on intermediate contour fitting and typically reconstruct static, single-phase, or partial cardiac geometries, limiting their ability to capture full-chamber dynamics. We propose a novel end-to-end framework for reconstructing temporally resolved whole-heart meshes from multi-view 2D cine MRI sequences by learning an image-to-mesh mapping. The framework incorporates a differentiable contour renderer inspired by the Beer-Lambert attenuation principle, enabling anatomy-aware supervision of 3D+t mesh deformation through contour-based projection losses. To improve temporal consistency across the cardiac cycle, we further introduce a multi-scale temporal modeling module that integrates global cycle-level dynamics with local inter-frame coherence to generate smooth and physiologically plausible mesh trajectories. The proposed method achieved a whole-heart mean absolute error of 1.68 $\pm$ 0.31 mm and a motion jitter of 0.77 $\pm$ 0.17 $\mathrm{mm}/\mathrm{frame}^{3}$, outperforming existing methods with lower reconstruction error and substantially improved motion smoothness. It also improved 2D contour alignment across multiple cine MRI views and supported downstream proof-of-concept electrophysiological simulation. The code will be released publicly upon acceptance of the manuscript for publication.

</details>


### [54] [NeoMap: Training-free Novel-View Synthesis from Single Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01962)
*Jinxi Li,Tianyi Zhang,Yafei Yang,Zihui Zhang,Peng Huang,Koon Wing Macgyver Lin,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeoMap 是一种无需训练的新视角视频合成框架，通过在预训练视频模型的数据流形上执行收敛的交替投影迭代来优化初始噪声，从而定位高保真、视角一致的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖相机条件注入、任务微调或逐步去噪引导来实现视角对齐，但这些做法往往引入伪影并损害全局场景一致性。核心洞察是：预训练视频模型已在其自然视频流形中编码了优质的 novel view 解，问题在于如何定位该解。

Method: 提出 NeoMap，核心机制为收敛流形交替投影迭代（convergent manifold alternating projection iterations），通过优化初始噪声在预训练视频模型的潜空间中搜索最优 novel view 解，全程无需训练。

Result: 在 Tanks-and-Temples、LLFF 和 DAVIS 三个标准 benchmark 上显著超越所有现有方法，取得 SOTA 的生成保真度和一流的视角一致性。

Conclusion: NeoMap 证明了无需训练、仅通过流形空间优化即可从通用预训练视频模型中提取高质量 novel view 的能力，为单图/单目视频的新视角合成开辟了新范式。

Abstract: We study the challenging problem of novel view video synthesis from single images or monocular videos. Existing methods, which operate under the assumption that pre-trained video models lack native novel view synthesis capability and enforce view alignment via camera conditioning, task-specific fine-tuning, or stepwise hard denoising guidance, often suffer from artifacts and compromised global scene consistency. In this paper, we introduce NeoMap, a novel training-free framework designed to locate high-fidelity, view-consistent novel view solutions from general pre-trained video models. The key to our approach is the core insight that promising novel view solutions are inherently encoded within the natural video data manifold learned by pre-trained models, and the core challenge is simply to locate this optimal solution. We solve this via our core mechanism: convergent manifold alternating projection iterations that optimize the initial noise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeoMap significantly outperforms all existing methods across 3 standard novel view synthesis benchmarks, including the challenging Tanks-and-Temples, LLFF and DAVIS datasets, achieving state-of-the-art generation fidelity and top-tier view consistency.

</details>


### [55] [Assessing VLM Reliability for Medical Image Quality Evaluation Under Corruption and Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01973)
*Sofiane Ouaari,Kevin Vorwalder,Nico Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对16个视觉语言模型（VLM）在医学图像质量评估（MIQA）任务上进行了零样本基准测试，发现像素化等退化严重降低模型评分，而文本属性（如机构声望、设备年限）会显著影响评估结果，揭示了当前VLM在客观性和隐私-可靠性权衡方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在医学任务中的应用日益广泛，但它们在真实世界条件下（图像退化、文本上下文干扰）的行为尚未被充分探索。自动化的MIQA有望减轻工作量，但在部署前需验证VLM的鲁棒性和客观性。

Method: 使用MediMeta-C数据集，在七种退化类型（像素化、亮度等）、五个严重等级下，对16个VLM跨七种医学影像模态进行零样本基准测试。评估维度包括：对退化模式的敏感性、退化对嵌入几何的影响，以及文本属性（人口统计、专业知识、基础设施、机构）对质量评分的影响。

Result: 像素化导致最大评分降幅（平均-20.58%，OCT模态达-34.4%），亮度影响最小（-0.81%）。嵌入位移与评分变化相关，同家族模型间相关性为0.67-0.83，但部分模型对退化乳腺X线评分反升31%。文本属性方面：机构声望提升评分+17.15%，设备年限降低-14.7%。InternVL-8B变化最大达+95.62%，MedGemma则为-37.7%。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在MIQA中存在明显局限：像素化虽保护隐私却降低性能，揭示隐私与可靠性之间的权衡；对上下文元数据的敏感性表明模型缺乏客观性，元数据既是隐私源也是偏差源。隐私保护与客观质量评估是部署前必须同时满足的关键需求。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied in medical tasks such as pathology description, report generation, and visual question answering. Medical Image Quality Assessment (MIQA) supports diagnostic accuracy and patient safety by determining whether images meet the standards required for clinical decision-making. Automating MIQA with VLMs may reduce workload, but their behavior under real-world conditions, where images may be degraded or textual context may affect judgments, should be further explored before deployment. We benchmark VLMs on medical image quality using the MediMeta-C dataset zero-shot across seven corruption types and five severity levels. We evaluate sensitivity to degradation patterns, the effect of corruptions on embedding geometry, and whether textual attributes (demographics, expertise, infrastructure, institution) alter scores. Across 16 VLMs and seven modalities, pixelation produced the largest score reductions (mean -20.58%, up to -34.4% for OCT), whereas brightness had limited effect (-0.81%). Embedding displacement was associated with score changes. Same-family models showed correlations of 0.67-0.83; some produced increases up to +31% for corrupted mammography. Textual attributes affected scores: institutional prestige raised them +17.15%, and equipment age lowered them -14.7%. The largest changes were +95.62% (InternVL-8B) and -37.7% (MedGemma). Current VLMs show limitations for medical image quality assessment. Pixelation, a privacy-preserving transformation, reduces performance, indicating a trade-off between patient privacy and reliability. Sensitivity to contextual metadata indicates limited objectivity and marks metadata as a privacy and bias source. Privacy protection and objective quality assessment are related requirements for use.

</details>


### [56] [MolSight: A Graph-Aware Vision-Language Model for Unified Chemical Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01982)
*Wenda Wang,Yihan Tong,Yuwei Hu,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MolSight 是一个图感知的视觉-语言模型框架，通过引入分子拓扑模块和分子对齐模块，将化学键邻接信息注入视觉 token 并实现视觉特征与化学符号语义的对齐，显著提升了分子图像的视觉理解与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子大语言模型难以充分捕捉分子结构的视觉表示，而分子视觉-语言模型虽具潜力，但在结构对齐和拓扑建模方面仍存在不足，限制了精确的分子理解。

Method: 提出 MolSight 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 分子拓扑模块（Molecular Topology Module），将化学键邻接信息注入视觉 token；(2) 分子对齐模块（Molecular Grounding Module），将视觉特征与化学符号语义进行对齐。

Result: 在多项化学视觉理解任务上，MolSight 显著优于现有视觉-语言模型、分子大语言模型及专用工具，达到了分子图像推理的新水平。

Conclusion: MolSight 通过融合图结构感知与视觉-语言对齐，有效弥补了现有模型在分子图像理解上的关键短板，为分子设计与药物发现中的视觉推理任务提供了强有力的新方案。

Abstract: Using molecular large language models (LLMs) as a unified framework for understanding molecular structures and functions is emerging as a new trend in tasks such as molecular design and drug discovery. However, these models struggle to fully capture the visual representation of molecular structures, limiting their potential. While existing molecular vision-language models (VLMs) show promise, they still face challenges in structural alignment and lack the necessary topological modeling for accurate molecular understanding. To address this, we propose MolSight, a graph-aware vision-language model framework designed to enhance the understanding of molecular images by VLMs. MolSight integrates a Molecular Topology Module to inject chemical-bond adjacency information into vision tokens, and a Molecular Grounding Module to align visual features with chemical symbolic semantics. Our experiments demonstrate that MolSight significantly outperforms existing VLMs, molecular LLMs, and specialized tools across multiple chemical visual understanding tasks, achieving a new level of molecular image reasoning.

</details>


### [57] [Open-Weather Robust 3D Detection via Dual-Critic Diffusion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01983)
*Shuyao Li,Chuanxing Geng,Heyang Sun,Qiang Zhou,Jingjing Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCDA框架，通过双评论家引导的扩散对齐，在不依赖天气标签的情况下将退化LiDAR特征恢复到干净流形，实现对未知天气类型的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-4D雷达融合方法依赖闭世界假设（训练和测试天气类型及严重程度一致），但实际中天气的开放性和多变性导致LiDAR退化模式剧烈变化，未见条件下性能骤降。

Method: 提出DCDA框架：(1) 以4D雷达为条件的扩散过程逐步精炼退化LiDAR特征；(2) 检测引导评论家（基于预训练干净天气模型）保持目标级判别力和定位精度；(3) 天气对抗评论家强制整体分布与干净天气表征一致。通过语义和分布约束而非显式天气建模实现对齐。

Result: 在构建的结构化开放天气基准（含留存的类型-严重程度组合）上，DCDA展现出对未见天气类型和严重程度的有效泛化能力。

Conclusion: DCDA作为一种天气无关的框架，无需配对数据或天气标签即可有效恢复退化LiDAR特征，显著提升了自动驾驶3D目标检测在开放天气条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robust 3D object detection under adverse weather remains a critical hurdle for autonomous driving. Despite progress with LiDAR-4D radar fusion, most methods are constrained by a closed-world assumption, implicitly requiring training and test weather to align in both type and severity. This premise fails in practice: the open-ended nature of weather, and even variations within a single type like rain, cause dramatically different LiDAR degradation patterns, leading to significant performance drops in unseen conditions. To address this, we present Dual-Critic Guided Diffusion Alignment (DCDA), a weather-agnostic framework that learns to recover degraded LiDAR features toward a clean manifold. Rather than modeling specific weather types, DCDA employs a 4D radar-conditioned diffusion process to progressively refine features, guided by two complementary critics. (i) A detection-guided critic, anchored by a pre-trained clean-weather model, ensures that the refined features retain object-level discriminability and localization accuracy. (ii) A weather adversarial critic enforces holistic distributional consistency with clean-weather representations. By aligning features through semantic and distributional constraints rather than explicit weather modeling, DCDA generalizes effectively to unseen weather types and severities without requiring paired data or weather labels. We further introduce a structured open-weather benchmark with held-out type-severity combinations and extensive experiments verify DCDA's advantages.

</details>


### [58] [Understanding Geometric Representations in Self-Supervised Vision Transformers via Subspace Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01987)
*Weichen Zhou,Yawen Zou,Chunzhi Gu,Ran Dong,Haoran Xie,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受控子空间干预框架，利用SVD分解线性探针权重，揭示自监督ViT编码密集几何信息的内部机制。发现DINOv2对齐空间特征便于线性提取，MAE则分散信号；几何表示高度可压缩；几何精度在中间层达到峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针虽广泛用于评估几何表示，但将特征视为黑箱，无法解耦底层拓扑结构。作者旨在打开这个黑箱，深入理解自监督ViT如何内部编码密集几何信息。

Method: 提出受控子空间干预框架：对已收敛的线性探针权重进行奇异值分解（SVD），分离出包含显式几何信号的低秩子空间。通过操纵这些子空间来干预和探究特征编码机制，分析不同预训练目标（DINOv2 vs MAE）、不同层的几何编码特性。

Result: (1) 预训练目标决定编码方式：DINOv2对齐空间特征支持高效线性提取，MAE分散信号需更广空间上下文。(2) 显式几何表示高度可压缩，密集预测头可约束到低秩子空间且性能损失极小。(3) 几何精度在中间层达到峰值，随后让位于语义抽象。

Conclusion: 通过连接内部编码机制与下游性能，为高效特征选择和轻量化解码器设计提供了理论基础。代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce a controlled subspace intervention framework to investigate how self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) encode dense geometric information. While linear probing is widely used to assess geometric representations, it treats features as a black box, failing to disentangle the underlying topology. To address this issue, we decompose the weights of converged linear probes to isolate the low-rank subspaces containing explicit geometric signals using Singular Value Decomposition (SVD). Our perspective yields three key insights: (1) Pre-training objectives determine how features are encoded. DINOv2 aligns spatial features for efficient linear extraction, while Masked Autoencoders (MAE) tend to disperse these signals, requiring a broader spatial context. (2) Explicit geometric representations are highly compressible, suggesting dense predictive heads could potentially be constrained to low-rank subspaces with minimal performance loss. (3) The layer-wise task affinity suggests that geometric precision peaks at intermediate layers before yielding to semantic abstraction in the final layers. By connecting internal encoding mechanics with downstream performance, these findings provide a basis for effective feature selection and lightweight decoder design. The source code is available at https://github.com/Zhou-Weichen/Geosubprobe.

</details>


### [59] [Training-free Controllable Human Motion Generation under Heterogeneous Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01990)
*Xiaofei Hui,Bo Yan,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIC框架，将扩散运动生成建模为随机控制问题，首次实现无需训练的、同时支持连续可微目标约束和离散/黑箱准则约束的运动生成，并引入控制导向的约束协调机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练可控运动生成方法要求约束必须是连续、可微的基于目标的函数，但许多真实需求是基于准则的，仅提供不连续、稀疏甚至黑箱反馈。因此需要一种能统一处理两类约束的免训练框架。

Method: 提出Motion-Inference-as-Control（MIC）框架：1）将扩散运动生成转化为随机控制问题，推导出逐步控制律，使准则约束无需可微性即可处理，目标约束作为特例自然兼容；2）设计控制导向的约束协调机制，在生成过程中自适应平衡和协调多种运动约束。

Result: 在多种约束设置下的实验验证了MIC框架的有效性，表明其能灵活处理连续目标约束和离散准则约束，并在约束协调方面表现优越。

Conclusion: MIC是首个通过随机控制视角统一处理可微与不可微约束的免训练运动生成框架，为灵活约束执行提供了原则性解决方案。

Abstract: Training-free controllable motion generation has attracted growing interest for enabling flexible constraint enforcement without constraint-specific training. However, existing training-free methods require constraints to be continuous objective-based with differentiable losses, while many real-world requirements are criterion-based and provide only discontinuous, sparse, or even black-box feedback. In this paper, we propose Motion-Inference-as-Control (MIC), the first training-free motion generation framework that handles both continuous objective-based and criterion-based motion constraints under a shared mechanism. The key idea is to cast diffusion-based motion generation as a stochastic control problem. This perspective not only provides principled and practically effective step-wise control laws that support criterion-based constraints without requiring differentiability and naturally accommodate objective-based constraints as a special case, but also motivates a control-oriented constraint coordination mechanism that adaptively balances and reconciles motion constraints during generation. Experiments across diverse constraint settings demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [60] [Mirror Illusion Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02015)
*Xiaopei Zhu,Zeyuan Li,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoMIA 是一种自动化镜面错觉艺术（Mirror Illusion Art）设计管线，可从前视图和镜面反射视图两张目标图像联合优化可打印3D物体的几何与纹理，通过四种机制抑制伪影，在约76秒内生成光滑完整的数字与实体作品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拓扑驱动和阴影类镜面错觉设计方法需要大量人工干预、仅优化形状，且常产生不平滑或不完整的几何体。因此需要一种能够联合优化形状与颜色的全自动方案。

Method: AutoMIA 将问题形式化为从两张目标2D图像（正面与镜面）反求可打印3D物体的逆向设计任务。引入四种优化机制：(1) PAC（投影对齐分量选择）减少表面噪声；(2) PWA（位置加权自适应抑制）消除背景噪声；(3) IVP（内部体素保留）防止内部断裂；(4) SCD（形状-颜色解耦优化）平衡形状与颜色优化过程。

Result: AutoMIA 能在单张 RTX 3090 上以平均约76秒、2.6 GB显存成功生成多样化且光滑的镜面错觉艺术作品，并在数字和物理世界中均得到验证。

Conclusion: AutoMIA 实现了镜面错觉艺术的自动化联合优化设计，显著提升了生成质量与效率，推动了逆向图形学与计算设计领域的发展。

Abstract: Mirror Illusion Art is a novel reflection-conditioned 3D illusion where one object yields two target appearances (front and mirror). The task is formulated as inverse design from two target 2D images (front and mirror) to a printable 3D object with geometry and texture. Prior topology-driven and shadow-based approaches demand substantial manual effort, optimize shape only, and often yield non-smooth or incomplete geometry. To address these challenges, we propose AutoMIA, an automated Mirror Illusion Art design pipeline that jointly optimizes shape and color. To stabilize optimization and suppress artifacts, four mechanisms are introduced: (1) projection-alignment component (PAC) selection to reduce surface noise, (2) position-weighted adaptive (PWA) suppression for background noise, (3) internal voxel preservation (IVP) to prevent internal fractures, and (4) shape-color decoupled (SCD) optimization that balance shape and color optimization. AutoMIA generate diverse smooth Mirror Illusion artworks successfully both in the digital and physical world, with only around 76s design time and 2.6 GB memory on average using a single RTX 3090, advancing inverse graphics and computational design. Our code is available at https://github.com/zxp555/AutoMIA.

</details>


### [61] [ESC: Emotional Self-Correction for Reliable Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02089)
*Tien-Huy Nguyen,Minh-Nhat Nguyen,Nguyen Nhat Huy,Hung Viet Nguyen,Huy Nguyen Minh Nhat,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Cuong Tuan Nguyen,Hoang M. Le,Dat Nguyen,Phat Kim Huynh,Min Xu,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ESC（Emotional Self-Correction），一种无需训练的VLM自校正框架。研究发现情感信号可以有效触发VLM的潜在自校正行为，通过外部验证器检测错误并注入情感反馈，促使模型反思并修正回答，在安全性、幻觉、视觉感知和多模态推理等基准上持续提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自校正方法依赖训练后处理或精心设计的反馈，计算成本高。本文探索情感线索能否在无额外训练的情况下激活VLM的潜在自校正能力。

Method: 提出ESC（Emotional Self-Correction）训练无关自校正框架：引入外部验证器检测可能错误的初始响应，注入情感反馈（如鼓励模型更谨慎、反思性推理），促使模型在不经额外训练的情况下生成修正后的响应。

Result: 在安全性、幻觉检测、视觉中心感知和多模态推理等多个基准上，ESC持续提升可靠性，同时保持整体模型效用。

Conclusion: 情感不仅可作为需要被识别的能力，还可作为VLM可扩展自校正的实用控制信号，为类人化、情感融合的可靠研究新方向奠定基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance across diverse multimodal tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to unreliable reasoning. Existing self-correction methods mitigate these issues but typically rely on post-training or carefully engineered feedback, incurring high computational cost. In this work, we revisit this challenge through the lens of emotional cues, asking whether they can activate latent self-correction behaviors in VLMs without additional training. \textbf{We find that emotional signals serve as an effective trigger for self-correction, encouraging more cautious and reflective reasoning}. Motivated by this finding, we propose \escabstract (\textbf{\underline{E}}motional \textbf{\underline{S}}elf-\textbf{\underline{C}}orrection), a training-free self-correction framework. ESC introduces an external verifier that detects potentially incorrect initial responses and injects emotional feedback to encourage model to reflect, and produce a better revised response without additional training. Extensive experiments across safety, hallucination, vision-centric perception, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that ESC consistently improves reliability while preserving overall model utility. These results suggest that emotion can function not only as an ability to be recognized, but also as a practical control signal for scalable self-correction in VLMs. \textbf{We therefore believe that ESC provides a strong foundation for a new reliable human-like, emotion-integrated research direction.} Our project is publicly available at \textcolor{red}{https://genai4e.github.io/ESC/}.

</details>


### [62] [UnderOneFacade: Worldwide Facade Semantic Segmentation Benchmark Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02018)
*Yi Wang,Fan Wang,Prabin Gyawali,Ziyang Xu,Anna Klimkowska,Yixiong Jing,Wanru Yang,Filip Biljecki,Christoph Holst,Benjamin Busam,Brian Sheil,Olaf Wysocki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 UnderOneFacade，目前最大规模的跨国家、跨大洲3D立面基准数据集，包含27亿个标注点的厘米级点云，具有层级化、统一的建筑语义标签。通过系统评估，表明现有方法在细粒度建筑元素识别和跨地理域泛化方面表现不佳，最佳模型在LoFG3基准上仅达33 IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球一致的语义数字孪生需要厘米级精度且地理可迁移的3D立面分割能力，但现有数据集存在地理覆盖狭窄、语义不一致、精度不足等问题，限制了立面解析的进展和跨域泛化评估。

Method: 构建了UnderOneFacade数据集——跨国家、跨大陆的大规模3D立面基准，包含厘米级精度点云和层级化、协调统一、基于建筑学的语义标签（共27亿标注点）。在此基准上，系统评估了代表性的基于点、图和Transformer的架构方法。

Result: 当前方法在细粒度建筑元素识别方面表现困难，且跨地理域时性能显著下降；在细粒度LoFG3基准上，最佳模型仅达到33 IoU。

Conclusion: UnderOneFacade以前所未有的规模将几何精度与标准化语义相结合，为开发鲁棒、可迁移的3D分割模型建立了严格的评估基准。

Abstract: Globally consistent semantic digital twins require centimeter-accurate and geographically transferable 3D facade segmentation. However, progress in facade parsing is limited by the lack of large-scale, standardized benchmarks for evaluating cross-domain generalization. Existing datasets are geographically narrow, semantically inconsistent, or insufficiently precise. We introduce UnderOneFacade, the largest cross-country and cross-continent 3D facade benchmark to date, comprising centimeter-accurate point clouds with hierarchical, harmonized, and architecturally grounded semantic labels totaling 2.7 billion annotated points. Through a systematic evaluation of representative point-, graph- and transformer-based architectures, we show that current methods struggle to recognize fine-grained architectural elements and degrade significantly across geographic domains, with the best models achieving only up to 33 IoU on the fine-grained LoFG3 benchmark. By combining geometric precision with standardized semantics at unprecedented scale, UnderOneFacade establishes a rigorous benchmark for developing robust and transferable 3D segmentation models. The dataset, evaluation scripts, and pretrained models will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [63] [Spatio-Temporal and Clinical Conditioning for Fine-Grained Radiology Report Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02024)
*P. Sloan,E. Simpson,M. Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAR3 提出了一种多模态、时空感知的检索式放射报告生成框架，通过将解剖区域信息与临床指征及纵向变化对齐，在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上超越了现有检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射影像需求增长与放射科人力短缺之间的矛盾日益突出，自动化报告生成有望缓解此压力。然而，现有检索式方法存在三大不足：(1) 生成方式僵化，(2) 缺乏显式的解剖学定位，(3) 未考虑疾病的纵向进展及可用的临床上下文信息。

Method: STAR3 框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 使用目标检测器识别具有解剖学意义的区域，实现区域级解剖信息对齐；(2) 将当前临床上下文（指征等）作为检索条件；(3) 建模先前检查与当前检查之间的影像变化，捕捉纵向疾病进展。最终通过时空注意力的多模态检索机制，生成兼具解剖定位和时间感知的报告。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上的实验表明，STAR3 在检索指标、自然语言处理指标以及临床指标上均优于当前的检索式报告生成方法。

Conclusion: 在检索过程中引入解剖、时间和临床三个维度的条件约束，能够显著提升自动化放射报告生成的质量，使其更贴近真实的临床报告实践。

Abstract: Radiology is vital to modern healthcare, but rising imaging demand and persistent workforce shortages strain reporting capacity and clinical workflows. Automated radiology report generation has the potential to support radiologists and help alleviate this burden; however, existing retrieval-based methods remain rigid, lack explicit anatomical grounding, and do not account for longitudinal disease progression or available clinical context. In this work, we introduce STAR3, a multimodal, spatio-temporal, attentive retrieval framework for radiology report generation that aligns region-level anatomical information with clinical indications and longitudinal changes across chest X-ray studies. Our framework employs an object detector to identify anatomically meaningful regions and retrieves semantically relevant report sentences conditioned on both current clinical context and changes observed between prior and current examinations. This design enables anatomically and temporally grounded report generation that better reflects clinical reporting practice. Experiments on the MIMIC-CXR dataset demonstrate that STAR3 outperforms current retrieval-based approaches on retrieval, NLP and clinical metrics, highlighting the value of conditioning retrieval anatomically, temporally and clinically for advancing automated radiology report generation.

</details>


### [64] [Evaluating Vision-Language Models as a Zero-Shot Learning Alternative to You Only Look Once and Optical Character Recognition for Nigerian License Plate Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02025)
*Ismail Ismail Tijjani,Ahmad Abubakar Mustapaha,Sunusi Ibrahim Muhammad,Muhammad Bashir Aliyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索了视觉语言模型在尼日利亚车牌识别中的零样本学习能力，评估了五种主流VLM在88张真实场景图像上的表现，结果显示Gemini和Qwen在字符错误率上显著优于其他模型，挑战了传统YOLO+OCR多阶段流水线的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LPR系统依赖YOLO+OCR多阶段流水线，存在资源需求高、非结构化环境下性能差、需要大量标注数据等局限性。因此，探索VLMs作为统一的零样本学习方案，以克服上述缺陷，尤其在数据稀缺的尼日利亚场景下具有重要实际意义。

Method: 构建了一个包含88张尼日利亚真实场景中采集的挑战性图像数据集；选取五种视觉语言模型进行零样本评估：Gemini 2.0 Flash Exp、Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct、GPT-4o、Claude 4 Sonnet、Llama 3.2 Vision 90b；以字符错误率（CER）作为核心评价指标，对比各模型在复杂场景下的识别精度与鲁棒性。

Result: Gemini和Qwen在字符错误率指标上显著优于GPT-4o、Claude 4 Sonnet和Llama 3.2 Vision 90b，在挑战性图像场景中展现出更高的准确性和鲁棒性。该结果对模型提供商的部分性能声明提出了质疑。

Conclusion: VLMs在车牌识别任务中展现出超越传统YOLO+OCR流水线的实用优势，具备作为统一零样本LPR解决方案的潜力；同时，不同VLM之间性能差异显著，提示实际部署时需谨慎选型，并对厂商的性能声明保持审慎态度。

Abstract: License Plate Recognition (LPR) systems are critical tools in traffic monitoring, security enforcement, and urban mobility management. Traditional LPR systems often rely on a multi-stage pipeline involving object detection using You Only Look Once (YOLO) and Optical Character Recognition (OCR), which suffer from limitations such as high resource demands, poor performance in unstructured environments, and the need for large annotated datasets. This study explores the potential of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified, zeroshot learning solution for Nigerian license plate recognition. Using a curated dataset of 88 challenging real-world images collected in Nigeria, we evaluate five selected VLMs: Gemini 2.0 Flash Exp (Google DeepMind), Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct (Alibaba), GPT-4o (OpenAI), Claude 4 Sonnet (Anthropic), and Llama 3.2 Vision 90b (Meta). Results based on Character Error Rate (CER) reveal that Gemini and Qwen significantly outperform other models in both accuracy and robustness, on the challenging image scenarios. This work highlights the practical advantages of VLMs over YOLO+OCR, questions the claims by model providers, and compares the performances of the VLMs.

</details>


### [65] [ComplexMimic: Human-Scene Interaction Imitation in Complex 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02034)
*Lu Pan,Hongwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ComplexMimic 是一个面向复杂环境中人-场景交互模仿学习的框架，通过双流策略（模仿专家+交互专家）和难度感知蒸馏策略，解决在复杂场景下交互成功与运动自然性之间的权衡问题，在三个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理的人-场景交互模仿学习方法多聚焦于简化场景，在复杂真实环境中的适用性受限。复杂场景下，存在交互成功执行与维持自然物理合理运动之间的固有权衡，亟需能同时兼顾两者的方法。

Method: 提出 ComplexMimic 框架，核心包含两大组件：（1）双流策略（Dual Flow Strategy），分别训练模仿专家（负责精确运动跟踪）和交互专家（负责复杂场景中的碰撞感知适应）；（2）难度感知蒸馏策略（Difficulty-aware Distillation），不同于平等对待所有专家的朴素多专家蒸馏，该方法根据失败统计和学习进展信号，自适应加权监督并优先关注“困难但可学习”的轨迹，从而缓解欠采样问题。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，ComplexMimic 的性能优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: ComplexMimic 通过双流专家互补与难度感知蒸馏，有效解决了复杂环境中 HSI 模仿学习的核心权衡问题，显著提升了物理合理交互的复现能力，具有良好的实际应用前景。代码已开源。

Abstract: Physics-based Human-Scene Interaction (HSI) imitation learning is crucial for embodied intelligence as it bridges the gap between kinematic 3D motions and real-world dynamics. However, most existing methods focus on simplified scene settings, leaving complex environments largely unexplored, which limits their applicability in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we focus on HSI mimicry in complex environments. Under this complex setting, we observe an inherent trade-off between successfully performing interaction and maintaining natural, physically plausible motions. To address this challenge, we propose ComplexMimic, a framework that reconstructs diverse HSI by interpreting imperfect MoCap data. First, we introduce a Dual Flow Strategy, which learns two complementary experts: an imitation expert for accurate motion tracking and an interaction expert for collision-aware adaptation in complex scenes. Second, naive multi-expert distillation, which treats all experts equally, often under-samples challenging behaviors, limiting effective learning. To mitigate this issue, we propose a difficulty-aware distillation strategy that adaptively weights supervision and prioritizes hard-yet-learnable trajectories guided by failure statistics and learning progress signals. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/LuPan23/ComplexMimic.

</details>


### [66] [Hierarchical Anti-Aesthetics: Protecting Facial Privacy against Customized Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02038)
*Songping Wang,Yueming Lyu,Shiqi Liu,Chen Zhao,Ziyuan Chen,Ning Li,Jing Dong,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化反美学（HAA）框架，通过从全局和局部两个层面降低定制化扩散模型的图像生成质量，从而保护人脸隐私，防止恶意模型泄露身份信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定制化扩散模型的兴起带来了个性化视觉内容生成的热潮，但也引发了恶意滥用导致的人脸隐私泄露风险。鉴于图像美学与人类对图像质量的感知高度相关，作者从美学角度出发，通过降低恶意模型的生成质量来减少人脸身份泄露。

Method: 提出层次化反美学（HAA）框架，包含两个分支：（1）全局反美学分支，通过构建全局反美学奖励机制和相应损失函数来降低整体美学质量和生成质量；（2）局部反美学分支，通过局部反美学奖励机制和损失函数将对抗扰动引导至人脸区域，从而破坏人脸身份信息。两个分支协同作用，在定制化生成过程中实现从全局到局部的反美学退化。

Result: 大量实验表明，HAA在身份移除方面优于现有方法，能够有效保护人脸隐私。

Conclusion: HAA框架从美学视角为人脸隐私保护提供了一种有效的新工具，通过层次化的反美学退化策略，在定制化扩散模型场景下实现了优于现有方法的身份保护效果。

Abstract: The rise of customized diffusion models has fueled a boom in personalized visual content creation, but it also introduces serious risks of malicious misuse, thereby posing threats to personal privacy. Image aesthetics are strongly correlated with human perception of image quality. Motivated by this observation, we address facial privacy protection from a novel aesthetic perspective by degrading the generation quality of maliciously customized models, thus reducing facial identity leakage. Specifically, we propose a Hierarchical Anti-Aesthetics (HAA) framework that exploits aesthetic cues at multiple perceptual levels. HAA consists of two key branches: (1) Global Anti-Aesthetics, which degrades overall aesthetics and generation quality by constructing a global anti-aesthetic reward mechanism and a corresponding loss; and (2) Local Anti-Aesthetics, which disrupts facial identity by using a local anti-aesthetic reward mechanism and loss to guide adversarial perturbations toward facial regions. By integrating both branches, HAA achieves anti-aesthetic degradation from a global to a local level during customized generation. Extensive experiments show that HAA outperforms existing methods in identity removal, providing an effective tool for protecting facial privacy.

</details>


### [67] [PWM-ArtGen: Part World Model for Articulated Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02045)
*Wentao Zheng,Ancong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PWM-ArtGen 提出一个统一的部分世界模型，通过耦合动作扩散与图像扩散，从单张图像联合学习视觉动态与运动学参数的联合分布，显著提升了铰接式3D物体生成的精度与零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么从缺乏动态信息的静态图像直接推断运动学参数，要么从生成动态中估计参数，后者存在两步累积误差。此外，现有标注数据集的规模和多样性有限，阻碍了对复杂真实物体的泛化。核心动机在于利用视觉动态与运动学参数之间的内在关联，并通过无标注数据实现协同训练。

Method: 提出统一的部分世界模型 PWM-ArtGen。核心理念是将铰接物体建模为动态系统，将动作扩散与图像扩散两个扩散过程耦合，并赋予各自独立的扩散时间步，从而使视觉分支能够利用无标注图像对进行协同训练。此外，作者构建了一个包含19.7k对无运动学标注的光照真实感部件级图像数据集以支撑协同训练。

Result: 实验表明，PWM-ArtGen 在静止状态下的生成性能大幅超越现有基线方法，并对外分布物体展现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模视觉动态与运动学参数的分布，并利用无标注数据协同训练，PWM-ArtGen 有效解决了单图像铰接3D物体生成中的运动学结构预测难题，同时具备优异的泛化性能。

Abstract: The key challenge in articulated 3D object generation from a single image is accurately predicting the underlying kinematic structure. Existing methods either infer kinematic parameters directly from a static image that lacks dynamic part-level kinematic relationships, or estimate parameters from visual dynamics generated from a single image, which is prone to accumulated errors of two steps. Moreover, the limited scale and diversity of existing annotated datasets further hinder generalization to complex, real-world objects. To overcome these limitations, we propose to learn the joint distribution of visual dynamics and kinematic parameters. Recognizing that articulated objects can be formulated as dynamic systems, we propose a unified Part World Model called PWM-ArtGen. To leverage unannotated data, this model couples action diffusion and image diffusion with independent diffusion timesteps, which enables visual branch co-training. We further curate a photorealistic dataset of 19.7k part-level image pairs without kinematic annotations, to support co-training. Experiments demonstrate that PWM-ArtGen substantially outperforms existing baselines in the resting state and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to out-of-distribution objects.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02494)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIP模型易受排版攻击（Typographic Attack），即图像中的无关文字会干扰视觉表征，使其偏向词汇语义而非真实视觉语义。本文提出一种无需训练的可解释性方法，通过采样分析和电路挖掘定位ViT中编码词汇信息的注意力头，并通过对这些电路进行简单干预（如调整注意力权重），在不额外训练的情况下显著提升模型对排版攻击的鲁棒性，在多个LVLM上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP作为大多数现代大视觉语言模型的基础视觉编码器，存在一个关键但未被充分探索的失效模式：图像中出现的无关文本会混淆视觉表征，使其偏向词汇意义而非真实视觉语义（即排版攻击）。这一脆弱性对自动驾驶等安全关键应用构成重大风险。现有防御方法或需额外训练，或缺乏可解释性，因此需要一种可解释且有效的鲁棒性提升方法。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的机制可解释性方法：1）对隐藏状态表征进行基于采样的解释，定量地将语义关注与词汇关注归因到各个注意力头；2）通过概率分析和电路挖掘，隔离出不成比例地编码词汇信息的特定ViT组件，从而定位排版攻击的机制性源头；3）对识别出的电路直接施加简单干预（如选择性调整注意力权重），无需任何额外训练。

Result: 1）所提出的干预方法在物体分类任务中显著提升了对抗排版攻击的鲁棒性，且优于监督式和免训练的防御方法；2）将该干预应用于多个SOTA LVLM的视觉编码器后，在RIO-Bench的排版攻击干扰下，视觉问答准确率获得了显著提升，验证了方法的有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 排版攻击的根源可追溯至ViT中特定的注意力头电路，通过对这些电路施加简单的训练无关干预即可有效提升CLIP及下游LVLM的鲁棒性。该机制可解释性方法兼具有效性、可解释性和泛化性，为安全关键应用中的视觉模型鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench.
