The combination of an improved speech projection mechanism and domain-specific synthetic data allows our model to outperform last year's best short-track system, while being considerably more compact and relying on a weaker LLM backbone. This year's results place our system tied for first place in the overall short track ranking.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Supervised Clarification: Input Rewriting with LLMs for Dialogue Discourse Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01964)
*Yiming Liu,Ziyue Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Xin Yu,Yingheng Tang,Tianyu Jiang,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了在零样本和无监督条件下，利用输入重写（澄清）来改进冻结的增量对话篇章解析器的可行性。研究发现，与有监督设置相比，无监督澄清极不可靠，常引入更多退化；即便使用parser-aware训练，也难以产生一致的改进。作者将澄清重新定义为选择性干预问题，并指出"可重写性预测"是关键的缺失能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前有监督的澄清模型在对话篇章解析（DDP）中展现出通过重写不完整话语来提升解析精度的潜力。但实际部署中往往缺乏澄清监督信号，必须依赖零样本提示或冻结解析器的反馈。本文旨在揭示这种现实条件下输入重写策略的真实可靠性，并诊断其根本局限。

Method: 在三个SDRT数据集和多个解析器上，系统评估了以下设置：(1) 零样本/无监督的仅基于最后一句话的澄清重写；(2) 解析器无关的重写分析；(3) Best-of-8重写分析以探测理论上限；(4) 使用GRPO训练的解析器感知澄清器，考察其是否能学会选择性弃权以减少退化。

Result: 无监督澄清远不如有监督设置可靠；解析器无关重写引入的退化多于修复；Best-of-8分析表明大量错误无法仅通过输入重写修复；GRPO训练的parser-aware澄清器虽可将退化减少37%（通过学习保守弃权），但仍无法产生能一致改进解析的选择性感知澄清。

Conclusion: 澄清应被重新定义为选择性干预问题。"可重写性预测"——即在干预前判断一个话语是否可被修复——是冻结篇章解析器在输入端优化的核心缺失能力，也是更广泛地改进智能体流水线的关键方向。

Abstract: Rewriting inputs to improve frozen downstream models has become a common strategy in modern NLP pipelines. Prior work on incremental dialogue discourse parsing (DDP) shows that supervised clarification models can rewrite fragmentary or underspecified utterances, such as resolving ellipsis or references, to improve parsing accuracy. In this work, we revisit this idea under realistic deployment conditions, where no clarification supervision is available and the clarifier must rely on zero-shot prompting or feedback from a frozen parser. Across three Segmented Discourse Representation Theory (SDRT) datasets and multiple parsers, we find that last-utterance clarification is far less reliable than suggested by supervised settings. Parser-agnostic rewriting often introduces more regressions than repairs, as edits that enable fixes also disrupt discourse cues relied upon by the parser. A best-of-8 rewriting analysis further reveals a practical ceiling: a large fraction of errors are not repairable through input rewriting alone. A parser-aware clarifier trained with GRPO reduces regressions by up to 37% by learning conservative abstention, yet still fails to produce selectivity-aware clarifications that consistently improve parsing. Together, these findings recast clarification as a selective intervention problem. We identify rewritability prediction, deciding whether an utterance is repairable before intervention, as the key missing capability for input-side optimization of frozen discourse parsers, and a critical direction for improving agentic pipelines more broadly.

</details>


### [138] [Towards a Phonology-Informed Evaluation of Multilingual TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01965)
*Sneha Ray Barman,Neeraj Kumar Sharma,Shakuntala Mahanta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分类器的框架，以人类语音为基准，审计神经TTS系统对语言特定音系模式的保真度。在阿萨姆语ATR元音和谐测试中，发现TTS合成语音中[+ATR]中元音有1/3被实现为[-ATR]，揭示了预期音系与产出语音之间的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经TTS系统虽在自然度上表现优异，但MOS等标准指标无法检测其是否准确保留了区分词及其语法形式的语音对比。需要一种超越主观自然度评价的诊断方法，专门审计TTS输出对语言特定音系模式的忠实度。

Method: 提出基于分类器的审计框架：首先在人类语音上训练一个音系特征分类器（如ATR特征分类），然后将该分类器应用于TTS合成语音，通过对比分类结果与底层音系规约，量化TTS系统对特定语音对比的保真度。以Meta MMS TTS生成的阿萨姆语语音为测试对象，聚焦于舌根前伸（ATR）元音和谐现象。

Result: 分类器从人类语音迁移至合成语音时性能损失极小。审计揭示：约1/3的底层[+ATR]中元音在合成语音中被实现为[-ATR]，这种偏差在人类语音中不存在。此外，在词层面，预测的ATR标签对和谐的分类准确率高于转写标签，表明预期音系与实际产出之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 该框架为TTS系统提供了任务特异性的音系诊断工具，可推广至其他具备可测量声学线索的音系对比，弥补了传统自然度评价在语言学忠实度检测方面的不足。

Abstract: Neural TTS systems can sound natural across languages, but naturalness does not guarantee the preservation of sound contrasts that distinguish words from their grammatical forms. Standard metrics like MOS do not test for this. We propose a classifier-based framework that audits TTS output against language-specific phonological patterns using human speech as a benchmark. Testing Assamese advanced tongue root (ATR) vowel harmony with Meta's MMS TTS, we show that a classifier trained on human speech transfers to synthesized speech with minimal loss. The faithfulness audit reveals that [+ATR] mid vowels are realized as [-ATR] in 1/3 tokens despite an underlying [+ATR] specification, a bias absent in human speech. At the word level, predicted ATR labels classify harmony more accurately than transcription labels, indicating a gap between intended and produced phonology. The framework offers task-specific diagnostics and generalizes to other phonological contrasts with measurable acoustic cues.

</details>


### [139] [Object Aligner: A Configurable JSON Schema Similarity Score for Graphs, Applied to LLM Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01972)
*Jan Drchal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Object Aligner (OA)，一个开源 Python 库，通过递归树对齐（匈牙利算法处理无序集合、序列对齐处理有序集合）对两个 JSON 对象进行确定性评分，核心贡献是"引用对齐"——利用 Weisfeiler-Lehman 颜色细化近似图同构来推断标识符双射，使评分对重标记不变；同时能无额外成本地输出排序修复建议，并在 GEPA 提示优化器中作为奖励信号表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 常需生成符合固定模式的 JSON，但衡量输出与金标准匹配度的现有方案均有缺陷：精确匹配过于脆弱，文本相似度忽略结构，LLM 裁判成本高、不透明且非确定性。此外，复杂结构化数据常形成图或超图，打破了现有相似度度量依赖扁平树的假设。

Method: 提出 Object Aligner：通过 JSON Schema 扩展进行配置，递归对齐两棵 JSON 树——无序集合用匈牙利算法，有序集合用序列对齐，按 Schema 声明的粒度给予部分得分。核心创新"引用对齐"通过 Weisfeiler-Lehman 颜色细化近似推断金标准与候选标识符间的双射，使评分对重标记不变。另设顺序敏感序列机制面向排序与规划任务。对齐结果可定位所有不匹配并输出排序修复建议。

Result: 作为奖励函数嵌入 GEPA 提示优化器时，Object Aligner 在所有数据集上表现为有帮助或保持中立。

Conclusion: Object Aligner 提供了一种确定性、可配置、对标识符重标记不变的 JSON 结构匹配评分方案，填补了现有方法的空白，并在提示优化等下游任务中验证了其实用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often asked to produce JSON conforming to a fixed schema, powering information extraction, tool calling, agentic planning, and knowledge-graph construction. Measuring how closely an output matches a gold reference is essential yet surprisingly hard: exact match is brittle, text similarity ignores structure, and an LLM judge is expensive, opaque, and non-deterministic. We address this with Object Aligner (OA), an open-source Python library that scores two JSON objects deterministically by recursively aligning their trees (the Hungarian algorithm for unordered collections, sequence alignment for ordered ones) and awarding partial credit at the granularity the schema declares. The Object Aligner is configured entirely through a set of JSON Schema extensions, so adapting it to a new task involves annotating a schema rather than writing code. Complex structured data, however, are rarely flat trees: records may form graphs or hypergraphs keyed by arbitrary identifiers, breaking the assumptions of prior similarity metrics. Our central contribution, referential alignment, closes this gap by inferring a bijection between gold and candidate identifiers and scoring every reference through it, so the score is invariant to relabeling. Since recovering this bijection exactly is graph isomorphism, the Object Aligner approximates it with Weisfeiler-Leman color refinement. An order-sensitive sequence regime targets ranking and planning. Since the same alignment localizes every mismatch, the Object Aligner emits ranked repair suggestions at no extra cost. Used as a reward inside the GEPA prompt optimizer, Object Aligner helps or stays neutral across all datasets.

</details>


### [140] [Using embeddings to predict spoken word duration and pitch in Mandarin monosyllabic words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02002)
*Xiaoyun Jin,Mirjam Ernestus,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了上下文嵌入（CEs）是否能够预测汉语普通话单音节CV词在自然对话中的时长。结果表明，CEs不仅在类型层面，也在个例层面对时长具有显著预测能力，且预测精度足以将归一化时间下的基频轮廓反变换至毫秒时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，普通话词的基频（f0）轮廓可部分由其上下文嵌入预测，但尚不清楚上下文嵌入是否也能预测词的时长。本研究旨在填补这一空白，进一步探索上下文嵌入在语音时长预测中的潜力。

Method: 从普通话自然口语语料库中提取7470个单音节CV词个例，利用上下文嵌入作为预测变量，分别在类型层面（type-wise）和个例层面（token-wise）建立时长预测模型，并通过置换基线检验（permutation baseline）评估预测是否优于随机水平。同时，利用预测时长将[0,1]归一化时间的f0轮廓反变换至毫秒尺度，并与经验轮廓进行对比。

Result: 上下文嵌入在类型和个例两个层面均对时长有显著预测能力，均优于置换基线。预测时长精度足够支持将归一化f0轮廓成功反变换至毫秒时间尺度，生成的预测轮廓与经验轮廓接近，且同样优于置换基线。

Conclusion: 上下文嵌入不仅编码了基频信息，还编码了时长信息，这使得从归一化时间到绝对时间的反变换成为可能，进一步证实了上下文嵌入中蕴含丰富的语音韵律信息。

Abstract: Time-normalized f0 contours of Mandarin words in conversational speech have been shown to be predictable in part from their contextualized embeddings (CEs). The present study investigates whether CEs also predict spoken word duration for 7470 tokens of Mandarin monosyllabic CV words extracted from a Mandarin corpus of spontaneous speech. We show that CEs indeed are predictive for duration, above chance level, not only at the type level, but also at the level of individual tokens, as indicated by the results obtained with the type-wise and token-wise permutation baselines. We also show that the predicted durations are sufficiently precise to back-transform predicted f0 contours in [0,1] normalized time to contours on the ms time scale. The resulting predicted contours approximate empirical contours and also outperform a permutation baseline.

</details>


### [141] [EduArt: An educational-level benchmark for evaluating art history knowledge in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02007)
*Gianmarco Spinaci,Lukas Klic,Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EduArt 是一个面向艺术史教育与视觉推理的多模态 LLM 评测基准，包含 871 道人工编写的题目，覆盖 7 种格式和 2 种语言。评测发现，12 个模型在选择题上接近天花板，但在开放完成和错误识别等生成式格式上准确率急剧下降，揭示了识别与生成能力之间的显著分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型在通用基准测试中已接近满分，但这类聚合指标无法反映模型在单一学科内的真实行为。现有艺术类评测依赖合成题目且缺乏题目层面的属性分析，因此需要构建一个具有教育效度、多格式、多语言的艺术史基准，以区分模型的识别能力与知识运用能力。

Method: 作者构建了 EduArt 基准，包含 871 道来自意大利中学练习和美国 AP 艺术史考试的人工编写题目，涵盖选择题、开放式填空、错误识别等 7 种格式，跨英语和意大利语两种语言。对 12 个模型（来自 6 个模型家族）在两种条件下（仅输出答案 vs. 要求书面论证）进行评测，采用经典测试理论（CTT）和心理计量学指标评估题目质量，并通过逻辑回归分离格式、语言、图像存在性和模型等因素对准确率的独立影响。

Result: 基准表现出良好的心理计量属性（平均区分度 0.514，82.3% 的题目为"良好区分器"）。选择题准确率在 6 个模型上接近天花板（超过 94%），但同一模型在开放式填空上降至 23.9%，在错误识别上仅 6.2%。格式是准确率的强独立预测因子。要求书面论证对准确率的影响以负面为主，且呈现模型家族依赖性。

Conclusion: 艺术史知识本身与运用知识的能力是两种不同的能力。单一格式（尤其是选择题）的基准测试会高估模型的真实能力。在艺术史学术研究中负责任地使用多模态 LLM，需要关注模型在生成和操控内容方面的能力分布，而非仅关注识别任务的表现。

Abstract: Large language models now score near ceiling on general benchmarks, but these aggregate measures reveal little about how models behave within single disciplines. Existing art-focused evaluations rely on synthetic questions and rarely report item-level properties. This paper introduces EduArt, an educational-level benchmark for art-historical knowledge and visual reasoning in multimodal LLMs. EduArt comprises 871 human-authored questions from Italian secondary-school exercises and US Advanced Placement Art History exams, spanning two languages and seven formats from multiple choice to in-text word placement and error identification. Twelve models from six provider families were evaluated under a default answer-only condition and a motivation condition requiring written justification, and characterized using Classical Test Theory and a logistic regression isolating the effects of format, language, image presence, and model. The benchmark showed strong psychometric properties (mean discrimination 0.514, 82.3 percent good discriminators), while multiple-choice accuracy saturated near ceiling for six models, showing recognition formats alone cannot distinguish frontier models. Format was a strong independent predictor of accuracy: models exceeding 94 percent on multiple choice fell to 23.9 percent on open completion (Claude Opus 4.6) and 6.2 percent on error identification (Claude Sonnet 4.6). The motivation condition changed accuracy in a predominantly negative, family-dependent direction. These dissociations indicate that art-historical knowledge and the ability to deploy it are distinct capabilities, and that single-format benchmarks overestimate what models can reliably do. Mapping this capability profile is a precondition for responsible use of multimodal LLMs in art-historical scholarship, where tasks demand producing and manipulating content rather than selecting from fixed options.

</details>


### [142] [OpenSafeIntent: Evaluating Intent-Calibrated Safe Completion Across Dual-Use Prompt Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02047)
*Rheeya Uppaal,Seungwoo Lyu,Selina Sung,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OpenSafeIntent基准，通过固定任务、变化意图（良性/双用途/恶意）的受控提示集，评估模型在意图校准下的安全完成行为，揭示提示级安全评估会掩盖关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估依赖孤立提示，无法捕捉模型在同一任务上随意图变化时的安全行为校准能力。需要在受控条件下评估模型是否真正理解意图并相应调整辅助水平，而非仅凭平均指标显得安全。

Method: 构建OpenSafeIntent基准，每个数据点包含同一任务的良性、双用途和恶意三种意图变体。通过跨模型套件测试，分析模型在不同意图变体间的一致安全性、双用途行为的改写鲁棒性、高风险主题的高级回答安全性，以及将模糊请求重构为安全任务时的安全边界跨越概率。

Result: 提示级安全评估掩盖重要失败：模型在匹配的意图变体间难以保持安全一致性；双用途行为在改写下脆弱；高风险主题的高级回答并非可靠安全；将模糊请求重构为安全任务可显著降低跨越安全边界的概率。

Conclusion: 安全完成应被评估为受控任务变体上的意图校准行为，而非独立提示上的单一安全-有用性权衡。OpenSafeIntent为细粒度安全评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Safe completion requires models to provide useful assistance without enabling harm, but this behavior is difficult to evaluate with isolated prompts. We introduce OpenSafeIntent, a benchmark of controlled prompt-sets that vary intent while holding the underlying task fixed. Each datapoint contains benign, dual-use, and malicious variants of the same task. This design lets us evaluate whether models calibrate assistance across intent shifts, rather than merely appearing safe on average. Across a broad model suite, we find that prompt-level safety hides important failures: models often fail to remain safe across matched intent variants, dual-use behavior is brittle under paraphrase, high-level answers on risky topics are not reliably safe, and responses that reframe ambiguous requests into safer tasks are substantially less likely to cross the safety boundary. Our results suggest that safe completion should be evaluated as intent-calibrated behavior over controlled task variants, not as a single safety-helpfulness tradeoff over independent prompts.

</details>


### [143] [SPLIT: Cross-Lingual Empathy and Cultural Grounding in English and Ukrainian LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02049)
*Anna Chorna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLIT基准，包含500条提示，覆盖五种情绪类别（压力、恐慌、孤独、内部流离、紧张），评估LLM在英语和乌克兰语中生成情感支持回复的能力。研究发现主流模型在切换至乌克兰语时性能下降，人类与AI评估者在文化基础维度上存在分歧，强调多语言情感支持不等于文化适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地部署于情感支持和危机场景，但其在低资源语言中的跨语言情感共情能力尚未被充分探索。现有基准侧重多语言性能，却很少考察危机相关的共情和文化基础，尤其缺乏对乌克兰语等中低资源语言的研究。

Method: 构建SPLIT基准（500条提示，覆盖压力、恐慌、孤独、内部流离、紧张五个类别）；从共情准确性、语言自然度、情境与文化基础三个维度评估三种LLM（Gemini-2.5-Flash、LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct、DeepSeek-V3）；在英语和乌克兰语两种语言下进行比较，并探索LLM-as-a-jury评估范式的可靠性。

Result: (1) Gemini-2.5-Flash和LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct在切换至乌克兰语时性能退化，而DeepSeek-V3相对稳定；(2) 人类评估者与AI评估者在共情和自然度上一致性较弱，在文化基础上则存在显著分歧；(3) 模型能生成乌克兰语文本，但不等于能提供乌克兰文化语境下的有效情感支持。

Conclusion: 生成乌克兰语文本与提供乌克兰语情感支持之间存在本质区别。研究呼吁未来基准设计应更注重文化定制，并强调以人为中心的评估方法在情感支持任务中的不可替代性。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in emotional-support contexts and crisis-related situations. Nevertheless, their cross-lingual abilities in these circumstances remain underexplored. Existing benchmarks emphasize multilingual performance but rarely examine crisis-related empathy and cultural grounding in low-to-mid-resource languages. We introduce SPLIT, a 500-prompt benchmark designed to evaluate LLM consistency in generating emotionally grounded responses across five categories: Stress, Panic, Loneliness, Internal Displacement, and Tension. We evaluate three technically diverse LLMs across three dimensions: Empathetic Accuracy, Linguistic Naturalness, and Contextual & Cultural Grounding. The framework aims to assess and compare the quality of LLM responses in both English and Ukrainian languages, as well as to explore the reliability of the LLM-as-a-jury paradigm. Our findings reveal that Gemini-2.5-Flash and LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct degrade when transitioning to Ukrainian, while DeepSeek-V3 remains comparatively stable within our benchmark. We additionally find that human and AI evaluators agree weakly on empathy and naturalness but diverge on cultural grounding. We further argue that producing Ukrainian text is not equivalent to producing Ukrainian emotional support. Our findings may assist in the future development of more culturally tailored benchmark designs, as well as encourage a stronger emphasis on human-centered evaluation.

</details>


### [144] [HaloGuard 1.0: An Open Weights Constitutional Classifier for Multilingual AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02079)
*Navaneeth Sangameswaran,Preetham S,Ashmiya Lenin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HaloGuard 1.0 是一个开源的输入安全防护模型，采用宪法分类器范式，以约十分之一的参数量在英/多语言提示安全基准上达到最优性能（0.8B版本平均F1=90.9，4B版本F1=92.1），同时保持低误报率。其核心包括46条安全政策与2,940子类别的安全宪法驱动合成数据生成、成对反事实样本、双层无害设计及46语言平衡覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前领先的开源防护模型体积庞大，且多语言安全检测能力不足。此外，训练数据构建缺乏系统性框架，导致误报（FP）和漏报（FN）难以精细化控制。作者旨在以更小模型实现更优的安全检测效果，并建立可解释、结构化的安全分类体系。

Method: 提出宪法分类器范式（constitutional-classifier paradigm）：(1) 设计包含46条政策、2,940子类别的自然语言安全宪法作为语料组织结构；(2) 基于宪法驱动合成数据生成，采用一对一严格配对反事实样本（固定主题和词汇，仅翻转意图）；(3) 双层无害设计，分别针对边界误报和基线误报进行优化；(4) 在46种语言上均衡采样，将语言视为表面形式而非对抗信号；(5) 持续对抗红队测试协议，防御内容级和智能体级攻击。

Result: 在7个提示安全基准上，HaloGuard 1.0-0.8B取得开放防护模型中最佳平均F1（90.9），FP率4.3，FN率9.5，优于高达27B参数的基线模型（超30倍体积）；4B版本平均F1达92.1，FP率降至3.5，额外算力用于提升精确率而非召回率。结构化裁定显示多数表面漏报实为基准标注错误。

Conclusion: 宪法分类器范式使得小模型（0.8B/4B）即可在提示安全检测任务上超越大型模型，且通过结构化宪法与双层无害设计实现了误报与漏报的精细化权衡。开源权重发布有助于社区复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: We present HaloGuard 1.0, an open-weights implementation of the constitutional-classifier paradigm for input safety. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on English and multilingual prompt-safety benchmarks at roughly one-tenth the model size of current leading open guard models. The safety constitution is the organising structure of the corpus: a natural-language constitution of 46 policies and 2,940 subcategories drives synthetic data generation, with exhaustive one-to-one paired counterfactuals that hold topic and vocabulary fixed while flipping intent, a two-tier harmless design that separately targets boundary and baseline false positives (FPs), and balanced multilingual materialisation across 46 languages that treats language as a surface form appearing on both sides of the boundary rather than as an adversarial signal. Across seven prompt-safety benchmarks, HaloGuard 1.0-0.8B attains the best average F1 (90.9) of any open guard we evaluate, outperforming baselines up to 27B parameters (over 30 times larger) while holding false-positive rate (FPR) to 4.3 and false-negative rate (FNR) to 9.5. The HaloGuard 1.0-4B variant reaches average F1 of 92.1 and FPR of 3.5, spending its extra capacity on precision rather than recall. A structured adjudication of the remaining failures indicates that most apparent missed-harm cases are benchmark mislabels rather than genuine model misses. An always-on adversarial red-teaming protocol continuously hardens the guard against both content-level and agentic attacks. We release the models as open weights.

</details>


### [145] [Unlocking Speech-Text Compositional Powers: Instruction-Following Speech Language Models without Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02214)
*Congrui Du,Yang Zhang,Kaizhi Qian,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpeechCombine 提出一种无需语音指令微调的语音语言模型训练方法：仅通过30k小时语音预训练得到语音适配模型，再将其权重与文本LLM的指令微调权重差异进行组合，即可将文本指令跟随能力迁移至语音领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音语言模型（SLM）的训练范式高度依赖大规模语音指令微调数据集，但语音序列远长于文本序列，导致扩展困难、成本高昂。需要一种避免大规模语音指令数据依赖的新训练路径。

Method: 以文本LLM为基座，首先在30k小时语音数据上进行连续预训练，获得语音适配模型；然后计算文本LLM基座与其指令微调版本之间的权重差，将该权重差直接叠加到语音适配模型的权重上，得到最终的指令跟随语音语言模型，全程无需任何语音指令微调。

Result: 简单的权重组合策略不仅能保留原始文本LLM的知识和能力，还能有效将其迁移至语音领域，验证了无需依赖大规模语音数据即可训练指令跟随SLM的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为SLM训练开辟了新方向，证明通过权重空间的算术组合即可实现跨模态能力迁移，从而规避对海量语音指令微调数据的依赖。

Abstract: Instruction tuning for speech language models (SLMs) is substantially more challenging than for text-based large language models (LLMs), as it requires learning a new modality and a wide range of speech-specific instructions in addition to those supported by text LLMs. Existing SLM training approaches largely replicate the text LLM training paradigm by synthesizing large-scale speech pre-training and instruction-tuning datasets. However, this strategy is difficult to scale, since speech sequences are significantly longer than text sequences. In this paper, we propose SpeechCombine, an instruction-following speech language model trained without any instruction tuning, using only a single round of speech pre-training on 30k hours of data. Starting from a text LLM base model, we perform continuous pre-training on speech utterances to obtain a speech-adapted model, and then directly combine its weights with the weight difference between the instruction-tuned and base versions of the text LLM. Our results show that this simple combination strategy not only preserves the knowledge and capabilities of the original text LLM, but also effectively transfers them to the speech domain. These findings suggest a new direction for SLM training that avoids reliance on massive speech data.

</details>


### [146] [Challenges and Recommendations for LLMs-as-a-Judge in Multilingual Settings and Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02235)
*A. Seza Doğruöz,Xixian Liao,Verena Blaschke,Jakob Prange,Senyu Li,David Ifeoluwa Adelani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统调查了ACL Anthology中在多语言/低资源语言场景下使用LLM-as-a-Judge的论文，发现仅33/650篇涉及该场景，且普遍存在评估不一致、过度信任LLM判断、依赖单一评判模型等问题，最终提出了针对性建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为NLG任务的主流评估范式，但其有效性主要在英语中得到验证。LLM在低资源语言中能力有限，且多语言场景下缺乏充分的人工验证，目前对该问题的范围和现有实践缺乏系统性了解。

Method: 对ACL Anthology中提及LLM-as-a-Judge的650篇论文进行系统筛选，识别出33篇聚焦多语言或低资源语言场景的论文，对其进行深入分析，考察评估一致性、对LLM判断的信任程度以及评判模型使用的多样性。

Result: 650篇提及LLM-as-a-Judge的论文中仅33篇（约5%）关注多语言/低资源场景；分析揭示三大问题：评估结果不一致、研究者倾向于过度信任多语言场景下的LLM判断、每项研究普遍仅依赖单一评判模型。

Conclusion: 当前多语言/低资源场景下LLM-as-a-Judge的使用存在严重的方法论缺陷和信任过度问题，作者提出了具体建议以帮助NLP社区更合理地在这一场景中使用LLM评判器。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become the dominant evaluation paradigm for many natural language generation tasks, due to shortcomings of conventional metrics and high correlations with human judgment, albeit mostly in English. There are now attempts to extend LLM-as-a-Judge to multilingual settings including low-resource languages. However, LLMs have limited proficiency in low-resource languages, and there is often no adequate human validation in these settings. To highlight the scope of the problem and current practices, we explore the use of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators in ACL Anthology papers focusing on multilingual settings and low-resource languages across a diverse set of tasks. Out of 650 papers mentioning LLM-as-a-judge, only 33 of them focus on low-resource or multilingual settings. Our in-depth analysis of these papers indicates inconsistent evaluation outcomes, a tendency to overtrust LLM judgments in multilingual settings, and the widespread reliance on a single judge model per study. To help the NLP community further, we conclude with recommendations about how to use LLM-as-a-Judge in multilingual and low-resource settings.

</details>


### [147] [BamiBERT: A New BERT-based Language Model for Vietnamese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02259)
*Dat Quoc Nguyen,Thinh Pham,Chi Tran,Linh The Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BamiBERT 是一个针对越南语的新型 BERT 预训练语言模型，在 129GB 通用领域语料上从头训练，支持 2048 token 上下文长度，无需外部分词，在 8 个基准测试的 15 项指标中 11 项取得最佳成绩，成为越南语"base"规模编码器的新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前越南语事实上的标准文本编码器 PhoBERT 存在关键局限性：需要依赖外部分词工具进行分词、上下文长度受限等。BamiBERT 旨在解决这些问题，提供一个更强、更易用的越南语预训练语言模型。

Method: 基于 BERT 架构从头训练：使用 129GB 通用领域越南语文本语料，训练 20 个 epoch；支持最长 2048 token 的上下文窗口；直接处理原始文本输入，无需外部词分割步骤。

Result: 在 8 个越南语 NLP 基准测试的 15 项评估指标中，11 项取得最佳成绩，3 项取得第二。在"base"规模的越南语编码器中达到新的 state of the art，并展现出较强的跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: BamiBERT 成功超越了 PhoBERT，成为越南语 base 级编码器的新标杆。模型已在 HuggingFace 上开源发布。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce BamiBERT, a new BERT-based pre-trained language model for Vietnamese that addresses key limitations of PhoBERT -- the current de facto Vietnamese text encoder. Trained from scratch on a 129GB corpus of general-domain Vietnamese text for 20 epochs, BamiBERT supports an extended context length of up to 2048 tokens and operates directly on raw input, eliminating the need for external word segmentation. Across 8 Vietnamese benchmarks, it achieves the best score on 11 of 15 metrics and the second-best on 3 others, setting a new state of the art among "base"-sized Vietnamese encoders and demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. We release BamiBERT at: https://huggingface.co/Qualcomm-AI-Research/BamiBERT

</details>


### [148] [CheckRLM: Effective Knowledge-Thought Coherence Checking in Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02262)
*Dingling Xu,Ruobing Wang,Qingfei Zhao,Yukun Yan,Zhichun Wang,Daren Zha,Shi Yu,Zhenghao Liu,Shuo Wang,Xu Han,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CheckRLM 是一个通过在推理过程中引入检索增强生成（RAG）来实时检测和纠正事实错误的框架，能有效减少长链推理中的错误累积，且成本较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型（RLM）虽然通过延长推理链提升了复杂任务表现，但其推理链容易包含事实错误，尤其在知识密集型任务中。现有方法缺乏在推理过程中及时检测和纠正这些细微知识不一致的机制。

Method: CheckRLM 框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 从推理链中提取事实声明，在推理过程中识别和定位细微的知识不一致；(2) 检测到错误后，通过利用外部知识进行最小成本的精确修正，保证推理链与正确知识之间的连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CheckRLM 显著优于现有基线方法，在长链推理中展现出强大的错误累积抑制能力，同时保持较低的计算/调用成本。

Conclusion: CheckRLM 通过 RAG 驱动的实时检错与修正机制，有效提升了推理语言模型在知识密集型任务中的可靠性，为减少长链推理的错误累积提供了一种低成本、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) have significantly improved performance on complex tasks by extending the reasoning chain. However, these chains are prone to containing factual errors, particularly in knowledge-intensive tasks. To address this issue, we propose CheckRLM, a framework that improves the reliability of the reasoning process through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) by timely checking and correcting factual errors. Specifically, CheckRLM extracts factual claims from the reasoning chain to identify and localize subtle knowledge inconsistencies during inference. Upon detection of errors, a refinement mechanism performs minimal-cost yet precise corrections by leveraging external knowledge, ensuring coherence between the reasoning chain and correct knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CheckRLM substantially outperforms existing baselines, exhibiting a strong capability to mitigate error accumulation in long-horizon reasoning with lower costs. The code and data are available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/CheckRLM.

</details>


### [149] [On the Role of Directionality in Structural Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02307)
*Zichao Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对SLOG基准测试中涉及方向性区分的任务类别，指出现有SOTA方法AM-Parser的代数操作未编码方向信息。作者基于CCG有向类型重新设计了符号后端（确定性CKY+单线性解码器），在相同BERT编码器下超越AM-Parser，方向性类别提升显著；配合更强编码器后，递归深度类别的瓶颈也得以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLOG测试中的多个类别显式涉及方向性区分（如修饰语位置变换、论元抽取位置），但此前最优的AM-Parser所采用的AM代数操作并未编码方向信息，导致在方向敏感任务上存在系统性缺陷。作者旨在通过引入方向感知的符号表示来解决这一结构性瓶颈。

Method: 围绕CCG（组合范畴语法）有向类型重新设计符号后端，采用确定性CKY解析算法配合单一线性解码器，仅含30K可学习参数。编码器端分别测试了BERT-base和DeBERTa-v3-large两种配置，用于对比方向性表示与编码器能力各自的贡献。

Result: 在BERT-base编码器下，CCG系统达到75.9±6.4%的LF完全匹配率，超越AM-Parser的70.8±4.3%。按SLOG类别分组：CCG系统在全部5个位置变换类别上远超AM-Parser（+29.9个百分点），而AM-Parser在全部6个递归深度类别上仍占优。换用DeBERTa-v3-large后，整体提升至90.7±4.9%，编码器增益主要集中在递归深度类别，与方向性改进形成互补。

Conclusion: 方向性表示将系统瓶颈从符号层（AM-Parser在相关类别上存在0%的性能天花板）转移到了神经层，使得更强的编码器能够有效发挥作用。这表明方向感知的符号归纳偏置对于结构化语义解析至关重要，且与编码器升级形成互补增益。

Abstract: Several SLOG test categories explicitly involve directional distinctions (modifier position shifts, argument extraction positions), yet AM-Parser, the previous SOTA, uses an AM algebra whose operations do not encode direction. We redesign the symbolic backend around CCG directed types (deterministic CKY + single linear decoder, 30K learnable parameters). Under the same BERT-base encoder, the system achieves 75.9$\pm$6.4% LF exact match, surpassing AM-Parser (70.8$\pm$4.3%). Per SLOG's own category groupings, gains are highly directional: the CCG system outperforms AM-Parser on all 5 position-shift categories (+29.9pp), while AM-Parser outperforms on all 6 recursive-depth categories. Replacing the encoder with DeBERTa-v3-large yields 90.7$\pm$4.9%, with the largest encoder gains in recursive-depth categories, complementary to directionality's gains. Directional representations shift the bottleneck from the symbolic layer (AM-Parser's 0% category ceiling) to the neural layer, which improves with encoder upgrades.

</details>


### [150] [World Wide Models: Literary Tools for Cultural AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02369)
*Nina Begus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出，大语言模型（LLM）构成了一种大规模、自动化且单语化的新型文化相遇形态，主张借助文学学科的比较阅读、叙事学、批评理论和世界文学等工具来构建具有文化素养的AI，并发展出一个分层框架，以应对全球AI文本性中的结构性单语主义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM以大规模、自动化、单语化的方式重塑了文化相遇的形态，而当前AI开发缺乏对文化复杂性和多元性的足够关注。作者认为，文学学科长期积累的分析工具——比较阅读、叙事学、批评理论、世界文学与翻译——对于构建具有文化素养的AI已不可或缺，亟需将这些人文方法系统性地融入AI发展。

Method: 论文构建了一个分层框架（layered framework），将文学研究与AI开发进行交叉整合。具体路径包括：连接当代批评理论辩论与结构性单语主义问题；借鉴世界文学方法中的宏观结构（macrostructure）、流通（circulation）与不可译性（untranslatability）等概念，来分析和应对全球AI文本性。

Result: 该分层框架指向更精细的文本模型与更具多元性的AI阐释方式，揭示了文学学科工具在诊断和缓解AI单语主义偏见方面的核心作用，并为全球AI文本性的文化分析提供了可操作的概念工具。

Conclusion: 文学分析工具已成为构建文化素养型AI的必备资源。通过世界文学视角重新审视AI的文本性，能够有效挑战结构性单语主义，推动AI向更加多元、文化敏感的阐释方向发展。

Abstract: LLMs stage a new form of cultural encounter that is massive, automated, and monolingual. Literary disciplines have always negotiated cultural struggles with comparative reading of literature, narratological and poetic analysis, critical theory, world literature, and translation. These tools have now become indispensable for building culturally literate AI. The essay develops a layered framework toward more nuanced textual models and pluralistic interpretations of AI, emphasizing the natural intersections of literature and AI development, connecting current debates in critical theory with structural monolingualism, and suggesting a new application of world literature approaches to address global AI textuality through macrostructure, circulation, and untranslatability.

</details>


### [151] [HULAT2 at MER-TRANS 2026: Governed Multi-Agent Simplification for Spanish Easy-to-Read Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02381)
*Lourdes Moreno,Paloma Martínez,Marco Antonio Sanchez-Escudero,Miguel Domínguez-Gómez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HULAT2-UC3M团队在MER-TRANS 2026多语言简易阅读翻译共享任务的西班牙语赛道中，提交了三种全自动方案。其基于LangGraph的多智能体工作流（结合Gemini 2.5 Flash与RigoChat-7B-v2，采用信号引导路由）在SARI指标上取得了最佳成绩（44.05），优于基于LoRA适配的线性再生基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索面向简易阅读（Easy-to-Read）翻译的全自动方法，具体目标是验证信号引导的多智能体路由架构是否优于传统的生成-评估-再生成线性基线，以及额外的词汇支持层能否进一步提升参考指标导向的翻译质量。

Method: 提出三种全自动西班牙语简易阅读翻译方案：(1) RUN1: 基于LangGraph的多智能体工作流，整合Gemini 2.5 Flash和RigoChat-7B-v2，采用并行生成策略、内部质量信号、事件-条件-动作（ECA）路由、受控编辑与可追溯决策；(2) RUN2: 在RUN1基础上增加基于术语表与词汇资源的词汇支持层；(3) RUN3: 基于RigoChat的生成-评估-再生成线性基线，结合提示工程与LoRA适配。评估使用官方指标BLEU-Orig、BLEU-Gold、SARI和BERTScore，开发阶段还监控了语义保真度、可读性、词汇简洁性、句法清晰度与事实一致性等内部信号。

Result: 在官方排行榜上，RUN1取得最佳SARI得分44.0543，RUN2为43.1049，RUN3为38.5136。结果表明信号引导的多智能体路由架构显著优于线性再生基线，但添加词汇支持层并未自动提升基于参考的评分。

Conclusion: 在本次任务设定下，信号引导的多智能体ECA路由策略是有效的简易阅读翻译范式；然而词汇支持层的引入需进一步审慎设计方能转化为参考指标上的增益。未来需从段落级与文档级层面深入分析可读性、事实一致性及面向用户的使用充分性。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [152] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 MEDIAREF，一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，旨在以低成本、可复现的方式评估和改进 LLM 在自动化事实核查中的媒体背景审查（MBC）生成任务，覆盖 200 个媒体来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 RAG 的自动事实核查系统假设检索到的证据是可靠的，但现实中的信息可能矛盾、过时或来自不可靠/有偏见的来源。虽然已有工作通过媒体背景审查（MBC）评估信源可信度来应对这一挑战，但生成 MBC 依赖昂贵的商业搜索 API，限制了可复现性。

Method: 构建 MEDIAREF——一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，覆盖 200 个媒体来源；提出可复现的构建与更新方法；在此知识库上对主流 LLM 的 MBC 生成任务进行评估，并进行自动评估与定性评估。

Result: MEDIAREF 支持更高质量的 MBC 生成，且不依赖昂贵的商业搜索 API，同时具备可复现性。自动评估和定性评估均验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF 为低成本、可复现的源批判推理研究提供了可行方案，有望推动自动事实核查领域中基于信源可信度的下游验证任务发展。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [153] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）兴起后，NLP研究的发表场所从传统*ACL会议向通用机器学习（ML）会议的迁移趋势，并发现ML会议存在显著的引用溢价，驱动了这一转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的进步模糊了NLP与通用ML之间的学科界限，研究者想知道NLP领域的发表重心是否正在从传统NLP会议向通用ML会议转移。

Method: 利用2010至2026年发表的NLP研究成果，从两个角度展开分析：(1) 对比LLM时代前后，资深作者在*ACL主会、Findings子刊及通用ML会议中的发表份额变化；(2) 追踪新锐作者（以至少三篇一作NLP论文出道）的发表场所分布变化（2019–2024）。进一步采用因果推断方法，估计通用ML会议对论文引用量的因果效应（引用溢价）。

Result: 资深作者在*ACL主会论文份额下降19.2个百分点，在Findings子刊上升14.8个百分点，在通用ML会议上升8.6个百分点。新锐作者中，主要发表于*ACL的比例从84%（2019）降至74%（2024），主要发表于通用ML会议的比例从5%升至21%。因果推断表明，通用ML会议带来显著的引用溢价，影响投稿选择。

Conclusion: NLP研究的发表场所正发生显著迁移，从传统*ACL会议向通用ML会议转移，引用溢价是驱动该趋势的重要因素。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [154] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张NLP的文化测量不是被动记录而是"物质-话语实践"——测量装置本身参与构成文化现实。借助Karen Barad的"能动切割"概念，通过三个电视电影对白案例和三个装置审视，提出一个理论驱动、经验严谨、文化 contingent 的研究纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型越来越多地用于量化文化现象，但学界对何种测量构成"独特的文化测量"缺乏反思。作者指出，NLP文化研究往往忽视测量装置（模型、数据、标注、评估）本身已深度卷入它所测量的文化材料，这种"纠缠"需要被理论化。

Method: 以Karen Barad的"能动切割"（agential cut）为核心分析框架，将NLP文化测量重新概念化为一种"物质-话语实践"。通过三组案例分析：前三个聚焦电视电影对白中的结构、互动与偏离测量；后三个审视装置本身——文化标记的抹除、对历史材料的调谐、智能体工作流中的能动性。

Result: 分析表明，装置的每一项实质性设计选择都在划定现象与工具之间的边界，且该边界从一开始就是纠缠的，因为语言模型已内化了大量被测量的文化材料。文化测量的"客观性"因此是一种被建构的产物，而非对独立实在的反映。

Conclusion: 提出一个新的研究纲领：每一次"能动切割"都应被视为有意识的方法论承诺与伦理承诺。文化测量研究需要做到理论驱动、经验严谨、文化 contingent，承认并反思测量行为本身的构成性力量。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [155] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过缩放定律研究LLM计算规模与社会模拟保真度之间的关系。在85个Qwen3架构的Transformer模型（10¹⁸-10²⁰ FLOPs）和35个最大70B参数的开源模型上进行实验，涵盖观点建模、行为模拟和纵向预测三个子领域。结果表明：行为和观点模拟任务随规模显著提升，但纵向预测和代表性不足群体的观点扩展较慢；行为模拟中，模型对人类认知偏差（如风险厌恶）的校准能力不随规模改善，微调也无法在0.5B到8B参数范围内提升性能。结论是：规模在多数场景下能提升社会模拟质量，但在低资源领域存在异常值且改进不那么可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM社会模拟虽有前景但保真度不足。本文试图回答一个关键问题：当前语言模型的缩放范式能否弥合这些差距，还是说模拟保真度与通用能力是正交的、值得独立研究？

Method: 采用缩放定律方法：(1) 在固定计算预算（10¹⁸至10²⁰ FLOPs）下，使用DCLM网络文本语料预训练85个Qwen3架构Transformer模型；(2) 评估35个更大规模（最大70B参数）的开源模型，从损失值预测下游准确率；(3) 在观点建模、行为模拟、纵向预测三个子领域测量社会模拟保真度与计算规模的关系。

Result: 三个子领域均发现显著的计算缩放效应，但存在分化：(1) 多数行为和观点模拟任务随规模快速改善，尤其是英文网络语料中充分代表的人群；(2) 纵向预测和代表性不足的观点扩展较慢，尤其当任务与MMLU等通用知识推理基准相关性较低时；(3) 行为模拟中，模型对人类认知偏差（如风险厌恶）的校准能力以及从相关任务学习关联奖励的人类启发式方面，缩放失效，微调也无法在0.5B-8B参数范围内提升性能。

Conclusion: 规模将在多数场景下改善社会模拟，但存在异常值，且在低资源领域中改进的可靠性较低。社会模拟保真度并非完全与通用能力正交，但某些特定维度确实需要超越单纯缩放的专项研究。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [156] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过从LibriVox有声书中提取声学特征（音调、语速、响度等），系统性地研究了旁白叙述质量与有声书消费数据（观看率）之间的关系，发现声学信息本身与吸引力存在稳健关联，且该效应在控制书名影响后依然显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旁白叙述是有声书聆听体验的核心，但叙述质量如何影响有声书的吸引力尚未被系统性地量化研究。不同体裁、书名和受众可能调节这种效应，因此需要数据驱动的方法来揭示这些关系，以优化有声书个性化和旁白选角。

Method: 利用预训练音频模型从LibriVox有声书中提取声学和嗓音特征（如音调、语速、响度等）；将这些特征与消费数据（以观看率view-rate衡量）进行关联分析；考察特征与体裁（genre）和书名（title）的交互效应；进一步使用更细粒度的专有参与度指标进行验证。

Result: 尽管消费数据有限，声学信息本身与有声书吸引力之间存在稳健的关联，且在控制书名效应后该关联依然成立；更细粒度的专有参与度指标进一步验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地将旁白叙述质量、体裁、书名与有声书消费联系起来进行计算研究的工作，证明了数据驱动的声学分析在改进有声书个性化推荐和旁白选角方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [157] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VRRL（视觉反思强化学习）框架，通过随机掩码轨迹前缀和缓冲回放机制，增强LVLM在思维链推理中的视觉基础自反思能力，显著提升分布外场景下的视觉推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在进行思维链推理时，虽表现出自反思行为（即回顾并修正早期错误），但反思过程中往往未能充分关注视觉输入，导致无法将反馈转化为基于视觉证据的有效修正，尤其在分布外图像上表现不佳。核心痛点是"反思缺乏视觉基础"。

Method: 提出VRRL强化学习训练框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 训练时随机掩码轨迹前缀，迫使模型从错误的中间预测中恢复，而非仅在早期出错；(2) 引入经验回放缓冲区（buffered roll-ins），将模型暴露于多样化的失败状态，迫使模型学会纠正这些错误。

Result: 在表格、图表视觉定位任务及空间导航基准上评估。现成模型和传统微调模型在分布偏移下性能大幅下降，而VRRL通过有效利用自反思，在分布外准确率上显著优于标准RL和面向反思的微调基线方法。

Conclusion: 视觉基础的自反思对LVLM的鲁棒推理至关重要。VRRL通过强化学习显式训练模型在推理链中进行视觉扎根的自我修正，有效缓解了分布外泛化问题，为提升多模态模型的反思质量提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [158] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DramaSR-532K大规模基准数据集（53.2万条标注对白，900+角色）和DramaSR-LRM方法，利用大推理模型融合听觉、语言和视觉多模态线索进行说话人识别，在短对白场景下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长剧集视频理解的核心挑战在于将每句对白准确归属到对应角色（即说话人识别），这需要综合听觉声纹、语义语境和视觉信息，而现有方法往往无法有效整合这些多模态线索，尤其在短对白场景下声学特征不可靠。

Method: 提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大推理模型（LRM），通过多模态工具调用自主聚合上下文证据——整合听觉（声纹）、语言（对话语义）和视觉（人物画面）三种模态信息，实现高保真的说话人归因。

Result: 实验表明DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，特别是在短对白（声学生物特征不可靠）场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模多模态基准数据集DramaSR-532K和提出基于大推理模型的DramaSR-LRM方法，有效推动了长剧集视频中说话人识别任务的发展，代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [159] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LACUNA 是首个具备参数级定位真值的遗忘测试基准，通过在预定义权重中注入合成个体PII，揭示现有SOTA遗忘方法虽输出表现良好但定位不精确且易受重浮现攻击；当定位准确时，简单梯度方法即可实现强擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准仅评估输出层面，无法判断遗忘是否真正从参数中擦除知识还是仅混淆了知识，且重浮现攻击的成功加剧了这一担忧，缺乏参数级真值的评估框架。

Method: 构建LACUNA测试基准：通过掩码持续预训练将合成个体的PII注入1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，实现参数级定位真值；以此评估SOTA遗忘方法的定位精度、擦除效果及抗重浮现攻击鲁棒性。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法尽管输出层面表现强劲，但参数定位极不精确且易受重浮现攻击；而当定位成功时，即使是简单的梯度遗忘方法也能实现强擦除和对重浮现攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确的参数定位是鲁棒遗忘的关键，LACUNA作为首个参数级真值遗忘基准，可补充行为评估并推动基于定位的遗忘研究。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: X-VAE 提出用预训练自编码器的隐变量经验分布替代标准正态先验，构建数据自适应高斯先验，并引入隐变量缩放因子以控制生成样本的多样性与保真度之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE假设隐空间服从各向同性标准高斯先验，但该假设对复杂数据集往往偏离真实的隐表示分布，导致重构精度下降、样本质量不足及隐空间表达能力受限。

Method: 首先预训练一个自编码器（AE），提取其隐编码的经验均值和标准差，以此参数化一个数据自适应的高斯先验，替代标准正态先验；在生成阶段引入隐变量缩放因子，显式调控采样隐向量的方差；同时推导了该先验对应的KL散度目标函数。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验表明，X-VAE在保持重构质量的同时，生成的隐表示与经验数据分布更对齐，可控性增强，生成样本更真实。

Conclusion: X-VAE通过数据自适应先验和缩放因子机制，有效弥合了先验与真实隐分布之间的不匹配，在工业设计等需严格约束的场景中展现出良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [161] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LF-IBIS是一种无需似然函数的贝叶斯强化学习算法,结合近似贝叶斯计算与迭代批量重要性采样,在环境动力学无显式似然函数的情况下实现全贝叶斯推断,输出环境参数与最优策略的后验分布,并通过临床试验模拟和在线策略更新实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数贝叶斯强化学习方法依赖显式似然函数,但在现实场景中似然函数往往不可获取或难以处理。因此需要一种无需似然函数即可进行贝叶斯推断的BRL方法。

Method: 提出LF-IBIS算法,将近似贝叶斯计算(ABC)与迭代批量重要性采样(IBIS)相结合,在线更新智能体信念,在无显式似然函数条件下生成环境参数和最优策略的近似后验分布。

Result: 在临床试验的响应自适应随机化模拟研究中,通过闭式后验验证了方法有效性;在无闭式后验的场景中,成功实现了基于最优策略后验分布的在线策略更新。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS有效解决了无显式似然函数条件下的BRL问题,能够量化策略不确定性,为探索-利用权衡的贝叶斯处理提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [162] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，在一般缺失机制下建立了√n超额风险界并证明了估计中心的一致性，在MCAR机制下推导了√n收敛速率和渐近正态性，并给出了估计中心收敛到真实中心的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典k-means无法直接用于不完全数据，现有方法多关注实际聚类精度而缺乏渐近理论保证。本文旨在填补缺失数据下k-means聚类统计性质研究的理论空白。

Method: 基于经验风险最小化框架，在一般缺失机制下建立√n超额风险界和一致性；在MCAR机制下推导√n收敛速率和渐近正态性；分析不完全数据估计中心收敛到完整数据真实中心的充分条件（涉及缺失概率与簇分离度），并揭示高维情形下各维度中心需互异的挑战。

Result: 在一般缺失机制下得到√n超额风险界和一致性；MCAR下获得√n收敛速率和渐近正态性；证明了估计中心收敛到真实中心需满足缺失概率与簇分离度的充分条件；发现MCAR下达到√n速率和收敛到真实中心均要求k个真实中心在每个维度上互异，揭示了高维应用的显著挑战。数值模拟支持了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据k-means聚类提供了完整的理论保证，从超额风险界、一致性、收敛速率到渐近正态性均给出了严格证明。同时揭示了高维情形下各维度中心需互异的必要条件，指出了高维应用的根本性困难。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [163] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的方法论，结合Ghost变量与Shapley值，定义自相关性与偏自相关性函数作为滞后重要性度量，并设计了一种基于单步预测的缺失特征替代策略，通过模拟与真实数据验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列分析中的自相关函数（ACF）和偏自相关函数（PACF）依赖于线性假设，无法直接适用于机器学习等黑箱预测模型。作者旨在开发一种模型无关的滞后相关性度量框架，填补这一空白。

Method: 结合Ghost变量框架与Shapley值加性重要性度量，提出自相关性（auto-relevance）和偏自相关性（partial auto-relevance）函数来衡量滞后变量的重要性。针对基于联盟的方法中缺失特征问题，提出用同一模型的单步预测值来替代缺失特征的创新方法。在季节性ARMA族模型和循环神经网络（RNN）上进行了模拟与真实数据评估。

Result: 所提出的相关性度量在几乎所有测试案例中都能成功展示出预期的滞后结构，验证了该方法在识别时间序列滞后依赖关系方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该联合框架特别适用于时间序列场景，能够为机器学习预测模型提供可解释的滞后结构分析，具有模型无关性和广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [164] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种范式转换：将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题替换为参数密度上的连续变分代理问题。通过在加权Sobolev空间中定义一族λ-凸泛函，证明了该变分问题是全局适定的、稳定的，且具有近乎C³的正则性。该方法可直接利用椭圆正则性和凸分析，最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，无需迭代优化，并给出了显式的泛化误差控制和有限宽度网络的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经网络优化的理论基础薄弱，非凸景观和随机启发式方法使得理论分析困难。现有的Wasserstein梯度流或平均场方法面临正则性不足和离散化挑战。作者希望建立一个具有良好数学性质（适定性、正则性、凸性）的连续框架，从根本上解决这些问题。

Method: 将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题重新表述为加权Sobolev空间中参数密度上的λ-凸变分问题。利用椭圆正则性理论和凸分析方法，证明最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，从而绕过传统迭代优化。进一步通过变分演算框架分析泛化误差和有限宽度网络的逼近速率。

Result: (1) 变分问题全局适定、稳定，且解具有近乎C³正则性；(2) 最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，无需迭代优化；(3) 泛化误差相对于正则化参数α的收敛速率为1/α；(4) 规模为N的有限宽度网络以O(1/N)速率收敛到连续最优解。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了神经正切核（NTK）机制与特征学习机制之间的鸿沟，通过变分演算的视角为理解过参数化提供了一个原则性框架，为神经网络优化的理论基础开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


### [165] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向反事实决策的不确定性量化框架，引入"策略耦合覆盖"这一新概念，将其作为不确定性与行动之间的最优接口，并开发了PC-RACP两阶段方法，在有限样本下实现覆盖保证的同时获得更高效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预测驱动的反事实决策中，统计有效性（如共形预测的覆盖保证）与决策最优性之间存在鸿沟：覆盖保证独立于决策规则，无法直接指导"该采取什么行动"。这在反事实场景中尤为突出——实际发生的结果依赖于所采取的行动，不确定性无法与决策规则分离来指定。

Method: 作者构建了一个决策论框架：(1) 定义"策略耦合覆盖"——即由预测集自身诱导的行动下所实现结果的覆盖率，作为不确定性与行动的最优无损接口；(2) 证明在该覆盖下，自然的最大-最小规则在分布模糊性下是极小极大最优的；(3) 证明在策略耦合覆盖下优化预测集等价于更强的通用覆盖形式，也等价于对策略和效用证书的直接风险规避优化，并由此导出总体最优预测集的显式形式；(4) 提出PC-RACP两阶段算法：第一阶段估计倾向得分和条件结果分布，第二阶段通过风险规避优化构造预测集，具备有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 理论层面，建立了策略耦合覆盖与极小极大最优性、通用覆盖、风险规避优化三者之间的等价关系，并给出了总体最优预测集的显式解。实验层面，模拟数据和真实邮件营销实验均表明，PC-RACP在保持有效覆盖的同时，比现有方法获得更高效用；忽视决策问题的反事实结构对有效性和效用都是次优的。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是连接不确定性量化与反事实决策的关键桥梁。PC-RACP方法在理论和实践上都证明，将反事实结构纳入预测集构造过程，能够同时提升统计有效性和决策效用。该框架为高 stakes 预测驱动决策提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [166] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种加性深度学习框架，将物理化学描述符（MLP化学分支）与分子图拓扑（GNN结构分支）分离编码，仅在预测阶段通过加性模型组合，从而实现对化学与结构贡献的透明分解。结合预训练-微调策略，在AqSolDB和BigSolDB2数据集上取得了竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水溶性预测模型大多将物理化学描述符和分子图信息融合为单一表征，导致无法区分预测是由整体化学性质、分子结构还是两者共同驱动的，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 设计了一个双分支加性深度学习框架：化学分支用MLP编码物理化学描述符，结构分支用GNN编码分子图拓扑，两者仅在预测阶段通过加性模型（可选乘性交互项）组合。采用先在AqSolDB上预训练、再在BigSolDB2上微调的策略。解释性分析包括：分支输出的最佳线性投影、溶解性类别间的分子级嵌入汇总、以及基于GNNExplainer在官能团层面聚合的原子级掩码。

Result: 化学分支与已知的物理化学描述符对齐良好，结构分支捕获了与溶解性相关的图拓扑和官能团模式。预训练-微调策略显著提升了精度并降低了运行间差异。在两个数据集上均取得了竞争性预测性能。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持竞争性预测精度的同时，首次实现了化学信息与结构信息对水溶性预测贡献的透明分离，为药物发现早期的分子性质预测提供了更具可解释性的工具。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [167] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过对GPT-4o模拟的美国人及华裔美国人人格进行IPIP-50问卷测试，发现LLM的人格表达包含两个可分离的成分：聚合特征（大五人格得分）对问题顺序干扰具有鲁棒性，而几何特征（SPD流形上的协方差结构）高度依赖框架对齐。这一"崩溃-恢复"模式揭示了人格几何并非内在属性，而是框架依赖的协调模式，为LLM人格评估建立了"双重本质"框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM人格评估依赖聚合分数，丢弃了项目间的相关结构信息。研究者旨在检验这种几何结构是LLM的内在固有属性，还是依赖于问卷框架（如题目顺序）的产物，从而揭示当前基于心理测量问卷的评估方法的根本局限。

Method: 使用IPIP-50问卷测试GPT-4o模拟的美国人和华裔美国人两种人格；通过操控题目顺序（共享框架 vs. 错位框架），构建项目间相关矩阵，并在对称正定（SPD）流形上分析其几何结构；对比聚合特征（大五得分）和几何特征在不同框架条件下的稳定性与恢复能力。

Result: 聚合特征在随机化下下降21%但对框架具有鲁棒性；几何特征在框架错位下崩溃（下降42%），但在共享框架下大幅恢复至84%，超过聚合特征的恢复水平（76%）。这一崩溃-恢复模式表明人格几何是框架依赖的协调模式，而非内在属性。

Conclusion: LLM人格具有"双重本质"：聚合特征框架鲁棒但信息有限，几何特征框架依赖但编码了聚合所不可见的丰富信息。这对LLM人格评估提出了框架感知的必要性，并挑战了静态特质观的传统人格概念。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多层Q矩阵嵌入的神经网络认知诊断模型（M-QCDNet），将Q矩阵作为结构先验融入神经网络，结合L2惩罚损失函数平衡预测性能与结构对齐，并开发了基于可解释对齐的评估指标，旨在实现兼具心理测量透明度和神经灵活性的可解释AI认知诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前认知诊断领域存在心理测量模型（如CDMs）可解释性强但灵活性不足，而深度学习神经网络虽预测能力强却缺乏结构可解释性的矛盾。亟需一种既能保持潜在掌握轮廓在认知理论上可解释、又具备神经网络表达灵活性的融合方法。

Method: 提出M-QCDNet框架：（1）利用Q矩阵作为结构先验，在神经网络中显式建模题目-技能关系，确保潜在掌握轮廓的可解释性；（2）设计带有L2惩罚项的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不一致的技能激活施加惩罚，实现预测性能与结构对齐的平衡；（3）开发基于可解释对齐的评估矩阵，量化预测技能激活与题目级技能之间的对应程度。

Result: M-QCDNet能够在保持诊断效度的同时实现良好的预测性能，可早期识别学习困难并支持基于掌握的干预措施，提出的可解释对齐指标能有效量化模型的结构一致性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet成功将诊断效度嵌入模型设计，弥合了心理测量透明度与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟，推动了面向认知诊断的可解释、公平且可操作的AI发展，具有重要的课堂实践价值。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [169] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对跨被试EEG压力检测的频域-时域联合建模方法IIRMANet，在SPD流形空间中按频率点独立构建协方差矩阵，并通过频率聚类聚合和切片内-切片间注意力机制，有效融合频率特异性特征与全局时序上下文，在三数据集上以仅1.60M参数量达到82.78%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG压力检测面临两大挑战：(1) 压力相关模式既依赖于被试个体又具有频率特异性，传统黎曼方法仅在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了关键的神经振荡信息；(2) 标准时序分词策略会割裂切片间的时序连贯性。因此需要一种能同时保留通道几何结构、频率特异性线索以及全局时序上下文的方法。

Method: 提出IIRMANet（Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network），核心包含三个模块：(1) 在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵，并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道几何结构与频点特异性判别信息；(2) 频率聚类聚合模块，自动形成与EEG节律对齐的数据驱动频率簇，筛选信息丰富的频谱成分并降低冗余；(3) 切片内-切片间注意力模块，自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态与跨EEG序列的全局时序上下文。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上一致优于五种SOTA基线方法，最高平衡准确率达82.78%，同时模型仅1.60M参数量和31.95M FLOPs，具有较高效率。

Conclusion: 通过将黎曼流形建模从时域拓展到频域，并联合频率聚类与时序注意力机制，IIRMANet有效解决了跨被试压力检测中频率特异性和时序连贯性兼顾的难题，在性能与效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [170] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了编程示例推断（PBE）系统中的最坏情况对抗性示例攻击，提出了版本空间划分聚合（VPA）防御策略。研究发现在低边际的PBE任务中存在随机噪声评估无法捕捉的对抗鲁棒性维度，VPA仅在干净语义保持划分投票边际时才有效，而这在现实任务中往往失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PBE鲁棒性研究通常将错误示例建模为随机噪声，但忽略了恶意攻击者精心选择示例以最大化损害合成程序质量这一更危险的场景。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的对抗鲁棒性维度。

Method: 1. 形式化有限PBE版本空间上的固定集最坏情况破坏问题；2. 为字符串转换DSL实现了有界池内精确搜索和启发式破坏搜索；3. 提出版本空间划分聚合（VPA）防御，在不相交示例组上合成程序并通过语义签名投票；4. 在策划/生成的DSL任务、SyGuS PBE_SLIA切片、SYNTRA Playgol v2及噪声PBE基线等多种数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 一次策划编辑即可翻转全部8个spike任务（随机控制仅10.3%-16.7%成功）；生成的低边际行在预算1下被翻转但VPA可恢复；在公开SyGuS上投票边际接近1，自适应攻击者使VPA准确率降为零；Playgol在141个接受行上显示正配对自助法间隙；小型精确输出提示实验在20个受控边际-1任务上，本地和API模型均呈现相同的干净到被攻击的定性模式。

Conclusion: 低边际PBE任务确实存在对抗鲁棒性维度，随机干扰评估无法捕捉这一风险；VPA仅在干净语义保持足够投票边际时才有效，在现实任务中往往不满足该条件，因此防御效果有限。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [171] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于心电图识别，通过将PRQST关键标志点作为领域知识，构建双流有向图分别建模心电周期内和跨周期的时空依赖关系，在九分类任务中取得了优于现有模型的性能，尤其对罕见类别提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型在医疗等专业领域（如心电图识别）中可解释性不足，单纯依赖端到端卷积神经网络难以捕捉心电图领域专家所关注的PRQST关键标志点及其时空关系，需要将领域知识显式融入模型以提升性能和可解释性。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络：1）引入PRQST关键标志点作为领域知识；2）构建双流有向图——空间有向图捕捉关键点间的空间位置关系（intra-cycle），时间有向图刻画相邻心电周期间的时间依赖关系（inter-cycle）；3）在首届中国心电图智能大赛九分类数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在九分类心电图识别任务上，模型整体平均F1分数达88.1%，罕见类别平均F1分数达76.3%，均优于现有最优模型；引入领域知识显著提升了检测性能，尤其是对罕见类别的识别效果。

Conclusion: 将心电图领域的PRQST关键点知识显式融入图卷积网络，通过双流有向图同时建模心电信号的周期内空间结构与跨周期时序依赖，是一种有效提升心电图识别性能和可解释性的方法，对罕见类别检测的提升尤为突出。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [172] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了多探针网格（multiprobe grid）近似最近邻搜索算法在数据集规模 N 和维度 d 上的扩展特性，发现其在 GloVe 嵌入上存在此前未被报道的维度扩展交叉现象——多探针网格保持近似恒定的维度扩展指数，而其他基于图、树和分区的方法吞吐量则持续退化。该方法还具有近线性的查询扩展和更低的索引构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的 ANN 方法在现代大规模扩展分析中一直缺位。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地刻画网格方法在 N 和 d 两个维度上的扩展行为，并探究其在索引构建频繁或高维场景下的竞争力，同时为高效 Transformer 架构的成本分析提供参考。

Method: 采用多探针网格算法（multiprobe grid），在 GloVe 嵌入数据集族上，系统性地对数据集规模 N 和维度 d 进行扩展实验，并与基于图、树和空间分区的代表性 ANN 方法进行吞吐量和索引成本的对比分析。

Result: 发现一个此前未被报道的 d-扩展交叉现象：多探针网格的维度扩展指数近似恒定，而对比方法则随 d 增大吞吐量显著退化。在 N 上的查询扩展近乎线性，索引构建成本低于其他 ANN 方法。

Conclusion: 网格类方法（如多探针网格）在索引重建频繁或高维场景中具有竞争力，其维度鲁棒性和低索引成本是核心优势。此外，由于自注意力可形式化为 ANN 操作，ANN 算法的 N 和 d 扩展特性可为高效 Transformer 架构的成本分析提供指导。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [173] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG
