TL;DR: 该书构建了一个统一的、以证明为导向的现代深度学习理论框架，从经典的逼近、优化和泛化理论出发，延伸到过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等当代机制，将广泛的文献组织为连贯的研究叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已发展得过于庞杂，超越了任何单一数学解释所能覆盖的范围。该书旨在将零散的理论文献整合成一个统一的、以证明为导向的叙述体系，为研究者提供一张严谨的理论地图。

Method: 该书采用统一的组织框架：每种理论都通过其控制的对象、使其有效的假设、以及其无法解释的现象三个维度来审视，从而将经典理论（逼近论、优化理论、泛化理论）与当代机制（过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐、涌现）串联为一条连贯的研究路径。

Result: 该书呈现了深度学习理论当前的状态——强大但不完备，且日益聚焦于一个核心问题：学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现出来。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论已从经典基础走向对涌现机制的探索，当前理论虽成果丰硕，但尚不完整，未来的研究重心在于揭示规模、数据、架构与训练如何催生学习机制。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [174] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 IonSense-QKG，一个面向锂离子电池数据集的量子就绪度元数据框架，通过引入量子就绪度评分（QRS）等元数据增强公共数据集记录，帮助研究者筛选适用于混合量子-经典机器学习工作流的数据资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标注质量等方面差异巨大，直接影响其是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流。现有索引缺乏量子相关元数据，研究者难以快速判断数据集的量子适用性。

Method: 基于 EV-Battery-IonSense 索引，为公共电池数据集记录扩充量子相关元数据字段（任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估测量子比特范围、NISQ 可行性等）；设计透明的量子就绪度评分（QRS）作为数据集筛选启发式指标；提供元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接校验工具及 SQL 查询示例。

Result: 框架实现了基于元数据的查询式发现，可识别适用于紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标注异常检测及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG 将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为以数据为中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，使数据集筛选从经验性选择转变为系统性元数据驱动的决策过程。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [175] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 条件推断森林（CIF）在预测基准测试中表现优异（分类第4/17，回归第3/18），运行时间主要受自适应停止和阈值搜索数量影响，Bonferroni校正的蒙特卡洛置换p值可控制节点级错误拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推断树（CIT）和条件推断森林（CIF）通过预测试特征来减少分割选择偏差，但重复的置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算开销大。现有研究缺乏将CIT/CIF作为top-k特征排序方法用于下游预测的系统性评估，同时对其计算瓶颈缺乏理解。

Method: 研究采用三类实验：（1）在22个分类数据集（17种方法）和8个回归数据集（18种方法）上进行下游预测基准测试；（2）控制Bonferroni校正后，通过运行时间消融实验测试自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对拟合时间及预测分数的影响；（3）在稀疏高维仿真中进行特征恢复实验。理论上证明，若节点特征与置换预算不依赖节点响应，Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值在完全置换零假设下可控制节点级拒绝。

Result: CIF在分类任务排名第4/17，回归任务排名第3/18。消融实验表明，关闭自适应停止使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍，使用精确阈值搜索增加1.9-10.8倍，而下游分数变化不超过0.011。稀疏高p仿真显示，森林特征采样可能使信息特征在多次分割决策中被遗漏。

Conclusion: CIF可作为下游预测任务中有效的top-k特征排序方法；自适应停止和阈值搜索数量是主要的计算瓶颈，对其进行调整可大幅降低运行时间且几乎不损失预测性能。但高维稀疏场景下特征采样策略需引起注意。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [176] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的中枢神经系统肿瘤甲基化分类方法，在91类分类任务中达到86%准确率，在甲基化类别家族级别达到93%，相较现有最优方法分别提升约4和5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于DNA甲基化谱的中枢神经系统肿瘤分类方法在跨队列可迁移性、方法论严谨性和多类别鲁棒评估方面仍存在不足，亟需一种方法论上更严格、性能更优的机器学习分类方案。

Method: 提出一种结合稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）进行降维、多项逻辑回归（multinomial logistic regression）进行分类的机器学习方法。在包含2,801例样本的参考队列上进行分层3折交叉验证，并在1,104例独立临床评估队列上进行外部验证，分别在91类细粒度级别和甲基化类别家族级别进行评估。

Result: 在参考队列上分层3折交叉验证平均准确率达96%；在独立临床评估队列上，91类级别准确率为86%，家族级别准确率为93%，相比现有最优方法（82%和88%）分别提升了约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法在方法论上更加严谨，跨评估场景下均一致优于现有最优方法，5个百分点的提升在临床诊断中具有实际意义，可直接影响肿瘤亚型判定、治疗选择及下游临床决策，能够实质性地提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [177] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种三项缩放律，将训练数据显式拆分为训练步数和批量大小，能够正确恢复最优批量大小的缩放行为，且可利用次优批量大小的训练数据进行稳健拟合，显著减少所需训练运行次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放律通常将训练数据视为单一变量，未显式分离训练步数和批量大小的影响，导致无法准确刻画最优批量大小的缩放规律，且需要大量最优配置下的训练运行来拟合。本文旨在构建一个更精细的缩放律，能够同时建模模型规模、训练步数和批量大小三者的关系。

Method: 提出"三项缩放律"（three-term law），将训练数据分解为训练步数（S）和批量大小（B）两个独立项，与模型参数量（N）共同构成三项参数化损失函数。通过在包含次优批量大小的大量训练运行上进行拟合，并利用该函数形式推导最优批量大小及次优批量下的缩放行为。

Result: 所提出的三项律正确恢复了最优批量大小的缩放规律；由于能够利用次优批量大小的训练运行，拟合所需的训练运行数量显著减少；该定律还能推导次优批量大小下的缩放律，并与先前关于临界批量大小的实证发现一致。

Conclusion: 三项缩放律通过对训练步数和批量大小的显式分离，提供了比传统缩放律更精确、更数据高效的建模框架，验证了其在最优批量恢复、数据效率及与已有临界批量理论一致性方面的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [178] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私（LDP）与泛化误差之间的关系并非简单的三元悖论，而是取决于隐私强度：强隐私（高噪声）下，隐私增强反而降低泛化误差；弱隐私（低噪声）下，隐私增强则损害泛化。该非单调行为通过算法稳定性分析得到理论证明和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在分布式学习中建立了拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私与优化误差之间的三元悖论。本文旨在探究该悖论是否普遍适用于泛化误差，抑或泛化误差与鲁棒性、隐私之间的关系具有不同的行为模式。

Method: 作者通过理论推导，针对LDP约束下的拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习，建立算法稳定性的上下界匹配分析，分别在高噪声（强隐私）和低噪声（弱隐私）两种机制下刻画隐私对泛化误差的影响，并辅以实证评估加以验证。

Result: 在高噪声（强隐私）机制下，增强隐私可降低泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私之间无张力；在低噪声（弱隐私）机制下，增强隐私会恶化泛化性能，鲁棒性与隐私之间的张力重新出现。泛化误差因而呈现非单调行为。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性、LDP与泛化误差之间的三元悖论并非普遍成立，而是高度依赖于隐私机制。这一发现修正并细化了先前仅针对优化误差建立的三元悖论框架，对分布式隐私鲁棒学习的设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [179] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 即使当前商业LLM API仅暴露单个token的logit且不支持logit偏置，NightVision攻击仍能通过新颖的"公共集合提示"技术和首token时间测量，恢复模型的隐藏维度、深度和参数规模等架构参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已表明，通过top-k logits或logit偏置函数等受限API访问可以恢复LLM架构细节，导致商业LLM提供商进一步收紧API（仅暴露单个logit，取消logit偏置能力）。本文旨在探究在当今更严格的API限制下，是否仍能恢复LLM的架构参数，从而评估当前API安全措施的充分性。

Method: 提出NightVision攻击方法：1）"公共集合提示"技术——通过构造多个提示，使它们对同一组输出token暴露log概率，再对这些结果进行谱分析以推断隐藏维度；2）利用端到端首token时间（TTFT）测量结合估计的隐藏维度，推断模型深度和参数规模。实验在32个开源LLM上评估，并进行大量消融实验分析token预算和模型属性对精度的影响。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上，隐藏维度恢复的平均相对误差为23%（MoE模型仅9%）；对于参数量超过30亿的模型，深度和参数规模的恢复误差约为53%。消融实验展示了精度如何随token预算和模型属性变化。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制仍不足以完全隐藏底层模型的架构细节，表明需要更强的API防护措施来保护商业LLM的知识产权和架构隐私。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [180] [Role-Aware Neural Convex Divergence Heads for Asymmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01762)
*He Huang,Lu Shen,Yunfeng Huang,Li Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种角色感知的神经凸散度头（role-aware neural convex divergence head），用于非对称表示学习。该头在计算输入凸神经Bregman散度之前，先施加源角色和目标角色投影，在角色投影空间中产生非负的结构化得分，在多个语义和本体基准上一致提升了方向性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多表示学习问题涉及有向关系（如词汇蕴含、句子蕴含、本体层次和引用链接），但标准的欧氏距离、余弦相似度和马氏距离都是对称的，无法建模方向性；而通用神经打分器虽能建模方向性，却缺乏几何结构，难以解释。因此需要一种兼具方向性建模能力和几何结构可解释性的方案。

Method: 提出角色感知神经凸散度头：1）对输入表示分别施加源角色投影和目标角色投影；2）在角色投影空间中计算输入凸神经网络（ICNN）参数化的Bregman散度；3）得到非负的结构化方向性得分。论文进一步分析了该方法的投影空间恒等性、源角色凸性、方向间隙分解以及基于Hessian矩阵的局部曲率特性。

Result: 在词汇、句子、本体和有向图基准上的实验表明：1）在主要语义和本体基准上，角色感知投影相比普通ICNN-Bregman头在十次随机种子下一致提升了方向性准确率，同时保持零负散度率；2）在大规模固定特征的引用预测任务上，专用对称或双曲基线在排序准确率上仍更强，揭示了方法的边界情形。

Conclusion: 该头最适合被理解为一个结构化、可解释的即插即用距离模块，适用于方向性关系重要的任务，但在大规模固定特征场景下并非最优选择。

Abstract: Many representation learning problems involve directed relations, such as lexical entailment, sentence entailment, ontology hierarchy, and citation links. Standard Euclidean, cosine, and Mahalanobis heads are symmetric, while generic neural scorers can model directionality but provide limited geometric structure. This paper proposes a role-aware neural convex divergence head for asymmetric representation learning. The head applies source- and target-role projections before evaluating an input-convex neural Bregman divergence, yielding a nonnegative structured score in the role-projected space. We characterize its projected-space identity, source-role convexity, directional-gap decomposition, and Hessian-based local curvature. Experiments on lexical, sentence, ontology, and directed graph benchmarks compare symmetric distances, unstructured asymmetric scorers, order/hyperbolic baselines, plain ICNN-Bregman heads, and the proposed role-aware variant. Across ten random seeds on the main semantic and ontology benchmarks, role-aware projections consistently improve directional accuracy over plain ICNN-Bregman heads while preserving zero observed negative divergence rate. The results also identify a boundary case: on large fixed-feature citation prediction, specialized symmetric or hyperbolic baselines remain stronger in ranking accuracy. Overall, the proposed head is best understood as a structured and interpretable plug-in distance module for tasks where directional relations matter.

</details>


### [181] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概念验证流程，利用铁路道口图像与结构化事故报告等多模态数据，通过微调的紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）实现对铁路道口安全风险的自动化评估，高风险/低风险分类宏F1达0.757，FRA安全评分估计RMSE为0.078、相关性为0.492。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路道口安全评估传统上依赖人工专家判断和官方统计，耗时且成本高。本研究旨在探索能否利用视觉线索与结构化事故数据，构建一个与专家意见及美国联邦铁路管理局（FRA）评分标准相一致的自动化多模态安全评估AI系统。

Method: 采用路由微调的紧凑型视觉语言模型（routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline）作为核心架构，设计多模态数据处理管线：输入端融合道口图像（单张或多张）与结构化数据（如官方事故报告）；输出端同时支持高风险/低风险二分类和FRA安全评分的回归估计。

Result: 高风险/低风险分类宏F1分数为0.757；FRA安全评分估计的RMSE为0.078，相关性系数为0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 该概念验证系统证明了融合视觉与结构化多模态数据的VLM管线可有效用于铁路道口安全自动化评估，为构建符合监管标准（FRA）的AI辅助决策系统奠定了可行基础。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [182] [Regularized Variational and Spectral Log-Density-Ratio Estimation in the Gaussian Location Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01895)
*Francis Bach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在高斯位置模型中研究岭正则化对数密度比估计，比较变分估计量与谱估计量的渐近风险。结果表明：样本量大时变分估计量更优，样本量小时谱估计量因方差更低而占优；同时探讨了核范数惩罚用于特征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维设定下，对数密度比估计是分类、两样本检验等任务的核心。现有方法（变分类与谱方法）在不同样本量下的相对表现缺乏理论比较，作者旨在填补这一空白，为实践中的方法选择提供理论指导。

Method: 在具有共同协方差的高斯位置模型（q~N(0,I), p~N(Δ,I)）框架下：1) 变分估计量通过KL散度拟合加L2平方惩罚；2) 谱估计量将单一变分问题替换为一族岭正则化最小二乘问题。利用凸高斯极小极大定理(CGMT)推导变分极限的标量熵最小化表征，利用加权样本协方差矩阵预解式的确定性等价推导谱极限。在观测数与维数同阶发散的渐近体系下比较两者的总体风险。

Result: 推导了两类估计量的高维确定性渐近等价公式。在固定信号的长宽比扫描和正则化参数优化下：观测较多时，正确设定的变分估计量风险更小；观测较少时，谱估计量因其基于协方差的构造具有更低的方差而占优。还对核范数惩罚进行了部分分析，展示其在特征学习中的潜力。

Conclusion: 在本文分析的准则和渐近体系下，变分估计量与谱估计量之间存在明确的样本量权衡：大样本偏向变分方法，小样本偏向谱方法。该结论为高维对数密度比估计的方法选择提供了清晰的理论依据，核范数惩罚的引入拓展了特征学习的可能性。

Abstract: We study ridge-regularized log-density-ratio estimation in the Gaussian location model with a common covariance matrix. By affine invariance, the model is written as q $\sim$ N(0, I), p $\sim$ N($Δ$, I), with linear features, where $Δ$ is a mean vector. The variational estimator is the empirical Kullback-Leibler (KL) log-normalized fit with a squared L2-penalty on its nonconstant coefficient, and the spectral estimator recently introduced in [1] replaces a single variational problem by a continuum of ridge-regularized least-squares problems. We derive high-dimensional deterministic asymptotic equivalents when the numbers of observations and dimension tend to infinity with fixed ratios. The regularized variational limit is characterized by a scalar entropy minimization problem derived from the convex-Gaussian-min-max theorem (CGMT), while the regularized spectral limit follows from deterministic equivalents for resolvents of weighted sums of two independent Gaussian sample covariance matrices. We use these formulas to compare population risks, with experiments focused on fixed-signal aspect-ratio sweeps and optimized regularization. Our conclusion is that with many observations, under the criteria and asymptotic regimes analyzed here, the well-specified variational estimator has the smaller risk, while with fewer observations, the spectral estimator is favored because its covariance-based construction has lower variance. We also study how a nuclear penalty can be used and partially analyzed to perform feature learning.

</details>


### [183] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer模型中离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略，在合成算术任务和真实临床预测任务上评估其表现。研究发现显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上最优，而混合token化方法（保留数值但先分箱再投影）提供了更稳健、广泛适用的替代方案，其最优分箱数遵循数据集规模的幂律。模型普遍表现出"够用即可"的数值计算而非精确算术，临床收益因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于Transformer的序列处理（尤其是电子健康记录数据）中，如何编码数值一直缺乏系统性研究。数值编码策略直接影响模型的数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性，但现有方法间的权衡尚不明确，缺乏实用的选择指导。

Method: 作者在合成算术任务（嵌入真实EHR数据中）和真实临床预测任务上，系统对比了三类数值编码策略：（1）离散编码（将数值完全token化）；（2）连续编码（直接使用原始数值）；（3）混合编码（先分箱再投影，保留数值信息）。通过控制变量实验，评估各策略在精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性上的表现，并分析最优分箱数与数据集规模的关系。

Result: （1）显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感的算术任务上表现最佳，但受架构限制；（2）混合token化方法（先分箱再投影）更为稳健且适用范围更广；（3）最优分箱数与数据集规模呈幂律关系；（4）模型普遍呈现"够用即可"的数值计算行为，而非精确算术；（5）引入实验室数值带来的临床收益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 在实际应用中，稳健性和可部署性往往比最大化数值精度更重要，因此混合token化方法应作为实用的默认选择。数值编码策略的选择需综合考虑任务需求、数据规模和部署约束。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [184] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge 是一个面向神经退行性疾病诊断的临床引导多任务 MRI 框架，它结合大规模自监督预训练与海马分割、萎缩分类及重建目标，再通过门控融合微调。在 ADNI 和 OASIS 队列上，该框架在 AD vs 认知正常分类中分别达到 88.17% 和 82.78% 的准确率，并在 MCI 相关和混合诊断场景中提升最大，同时展现出良好的跨队列泛化能力和基于概率的机会性筛查可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症的结构性 MRI 改变往往细微且异质性强，传统单任务方法难以充分捕捉这些差异，因此需要一个更鲁棒、可扩展的框架来提升诊断精度。

Method: 提出 NeuroBridge 框架，核心包括：(1) 大规模自监督 MRI 预训练；(2) 多任务学习，联合海马分割、海马萎缩分类与图像重建三个辅助目标；(3) 门控融合（gated fusion）机制进行下游诊断任务的微调。评估涵盖 ADNI 和 OASIS 两个独立队列，设计交叉队列迁移、预测概率-准确率关联分析以及基于概率的机会性筛查实验。

Result: 在 AD vs 认知正常分类中，ADNI 准确率达 88.17%，OASIS 达 82.78%；最大性能增益出现在 MCI 相关和混合诊断场景；框架在跨队列迁移中表现稳健；预测概率与分类准确率之间呈系统性正相关；基于概率的机会性筛查策略被验证为可行。

Conclusion: 临床先验引导的多任务表征学习优于传统单任务方法，NeuroBridge 为痴呆症的 MRI 评估和机会性筛查提供了鲁棒且可扩展的技术框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [185] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP 提出一种基于自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH）的广义 Gaunt 张量积方法，在保持 O(L^5) 效率的同时，恢复了 GTP 缺失的反对称路径，实现了与完整 CGTP 相当的表达力，尤其在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)-等变网络中 Clebsch-Gordan 张量积（CGTP）的 O(L^6) 复杂度限制了其可扩展性；Gaunt 张量积（GTP）虽降低了复杂度，但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达力不完整。因此需要一种既能保持渐进效率、又能恢复完整反对称交互的方法。

Method: 将 Gaunt 张量积从标量球谐函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH），利用 SWSH 的代数性质恢复 GTP 中缺失的反对称相互作用，同时自然包含张量积的奇宇称分量，构建更富表达力的等变基。

Result: 在 Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF 和 OC20 等多个基准测试上，SpinGTP 达到与完整 CGTP 相当的精度；在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: SpinGTP 为大规模三维原子系统模拟中高阶等变性提供了一条完备、可扩展且数学上严谨的路径。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [186] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种代码执行基础设施（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）在coding-agent强化学习中的开销差异，发现冷启动延迟差异高达110倍，完成百万条轨迹所需的工作时间差距达1.8倍，建议将执行基础设施纳入RL训练系统的优化范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有coding-agent强化学习将执行基础设施视为无关的背景实现细节，忽视了基础设施开销对大规模RL后训练效率的显著影响——每次rollout的小开销在百万级规模下会急剧放大，这是一个被错失的优化机会。

Method: 对比实验法：在四种执行基底（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）上，测量冷启动延迟和完成一百万条150步轨迹的预计工作小时数，量化不同基础设施方案的效率差异。

Result: 四种执行基底之间的冷启动延迟差异高达110倍，完成一百万条150步轨迹的预计工作小时数差距达1.8倍。

Conclusion: 未来的coding-agent RL系统应将执行基底的优化视为训练系统本身的核心组成部分，而非仅作为部署层面的附属管道。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [187] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了领域特定的专家剪枝对MoE模型效用与事实可靠性的影响，发现在生物医学领域适度剪枝可保持效用但极端剪枝增加幻觉风险，跨领域时则效用与可靠性均快速下降，强调高风险部署中可靠性评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE专家剪枝研究主要关注基准效用，忽视了剪枝对事实可靠性的影响，尤其在高风险的生物医学等垂直领域，这一空白亟需填补。

Method: 在生物医学（域内）和通用（跨域）场景下，系统评估4种MoE模型、6种剪枝方法在多种剪枝比例下的生成与分类任务表现，同时测量效用指标和幻觉/可靠性指标。

Result: 适度剪枝可维持域内效用且可靠性未立即下降，但极端剪枝比例下幻觉风险显著增加；切换到通用领域后，效用和可靠性均快速恶化。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖任务与领域，仅凭效用指标评估剪枝后MoE模型不足以应对高风险部署，必须纳入可靠性评估。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [188] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知的混合神经架构GRS-KAN，将Rvachev的R函数集成到KAN框架中，通过可微R函数解析编码几何/逻辑约束，在回归任务中实现了最高67%的测试RMSE降低，同时提升了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN虽然擅长学习平滑非线性结构，但难以高效表示几何约束、不连续性和隐式边界。作者希望将已知的几何先验（如可行域、不连续面）以解析形式直接嵌入神经网络，从而结合数据驱动的平滑学习与基于规则的几何编码两种互补建模机制。

Method: 提出GRS-KAN架构，核心是将Rvachev的R函数（R-conjunction和R-disjunction）作为可微逻辑算子集成到KAN框架中。R函数能够解析地表示复杂几何支撑集，并通过可微操作直接纳入回归模型。设计了加法、乘法和不可知分支加权三种变体，其中不可知变体可自动判断几何先验是否有益。

Result: 在具有圆形和矩形支撑集的不连续回归问题上进行实验验证：几何感知GRS-KAN较标准KAN在测试RMSE上最多降低67%，边界定位精度显著提升，同时模型的可解释性因几何结构的显式解析表示而得到改善。

Conclusion: GRS-KAN成功将解析几何编码与KAN的平滑非线性学习相融合，不仅大幅提升了预测精度和边界定位能力，还保持了模型的可解释性。不可知变体进一步赋予了自动评估几何先验效用能力，为该方向提供了灵活实用的框架。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [189] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ember 是一种针对语言模型中嵌入表和LM-head矩阵的轻量优化器，仅需 O(V+D) 显存（远低于 Adam 的 O(2VD)），利用嵌入层独特的梯度几何特性，在监督微调、RL和预训练中提升 Pareto 前沿，并揭示了 token 优化轨迹可用简单的一维射线描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的嵌入表和 LM-head 作为连续程序与离散符号之间的读写接口，其梯度几何结构与密集隐藏层权重存在本质差异。现有优化器（如 Adam）为所有参数维护一阶和二阶矩估计，导致嵌入层优化状态占用 O(2VD) 的巨大显存开销，需要在多 GPU 间分片嵌入表。本文旨在利用这一梯度几何差异，设计一种仅需 O(V+D) 显存的轻量优化器，消除对 token 表优化器状态分片的需求。

Method: 提出 Ember 优化器，专门针对嵌入表和 LM-head 矩阵设计。核心思想是利用这些接口层独特的梯度几何特性，大幅减少所需维护的优化器状态量——从 Adam 的 O(2VD) 降至 O(V+D)，仅需千字节级别的优化器状态。Ember 可直接融入现有的 ZeRO/FSDP 分布式训练框架，无需对 token 表优化器状态进行分片。

Result: 实验表明，Ember 在监督微调、强化学习和预训练三种场景下均能有效扩展，跨不同 batch size 和参数规模均可提升 Pareto 前沿。此外，研究发现 token 的优化轨迹可以用简单的一维射线良好描述，这与"神经网络参数在高度非凸空间中导航"的流行观点相悖。

Conclusion: Transformer 训练所需的优化器空间出人意料地狭窄；嵌入层与 LM-head 的梯度几何特性可被利用来设计极轻量的专用优化器。Ember 以极低的显存代价实现了与 Adam 相当甚至更优的性能，为大规模语言模型训练提供了更高效的优化方案，并已开源以支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [190] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCGNM 是一种联邦学习客户端优化器，通过按类分组并对每组动量进行归一化来均衡多数类和少数类的梯度幅值；配合基于XAB的FedHOO算法优化重采样率，在长尾分布数据上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中类别不平衡问题严重，少数类预测性能差，但传统的集中式重采样/重加权技术因隐私约束和数据异构性无法直接应用。现有联邦方法对稀有类梯度中的噪声处理不足，且缺乏对小规模客户端场景下重采样率的高效优化。

Method: 提出FedCGNM：将类别按最小组内方差划分为少量组，每组维护一个动量并归一化到单位长度，以各组归一化动量的求和作为更新方向，以此均衡梯度幅值并抑制稀有类噪声。理论分析中显式建模了时变重采样率下的收敛性。进一步提出FedHOO：利用联邦并行性的X-armed-bandit算法，以线性代价在每客户端评估两个候选重采样率组合，高效优化小规模联邦中的重采样率。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准数据集和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM一致优于基线方法；在小规模联邦中，FedHOO进一步带来性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过类分组归一化动量机制可有效解决联邦长尾学习中的梯度不平衡与噪声问题，结合XAB的重采样率优化在小客户端场景下尤为有效。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [191] [The risk of KV cache compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01520)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度研究了KV缓存压缩的最小最大风险(minimax risk)，揭示了压缩精度与缓存内在可压缩性之间的关系，并据此提出了具有理论保证的实用KV缓存压缩算法设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前KV缓存压缩方法主要依赖经验实验驱动，缺乏系统性理论指导。现有理论结果仅表明最坏情况下压缩不可能，但未提供在可压缩场景下如何设计算法的指导。本文旨在填补这一理论与实践的空白。

Method: 作者通过刻画KV缓存压缩的minimax风险，将其表述为缓存内在可压缩性的函数，揭示了何时以及如何实现精确压缩。基于此推导出因果掩码(causal masking)下KV缓存压缩的新设计原则，并映射到prefill和自回归解码阶段，最终实例化为一个实用算法。

Result: 所提出的算法在LongBench基准测试上取得了有竞争力的表现，验证了理论指导实践的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为实用KV缓存压缩提供了一条有原则的路径，并具备理论保证，标志着该领域从纯经验驱动走向理论与实验相结合的范式转变。

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [192] [SINA: A Fully Automated Circuit Schematic Image to Netlist Generator Using Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01609)
*Saoud Aldowaish,Yashwanth Karumanchi,Kai-Chen Chiang,Mohammed Ayman Habib,Finn Murphy,Rishen Cao,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SINA 是一个开源的全自动电路原理图图像到网表转换工具，结合深度学习组件检测、连通分量标记、OCR 和 VLM，统一处理 IC 和 PCB 级原理图，能区分交叉线与连接点，整体网表生成准确率达 96.67%，比现有方法提升 2.72 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路原理图图像转换方法缺乏对 IC 级和 PCB 级原理图的泛化能力，在元件识别和连接关系推断方面存在困难，且无法区分导线交叉点与连接点。而大量已验证的电路设计以图像形式存在于文献和教科书中，无法被 EDA 工具直接处理，限制了 AI 在模拟和混合信号领域的应用。

Method: 提出 SINA 全自动流水线：(1) 深度学习模型进行鲁棒的元件检测；(2) 连通分量标记实现精确的连接关系推断；(3) OCR 提取元件参考标识符；(4) 视觉语言模型 (VLM) 进行可靠的参考标识符分配；(5) 专用的交叉线检测模块区分导线交叉与连接；(6) 使用图同构技术验证生成网表的正确性。

Result: 实验表明 SINA 整体网表生成准确率达到 96.67%，相比现有最先进方法提升 2.72 倍，同时统一支持 IC 级和 PCB 级原理图，并能有效处理交叉线检测问题。

Conclusion: SINA 作为首个开源的全自动原理图图像到网表生成工具，通过多模块协同实现了高精度、高泛化能力的网表转换，为构建面向 AI 的电路设计知识库奠定了基础，有望推动模拟和混合信号 EDA 领域的 AI 应用发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have revolutionized Electronic Design Automation (EDA), particularly through Large Language Models (LLMs) for circuit design tasks. However, their application to analog and mixed-signal domains remains limited by the lack of machine-readable representations of existing circuit design knowledge. Circuit schematic images found in research manuscripts, textbooks, and websites constitute a vast repository of validated designs; however, these visual representations cannot be directly processed by EDA tools. Converting them into machine-readable netlists is essential for enabling simulation, verification, and building comprehensive databases for AI-based models. Current conversion methods lack generalization across both Integrated Circuit (IC) and Printed Circuit Board (PCB) level schematics. Moreover, they struggle with component recognition and connectivity inference, and fail to distinguish between connected junctions and crossing wires. In this paper, we propose SINA, an open-source circuit schematic image-to-netlist generator. SINA is a fully automated pipeline that integrates deep learning for robust component detection, connected-component labeling for accurate connectivity inference, Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for component reference designator extraction, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reliable reference designator assignment. SINA handles both IC- and PCB-level schematics and incorporates dedicated crossing-wires detection to differentiate wire intersections from connections. We validate the correctness of the generated netlists using graph isomorphism techniques. Our experiments demonstrate an overall netlist generation accuracy of 96.67%, which is 2.72x higher compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [193] [MKGR: Multimodal Knowledge-Graph Representation Learning for Cold-Start Protein-Protein Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01627)
*Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向冷启动蛋白质互作（PPI）预测的多模态表示框架（\method），融合了区域感知的蛋白质序列编码与四种蛋白质中心生物医学知识图谱，通过桥接重构正则化和对偶门控模块，在冷启动场景下显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PPI预测方法依赖网络拓扑，当候选蛋白在训练阶段无任何PPI边时（冷启动场景），模型往往缺乏有效上下文而失效。因此，需要一种能够利用序列信息和多源生物医学知识图谱的框架来应对冷启动PPI预测挑战。

Method: \method框架包含三大核心组件：（1）区域感知的蛋白质序列编码器，从结构信息感知的序列区域中提取上下文表示；（2）四种蛋白质中心生物医学知识图谱（蛋白-药物、蛋白-疾病、蛋白-miRNA、蛋白-lncRNA），通过图注意力编码器学习模态特异的蛋白质嵌入；（3）桥接重构目标函数，通过恢复共享的蛋白-实体关联来正则化图学习；同时采用对偶级别的门控模块，为每对候选蛋白自适应融合序列证据与图谱证据。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上，针对novel-old和novel-novel两种冷启动设置，\method在ACC、F1、AUC、AUPR和MCC五项指标上一致地超越序列、网络及知识图谱等竞争基线方法。

Conclusion: \method通过有效整合序列信息与多模态生物医学知识，成功解决了冷启动PPI预测中的上下文缺失问题，展现出优异的预测性能和鲁棒性，为功能基因组学和药物开发中的蛋白互作发现提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Accurate protein-protein interaction (PPI) prediction is central to functional genomics, disease mechanism discovery, and drug development. A difficult setting arises when candidate interactions include proteins that have no observed PPI edges during training, where models relying on network topology alone often lose useful context. This paper presents \method, a multimodal representation framework for cold-start PPI prediction. \method\ combines region-aware protein sequence encoding with four protein-centered biomedical knowledge graphs, including protein-drug, protein-disease, protein-miRNA, and protein-lncRNA associations. The sequence branch extracts contextual representations from structurally informed sequence regions, while graph attention encoders learn modality-specific protein embeddings from sparse biomedical associations. A bridge reconstruction objective regularizes graph learning by recovering shared protein-entity associations, and a pair-level gating module adaptively integrates sequence and graph evidence for each candidate protein pair. Experiments on two benchmark datasets under novel-old and novel-novel cold-start settings show that \method\ consistently outperforms competitive sequence, network, and knowledge-graph baselines across ACC, F1, AUC, AUPR, and MCC.

</details>


### [194] [DeadPool: Resilient LLM Training with Hot-Swapping via Zero-Overhead Checkpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01646)
*Haotian Xie,Junlin Chen,Mingkai Zheng,Lishan Yang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeadPool 提出了一种面向大语言模型训练的容错机制，通过"热插拔"技术（利用空闲节点替换故障节点）实现零开销的无错执行和极低恢复延迟。核心思想包括：关键路径外的内存检查点机制和运行时通信器重构协议。在512块A100 GPU、65B参数规模下，恢复时间小于40秒，且无检查点开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练的容错机制存在两难困境：要么在无故障运行时引入不可忽视的额外开销，要么在恢复时经历较长的延迟——尤其是在部分计算节点发生永久性故障的场景下。这种"无故障开销"与"恢复延迟"之间形成帕累托前沿。DeadPool旨在同时优化这两个目标。

Method: DeadPool 包含两大核心技术：(1) 关键路径外的内存检查点机制（off-critical-path in-memory checkpointing），利用空间冗余在计算的同时异步完成检查点存储，实现零开销；(2) 运行时通信器重构协议（communicator reconstruction protocol），在节点永久故障时将故障节点替换为备用节点，无需终止整个训练作业，实现"热插拔"恢复。恢复时利用内存检查点进行最小量重计算以重建内存状态。

Result: 在最多512块NVIDIA A100 GPU和最多65B参数规模的LLM训练场景下评估，DeadPool实现了零检查点开销（无错执行期间），热插拔恢复在40秒内完成。

Conclusion: DeadPool 成功同时实现了零开销的无错执行和极低恢复成本，打破了现有容错机制在开销与恢复延迟之间的帕累托困境，为大模型训练提供了高效的容错解决方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art large language model (LLM) training takes tens of thousands of graphics processing units (GPUs) for months and encounters failures across the software and hardware stack. Existing fault-tolerance mechanisms either impose non-trivial overhead during failure-free execution or suffer from prolonged recovery latency, particularly under scenarios where a small subset of compute nodes experience permanent failures. %The tradeoff between failure-free overhead and recovery latency forms a space forms a Pareto frontier We present DeadPool to simultaneously address both optimization objectives. DeadPool incorporates a fault-tolerance mechanism that restores LLM training via hot-swapping, namely by replacing failed nodes with spare nodes without terminating the complete job. The hot-swapping of DeadPool is enabled by two ideas: First, it exploits an off-critical-path in-memory checkpointing mechanism for spatial redundancy. Second, it introduces a communicator reconstruction protocol that replaces failed nodes with spare nodes at runtime. DeadPool efficiently overlaps the in-memory checkpointing with computation, thus introducing zero overhead during error-free execution. Upon permanent node failures, DeadPool can rebuild memory states with minimal recomputation by leveraging in-memory checkpoints. We evaluate DeadPool across scales (up to 512 NVIDIA A100 GPUs) and LLMs (up to 65B parameters), and observe zero checkpoint overhead with hot-swapping recovery completing in under 40 seconds. These results show that DeadPool simultaneously achieves both zero-overhead error-free execution and extremely low recovery cost.

</details>


### [195] [CALM: Interpretable Cross-Modal Alignment for Biomarker Discovery from Unpaired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01656)
*Jueqi Wang,Zachary Jacokes,John Darrell Van Horn,Kevin A. Pelphrey,Michael C. Schatz,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM是一个框架，能在完全不相交的人群中学习脑区（ROI）与遗传通路之间的可解释关联。它通过线性投影将影像和遗传两种模态对齐到共享潜在空间，同时匹配类别条件分布并确保组别可分。在自闭症谱系障碍上，CALM揭示了免疫和代谢通路与特定皮层区域的关联，且与已有文献一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模数据集多为单模态（仅有神经影像或仅有遗传数据），缺乏同时包含两种模态的配对数据集，限制了脑结构与遗传因素交互作用的研究。需要一种方法能从不相交的群体中学习跨模态关联。

Method: CALM框架通过线性投影将脑ROI和遗传通路映射到共享潜在空间，同时优化两个目标：(1) 匹配两类模态的类别条件潜在分布；(2) 确保不同类别在潜在空间中的可分性。线性投影权重直接提供可解释的通路-ROI关联。训练仅使用单模态影像和遗传数据集，可在未见过的配对数据集上泛化。

Result: CALM在未见配对数据集上的泛化性能优于多种最先进方法和消融基线。学习到的关联具有稳定性（与配对基线对比验证）。在自闭症谱系障碍实验中，揭示了免疫和代谢通路与特定皮层区域的关联，与已有文献一致。

Conclusion: CALM为利用大规模单模态数据库研究脑疾病中跨模态交互开辟了新途径，能够在完全不相交的群体中有效发现脑区与遗传通路之间的可解释关联。

Abstract: The interaction between brain structure and genetic influences is key to understanding neuropsychiatric disorders. However, most large-scale datasets are unimodal, providing either neuroimaging or genetics data. We propose CALM, a framework that learns interpretable associations between brain ROIs and genetic pathways from completely disjoint populations. CALM aligns the two modalities in a shared latent space via linear projections that simultaneously match the class-conditional latent distributions and ensure group separability. These projections provide interpretable pathway--ROI associations. When trained on unimodal imaging and genetics datasets, CALM generalizes to an unseen paired dataset, outperforming several state-of-the-art methods and ablation baselines. We also demonstrate stability of the learned associations against a paired baseline. Our experiments on autism spectrum disorder reveal immune and metabolic pathways linked to specific cortical regions and are consistent with established literature. Thus, CALM opens the door to leveraging large unimodal repositories for studying cross-modal interactions in brain disorders across disparate datasets.

</details>


### [196] [Message Passing Based Two-Timescale Bayesian Learning for Joint Channel and Memory Hardware Impairments Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01660)
*Wei Xu,An Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于消息传递的双时间尺度贝叶斯深度学习框架(MP-TTBDL)，利用残差循环门控单元(RGRU)对硬件损伤的时隙内记忆效应进行建模，并通过Turbo-OAMP与DAMP模块的期望传播(EP)迭代交换外信息，实现大规模MIMO接收机中信道与硬件损伤的联合跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO接收机中的硬件损伤（如非线性失真、I/Q不平衡等）会引入符号间记忆和阵元间耦合，严重恶化信道估计精度。传统方法通常将损伤补偿与信道估计分离处理，难以应对损伤随时间缓慢漂移、信道快速变化的双时间尺度特性。

Method: 首先，采用残差循环门控单元(RGRU)对硬件损伤的时隙内记忆进行建模。其次，建立双时间尺度先验：对稀疏信道施加快变马尔可夫先验，对网络参数施加慢变高斯马尔可夫先验。然后，构建多时隙因子图并设计消息传递算法：时隙间消息具有闭式解；时隙内因子图被划分为信道跟踪模块（基于Turbo-OAMP进行稀疏信道估计）和损伤校准模块（基于专门设计的DAMP过程更新损伤参数），两模块通过期望传播(EP)迭代交换外信息直至收敛。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在不同在线损伤场景和SNR条件下，所提框架相比传统"先补偿后信道估计"方案，均能稳健地实现更低的信道估计误差。

Conclusion: MP-TTBDL框架通过双时间尺度先验建模和消息传递机制，有效解耦并联合优化了信道跟踪与硬件损伤校准，为大规模MIMO接收机在硬件非理想条件下的鲁棒信道估计提供了新方案。

Abstract: Hardware impairments in massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) receivers introduce inter-symbol memory and inter-element coupling, severely degrading channel estimation. This paper employs a residual recurrent gated unit (RGRU) to model the intra-slot memory of the hardware impairments and proposes a message-passing-based two-timescale Bayesian deep learning (MP-TTBDL) framework for joint channel and impairment tracking. Owing to small-scale fading, the wireless channel varies rapidly across slots, whereas hardware impairments drift slowly due to hardware aging and environmental variations. To capture these distinct physical timescales, a fastvarying Markov prior and a slow-varying Gaussian Markov prior are assigned to the sparse channel and the network parameters, respectively. Based on a multi-slot factor graph formulation, a message-passing algorithm is developed. Specifically, the inter-slot messages admit closed-form updates, while the intra-slot factor graph, due to its complex recurrent structure, is partitioned into a channel tracking module and an impairments calibration module. The channel tracking module performs sparse channel estimation via turbo orthogonal approximate message passing (Turbo-OAMP), and the impairments calibration module updates the impairment parameters via a specially designed deep approximate message passing (DAMP) procedure, with the two modules iteratively exchanging extrinsic information through expectation propagation (EP) until convergence. Simulation results show that the proposed framework robustly achieves lower channel estimation error than conventional compensators followed by channel estimation across different online impairment scenarios and signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions.

</details>


### [197] [Revisiting Decentralized Online Convex Optimization with Compressed Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01665)
*Hao Zhou,Xiaoyu Wang,Chang Yao,Mingli Song,Yuanyu Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将FTRL类型的算法引入带有通信压缩的分布式在线凸优化（D-OCO）框架，提出了两种算法：一种适用于全信息设置，另一种适用于Bandit设置。相较于已有的OGD类型算法，FTRL的对偶更新机制使其能更自然地结合通信压缩的平均共识技术，在算法设计和理论分析上更为优雅，且在Bandit设置下显著改善了遗憾界和通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式在线凸优化（D-OCO）面临通信瓶颈问题，已有工作研究了带压缩通信的D-OCO，但仅限于OGD类型的算法。而在精确通信的D-OCO中，最优算法均为FTRL类型。因此，作者希望填补这一空白：设计FTRL类型的算法来解决带压缩通信的D-OCO问题，以期获得更好的算法结构和理论性能。

Method: 核心洞察在于FTRL的对偶更新机制可以自然地与通信压缩的平均共识技术相结合。基于此提出两种算法：1）全信息设置下的FTRL型压缩通信算法，可匹配现有遗憾界；2）Bandit设置下的FTRL型压缩通信算法，显著改进了遗憾界和通信成本。

Result: 第一种算法在全信息设置下可匹配现有遗憾界；第二种算法在Bandit设置下，相比已有算法，在遗憾界和通信成本两方面均实现了显著改进。

Conclusion: FTRL类型的算法凭借其双更新机制，比OGD类型算法更适合应用于带通信压缩的D-OCO场景，不仅在算法设计上更简洁优雅，在理论保证上也更具优势，尤其是在Bandit反馈场景下。

Abstract: Decentralized online convex optimization (D-OCO) is a popular framework for distributed applications with streaming data. To tackle the communication bottleneck, previous studies have investigated D-OCO with compressed communication and proposed several algorithms that are variants of online gradient descent (OGD). However, for D-OCO with exact communication, the best existing algorithms are variants of follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL). In this paper, for the first time, we propose two FTRL-type algorithms for D-OCO with compressed communication. Compared with OGD-type algorithms, our algorithms are more elegant in both algorithmic design and theoretical analysis. The key insight is that the dual update mechanism of FTRL allows us to make a simple application of the technique for average consensus with communication compression. More specifically, our first algorithm considers the full-information setting, and can match the existing regret bounds. Our second algorithm is designed for the bandit setting, and can significantly improve both the regret bounds and communication costs of existing algorithms.

</details>


### [198] [UniWind: Toward Unified Day-Ahead Wind Power Forecasting via Physics-Informed State Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01670)
*Ronghui Xu,Tongxin Wu,Guozhen Zhang,Yihan Li,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniWind 是一种基于物理信息状态路由的风电功率预测模型，通过物理先验估计器构建站点校准的物理先验，并结合潜在状态编码器与状态感知功率校正器，将物理先验转换为最终功率预测，在多个数据集上表现出优异的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理模型跨风电场适应性差，而数据驱动模型容易混淆气象效应与运行状态（如停机、限电）导致的功率偏差。实际观测功率往往混杂了物理可用功率、局地环境影响和潜在运行状态，需要一种能解耦这些因素的方法。

Method: UniWind 包含三个核心组件：(1) Physical Prior Estimator：结合站点条件的单调扭曲与共享物理功率曲线，构建站点校准的物理先验，并施加物理上界约束，形成可用风功率的软包络；(2) Latent State Encoder：建模运行状态嵌入；(3) State-aware Power Corrector：通过知识引导的有监督状态路由和有界的状态特定专家校正，将物理先验转化为最终功率预测。

Result: 在超过20个真实世界数据集上的全量训练（full-shot）和跨风电场零样本（cross-farm zero-shot）实验中，UniWind 均展现出优异的预测精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UniWind 通过物理先验与数据驱动的状态路由相结合，有效解耦了气象效应与运行状态对风电功率的影响，在不同风电场之间具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Day-ahead wind power forecasting is essential for cost-effective power-system operation. It is primarily driven by future meteorological conditions while retaining temporal dependencies in power generation. In practice, observed wind-farm power often entangles physically available power with local environmental effects and latent operational states, such as shutdowns and curtailment. Existing physical models provide useful constraints but adapt poorly across wind farms, whereas data-driven models can capture rich correlations but often conflate meteorological effects with state-induced deviations. In this study, we propose UniWind, a wind power forecasting model based on physics-informed state routing. UniWind first employs a Physical Prior Estimator to construct a site-calibrated physical prior by combining site-conditioned monotonic warping with a shared physical power curve. It further applies a physical upper-bound constraint to shape this prior as a soft envelope of available wind power generation. UniWind then proposes a Latent State Encoder to model operating-state embeddings and transforms the physical prior into final power forecasts through a State-aware Power Corrector, which uses knowledge-guided supervised state routing and bounded, state-specific expert correction. Full-shot and cross-farm zero-shot experiments on more than 20 real-world datasets demonstrate the accuracy and robustness of UniWind.

</details>


### [199] [SCAPE: Accurate and Efficient LLM Training with Extreme Sparse Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01678)
*Mingkai Zheng,Junlin Chen,Haotian Xie,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCAPE 是一种通信高效的分布式优化器，利用 AdamS 第一矩的稳定性，基于第一矩统计量而非原始梯度构建稀疏掩码，实现高达 99% 的梯度稀疏化，同时通过掩码分区生成、一步延迟同步与计算重叠等机制，在保持模型质量的前提下将 Llama-500M 端到端预训练时间最多缩短 43.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 预训练中通信开销随模型规模和系统扩展急剧增长，现有方法中，原始梯度稀疏化在 Adam 类优化器下高稀疏度时不稳定，而量化通信受位宽限制且引入额外运行时开销，亟需一种既能实现高稀疏度又保持训练稳定性的通信压缩方案。

Method: SCAPE 的核心设计包括：(1) 利用 AdamS 第一矩（动量）的时序稳定性，从第一矩统计量中导出稀疏掩码，而非从噪声较大的原始梯度构建；(2) 将掩码生成分区到各工作节点，与优化器状态分片对齐，避免全局通信；(3) 延迟掩码使用一步，使掩码同步可与前向/反向计算重叠；(4) 从单一同步稀疏缓冲区重建第二矩更新所需量，省去额外的集合通信操作。实现基于 Megatron-LM，在 32 块 GH200 GPU 上验证。

Result: 在 GPT-345M (OpenWebText) 和 Llama-500M (SlimPajama-6B) 上，SCAPE 在 90% 和 99% 稀疏度下均保持训练稳定性、验证损失和下游任务精度。Llama-500M 端到端预训练时钟时间最多减少 43.3%，模型质量与稠密 AdamW/AdamS 相当。Llama-1.8B 每步加速比达 3.26 倍（对比稠密 AdamS）。

Conclusion: SCAPE 证明了基于第一矩的梯度稀疏化可以在极高稀疏度（99%）下保持 LLM 预训练质量，同时显著降低通信开销，为大规模分布式 LLM 训练提供了一种实用且高效的通信压缩范式。

Abstract: Communication increasingly dominates the cost of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training, especially under data-parallel and sharded training schemes, where gradient synchronization and parameter reconstruction overhead increase with model size and system scale. Existing communication-reduction methods either sparsify raw gradients, which can be unstable for modern Adam-style optimizers at high sparsity, or quantize communication, whose savings are fundamentally bounded by bit width and often incur additional runtime overhead. We present SCAPE, a communication-efficient distributed optimizer for LLM training that exploits the stability of AdamS's first-moment to enable aggressive sparsification without loss of LLM quality. Instead of constructing masks from raw gradients, SCAPE derives them from first-moment-based statistics, partitions mask generation across workers to align with optimizer sharding, and delays mask usage by one step so that mask synchronization can overlap with computation. SCAPE also reconstructs the quantities required for second-moment updates from a single synchronized sparse buffer, avoiding an additional collective. We implement SCAPE in Megatron-LM and evaluate its convergence by pre-training GPT-345M on OpenWebText and Llama-500M on SlimPajama-6B using 32 NVIDIA GH200 GPUs on TACC Vista. In both models, SCAPE preserves training stability, validation loss, and downstream task accuracy under 90\% and 99\% sparsity. For Llama-500M, SCAPE reduces end-to-end pre-training wall-clock time by up to 43.3\% while maintaining model quality comparable to dense AdamW and AdamS. For Llama-1.8B, SCAPE achieves up to 3.26$\times$ speedup per step compared to dense AdamS.

</details>


### [200] [WARP: Weight-Space Analysis for Recovering Training Data Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01686)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Aditya Goyal,John Cooper,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARP 是一种通过模型权重恢复微调模型训练数据中领域混合比例的新框架，其核心思想是利用模型融合生成伪检查点来模拟训练轨迹，并从权重空间的几何足迹中推断领域组成，在 BERT 和 GPT-2 上分别实现了 0.046 和 0.104 的平均 MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的训练数据配方（如领域混合权重）通常不公开，造成访问不对称：研究者只能研究模型本身，却无法了解产生模型的训练分布。现有方法（如成员推断）仅能在样本级别检测，无法刻画训练语料库的全局组成。因此需要一种能从模型权重直接恢复训练领域混合比例的方法。

Method: WARP 框架利用模型融合技术在基础模型和微调模型之间进行插值，生成伪检查点来近似缺失的训练轨迹，从而在权重空间中暴露训练数据的几何足迹。然后从这些模拟足迹中提取几何特征，通过无参数的 softmax 读出层或在合成混合数据上训练的 MLP 投影器，将几何特征映射为领域占比。

Result: 在 BERT 和 GPT-2 上的受控实验中，WARP 恢复领域混合比例的平均绝对误差（MAE）分别低至 0.046 和 0.104，优于成员推断方法以及一种能够访问真实训练轨迹的变体方法。

Conclusion: WARP 证明了仅从模型权重即可有效恢复训练数据的全局领域组成，为透明化模型训练数据配方提供了可行的技术路径，超越了现有样本级推断方法的局限。

Abstract: Foundation models are routinely released to the public, yet the data recipes used to train them -- such as domain mixture weights that determine how different sources are sampled -- are rarely disclosed. This creates an access asymmetry: researchers study the resulting models but lack visibility into the training distribution that produces them. Prior works for inferring training data, such as membership inference, detect at the level of individual samples and thus cannot characterize the global composition of the training corpus. We introduce WARP, a framework that recovers a fine-tuned model's training mixtures directly from its released weights. WARP interpolates between the base and fine-tuned models using model merging, generating pseudo-checkpoints that approximate the missing training trajectory and expose a geometric footprint of the training data in the weight space. From these simulated footprints, WARP extracts geometric features and maps them to domain proportions using either a parameter-free softmax readout or an MLP projector trained on synthetic mixtures. In controlled experiments with BERT and GPT-2, WARP recovers domain mixtures with an average MAE as low as 0.046 and 0.104 respectively, outperforming membership inference and a variant with access to the true training trajectory.

</details>


### [201] [Model Merging as Probabilistic Inference in Fine-Tuning Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01689)
*Long Minh Bui,Tuan Anh Le Van,Tung Phi Duc,Phi Le Nguyen,Jana Doppa,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率推断的模型合并新框架，将各单任务模型视为专家乘积（PoE）下的能量基模型（EBM），并发现现有方法隐式假设了高斯残差，而实际残差呈重尾分布。为此，作者采用柯西专家设计重尾PoE，显著提升了模型合并性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法主要依赖局部解空间的几何性质，缺乏从统计角度评估各任务特定更新方向在合并时跨任务有用性的有效手段。此外，经验发现合并模型与任务特定模型之间的方向残差常呈重尾分布，与现有方法隐含的高斯假设不匹配。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为专家乘积（PoE）场景下的概率推断问题：每个单任务解定义一个关于合并参数的能量基模型。推导出若干现有方法（如Fisher merging、RegMean、Task Arithmetic）均是该框架在高斯能量设计下的特例。进一步提出基于柯西分布的重尾PoE设计以匹配观测到的重尾残差行为，并给出可证明收敛的推断过程。

Result: 在多种任务和架构上的实验表明，基于柯西专家的重尾PoE方法相对于现有最优基线取得了显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 从概率推断视角重新审视模型合并，不仅统一了多种现有方法，还揭示了高斯假设的局限性；通过引入重尾分布（柯西专家）更好地捕捉实际残差分布，实现了更优的合并效果。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine existing single-task solutions into a multi-task solution without additional data-driven fine-tuning.~Most existing approaches achieve this using geometric properties of local solution spaces. However, such geometric views provide limited guidance for scoring how statistically useful each task-specific update direction is across tasks during merging. To address this, we formulate model merging from a new perspective of probabilistic inference under a product-of-experts (PoE) scenario where each single-task solution defines an energy-based expert model (EBM) over the merged parameters. We show that several existing model merging methods arise as special cases of our framework under energy designs that impose implicit Gaussian assumptions on directional residuals between merged and task-specific models. Empirically, we find that these residuals are often heavy-tailed which exposes a mismatch with the imposed light-tailed Gaussian structures. We address this with a heavy-tailed PoE design based on Cauchy experts, which better captures the observed residual behavior while admitting a provably convergent inference procedure. Experiments across multiple tasks and architectures show significant improvements over state-of-the-arts baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/MinhLong210/PoE-EBM-Merging.git.

</details>


### [202] [A Mathematical Introduction to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01693)
*Jianfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从采样的视角对扩散模型进行了面向证明的介绍，从经典采样动力学延伸到现代扩散采样器、误差分析及推理时控制，内容按难度分层编排，面向具有概率背景但未接触过随机微分方程的研究生初学者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为初学者提供一条连贯的学习路径，弥合从经典采样理论到现代扩散模型的鸿沟，帮助具有概率背景但缺乏随机微分方程和扩散模型知识的研究生逐步理解扩散模型的核心理论。

Method: 采用"单弧线"叙事结构：从经典采样动力学出发，逐步过渡到现代扩散采样器；内容分为三层——核心定义与完整证明、简化假设下的代表性估计、以及附带证明路线图的研究级定理，以板书笔记（lecture notes）形式呈现。

Result: 构建了一个自包含的、面向证明的教学框架，将扩散模型的采样视角、误差分析和推理时控制串联起来，使初学者能够在缺乏相关背景的情况下系统掌握扩散模型的理论基础。

Conclusion: 通过将内容围绕采样这一核心概念组织，并采用分层递进的证明结构，该教学材料有效降低了扩散模型理论的学习门槛，为目标读者群体提供了一条清晰的理论入门路径。

Abstract: These notes give a proof-oriented introduction to diffusion models from the viewpoint of sampling, tracing a single arc from classical sampling dynamics to modern diffusion samplers, their error analysis, and inference-time control. Throughout, the material is layered into core definitions and identities proved in full, representative estimates proved under simplifying assumptions, and research-level theorems stated with a proof roadmap. The intended audience is beginning graduate students with a background in probability but no prior exposure to stochastic differential equations, stochastic numerics, or diffusion models.

</details>


### [203] [Frequency Shift Physics-Informed Extreme Learning Machine for Solving High-Frequency Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01694)
*Xiong Xiong,Ruonan Zhai,Zheng Zeng,Sheng Zhou,Rongchun Hu,Zichen Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频率偏移物理信息极限学习机（FS-PIELM），通过平移高斯权重分布均值（而非缩放方差）来缓解神经网络求解高频PDE时的谱偏置问题，在七项基准测试中取得一至五个数量级的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络在求解高频解PDE时受谱偏置（spectral bias）困扰，即网络倾向优先学习低频分量。现有基于权重缩放的PIELM方法会放大方差，导致训练不稳定。需要一种既能引入高频表达能力、又不引入方差放大问题的初始化策略。

Method: 提出FS-PIELM框架：不通过缩放因子乘性调整随机权重，而是将高斯权重分布的均值进行平移（频率偏移），同时保持方差固定为1。开发两个变体：(1) FS-PIELM-L为每个神经元分配独立频率幅度；(2) FS-PIELM-G将神经元分组以增强鲁棒性。理论证明该框架下的频率方差保持有界并趋近于1，而传统方法的方差随目标频率二次增长。方法保持ELM的计算效率，仅需一次线性求解。

Result: 在Helmholtz、波动、Poisson、Klein-Gordon、热传导、对流-扩散六类方程共七个基准问题上进行实验，涵盖规则与复杂几何域。线性变体FS-PIELM-L在七项中的六项取得最优精度，相比现有PIELM变体提升一至近五个数量级。

Conclusion: FS-PIELM通过加性频率偏移机制有效克服了谱偏置问题，在保持ELM计算效率的同时显著提升高频PDE求解精度，代码将开源。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency solutions remains a central challenge in physics-informed machine learning due to spectral bias -- the tendency of neural networks to learn low-frequency components preferentially. This paper proposes a Frequency Shift Physics-Informed Extreme Learning Machine (FS-PIELM) framework that addresses this limitation through an additive mechanism for weight initialization. Rather than multiplying random weights by a scaling factor, the method translates the mean of the Gaussian weight distribution while keeping the variance fixed at unity, thereby avoiding the variance amplification inherent in scaling-based methods. Two variants are developed: FS-PIELM-L assigns independent frequency magnitudes to individual neurons, while FS-PIELM-G groups neurons for improved robustness. Theoretical analysis shows that the frequency variance under the proposed framework remains bounded and approaches unity regardless of target frequency, in contrast to the quadratic growth of conventional approaches. The method preserves the computational efficiency of extreme learning machines, requiring only a single linear solve. Experiments on seven benchmark problems spanning six equation types -- Helmholtz, wave, Poisson, Klein-Gordon, heat, and advection-diffusion -- on both regular and complex geometries show that the linear variant achieves the best accuracy in six of seven cases, with improvements of one to nearly five orders of magnitude over existing PIELM variants. The code and data accompanying this manuscript will be made publicly available at https://github.com/xgxgnpu/Physics-informed-vibe-coding/tree/main/FS-PIELM.

</details>


### [204] [Predicting Closed-Loop Performance of Latent World Models: Offline Checkpoint Selection for MPC and Model-Based RL Under Non-Markovian Rewards in LunarLander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01736)
*Nikolai Smolyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何仅用验证阶段的诊断指标来预测学习到的隐式世界模型的下游闭环性能，提出了一种基于最优控制理论的结构性诊断指标——可奖励观测分数（ROF）及其组合版本（CROF），用于离线检查点选择。CROF选出的世界模型在Gymnasium LunarLander v3上训练的基于模型的A2C策略，以约1/65的实境交互次数超越了无模型A2C基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型训练过程中，验证损失和多步预测RMSE在闭环性能崩溃后仍持续改善，导致无法依据这些常规指标选择最优检查点。因此亟需一种可靠的离线检查点选择方法。

Method: 作者在Gymnasium LunarLander v3上训练RSSM世界模型，以每个检查点的CEM-MPC回报作为闭环质量的oracle。从最优控制理论出发，设计了一组结构性验证时诊断指标（共40个），逐一与oracle做相关性评估。发现最强单一预测指标是可奖励观测分数（ROF），其衡量奖励预测器对可观测子空间的依赖程度。进一步将ROF与三个结构性正则项组合为单一离线选择分数CROF。

Result: CROF选出的世界模型训练的基于模型的A2C策略，超越公平评估的无模型A2C基线约24.5个回报点，且仅需约1/65的真实环境交互。同一世界模型也能驱动强大的零样本CEM-MPC策略。

Conclusion: 结构性验证诊断指标（尤其是ROF/CROF）能够有效替代在线闭环评估进行世界模型检查点选择，解决了验证损失等常规指标与闭环性能脱节的问题，显著提升了样本效率。

Abstract: We study how to predict the downstream closed-loop performance of a learned latent world model from validation-time diagnostics alone. Choosing the right checkpoint from a world-model training run is difficult: validation loss and multi-step prediction RMSE keep improving long after closed-loop performance has collapsed. We present a suite of structural validation-time diagnostics drawn from optimal-control theory and apply them to Gymnasium's LunarLander v3, which features shaped rewards. We train an RSSM [5, 4] world model on it and treat per checkpoint CEM-MPC return as the oracle for closed-loop quality. By evaluating 40 metrics against this oracle, we find that the strongest single predictor is the Reward Observability Fraction (ROF), which measures the reward predictor's dependence on the observable subspace. We combine ROF with three structural regularizers into a single-number offline checkpoint selection score, the Composite Reward Observability Fraction (CROF). The CROF-selected world model trains a model-based A2C policy that beats a fairly evaluated model-free A2C baseline by ~24.5 return points while using ~65x fewer real-environment interactions, and the same world model also drives a strong zero-shot CEM-MPC policy. Code and data: https://github.com/nsmoly/LunarLander_RSSM.

</details>


### [205] [Finite-Lag Operator Geometry of Recurrent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01746)
*Kanishka Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限滞后算子几何框架，用于分析循环神经网络中隐藏状态的递归表示。核心是通过条件传输律及其派生的传输张量、坐标环流等工具，将递归轨迹分解为条件扩散与相干位移，并证明其仿射协变性、估计稳定性及对确定性递归运动的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表示几何方法依赖静态快照，无法捕捉递归表示的轨迹动态特性。本文旨在开发一种基于有限滞后(source-successor pairs)的算子几何方法，从观测轨迹中提取有向的、滞后依赖的几何结构，以揭示递归系统中确定性运动与随机扩散的分解。

Method: 1. 定义有限滞后条件传输律 Q_Δ(dy|x)，通过稠密高斯源平滑算子估计；2. 推导以源为中心的传输张量 G_Δ，并证明其可精确分解为条件扩散(coherent spread)与相干位移(coherent displacement)两部分；3. 引入反对称坐标环流 W_Δ^ρ 来总结有向滞后流；4. 证明仿射协变性、稠密估计器在有界轨迹云上的稳定性，以及有限滞后分离定理；5. 通过线性高斯闭式解标定各几何量；6. 在性能匹配的repeat-copy网络上进行受控实验验证。

Result: 1. 传输张量可精确分解为条件扩散与相干位移，且该分解对确定性递归运动具有检测能力，优于无穷小carré-du-champ几何；2. 稠密估计器在有界轨迹云上稳定；3. 线性高斯闭式解明确给出了各量与更新矩阵A_Δ、源协方差、新息协方差的关系；4. 在repeat-copy网络实验中，相同性能下不同架构在总传输尺度与相干位移迹上表现出显著差异，而相干位移比例则依赖于度量和分辨率。

Conclusion: 有限滞后算子几何为递归表示提供了超越静态快照的分析工具，成功将轨迹动态分解为扩散与位移两个可解释成分，揭示了架构间的隐性差异。该方法具有仿射协变性和估计稳定性，为理解循环网络内部动态提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Recurrent representations are trajectories, but representation geometry is often measured from static snapshots. We develop finite-lag operator geometry for recurrent hidden states from observed source-successor pairs $(X_t,X_{t+Δ})$. The primitive is the conditional transport law $Q_Δ(dy\mid x)$, estimated by a dense Gaussian source-smoothing operator. From this directed finite-lag law we derive a source-centered transport tensor $G_Δ$, which decomposes exactly into conditional spread and coherent displacement, and an antisymmetric coordinate circulation $W_Δ^ρ$, which summarizes directed lagged flow. We prove affine covariance with explicit metric dependence of scalar summaries, dense estimator stability on bounded trajectory clouds, and a finite-lag separation result showing that source-centered transport detects deterministic recurrent motion not recorded by infinitesimal carre-du-champ geometry. A linear-Gaussian closed form calibrates the quantities in terms of the update $A_Δ$, source covariance, and innovation covariance. Controlled experiments validate the decomposition, circulation, covariance, and stability predictions. In performance matched repeat-copy networks, the framework reveals architecture dependent differences in total transport scale and coherent displacement trace, while coherent displacement fraction is metric and resolution dependent.

</details>


### [206] [Efficient Temporal Point Processes via Monotone Alternating Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01752)
*Cheng Wan,Quyu Kong,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单调交替样条（MAS）框架，用于替代现有单调神经网络（MNN）参数化累积条件强度函数（CCIF）的方法。MAS通过解耦内插与外推组件，解决了MNN存在的凸性限制、饱和极限及建模要求违反三大结构性死锁，在合成与真实数据集上均取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CCIF参数化方法统一依赖单调神经网络（MNN），但MNN存在三个结构性死锁：凸性限制、饱和极限以及违反CCIF建模要求，从根本上制约了其对复杂时序动态的表示能力，亟需新的参数化框架加以突破。

Method: 本文提出单调交替样条（Monotone Alternating Splines, MAS）框架，将CCIF建模解耦为内插（interpolation）和外推（extrapolation）两个独立组件。内插组件提供高精度的拟合能力，外推组件则支持鲁棒的泛化性能，二者协同克服MNN的不可约逼近间隙。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，MAS相对于现有MNN方法取得了显著的性能优势。

Conclusion: MAS框架通过灵活高效的CCIF建模方案，从理论和实践层面解决了MNN的结构性死锁问题，为时序点过程的累积条件强度函数建模提供了更具表达力和泛化能力的替代路径。

Abstract: Temporal point processes (TPPs) have widespread applications across various domains. Compared to modeling the conditional intensity of a TPP, modeling its cumulative conditional intensity function (CCIF) improves computational efficiency and eliminates numerical approximation errors. However, current CCIF parameterizations uniformly rely on Monotone Neural Networks (MNNs), which we identify as suffering from three structural deadlocks--convexity restrictions, saturation limits, and violations of CCIF modeling requirements--that fundamentally restrict their representational capacity for complex temporal dynamics. To resolve these bottlenecks, this paper proposes a novel framework called Monotone Alternating Splines (MAS). By leveraging distinct interpolation and extrapolation components, MAS provides a flexible and efficient framework for modeling CCIFs. Theoretically, MAS's interpolation provides strong fitting accuracy, while its extrapolation supports robust generalization, reducing the irreducible approximation gaps of MNNs. Extensive experiments show that MAS achieves superior performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [207] [Denser $\neq$ Better: Limits of On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continual Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01763)
*Meng Wang,Haohan Zhao,Wenzhuo Liu,Lu Yang,Geng Liu,Haiyang Guo,Guo-Sen Xie,Gaofeng Meng,Hongbin Liu,Fei Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了自蒸馏策略优化(SDPO)在持续后训练中的作用，发现SDPO虽然能加速域内专业化，但在分布外场景中会导致更严重的遗忘甚至崩溃，而GRPO等在线策略强化学习方法则更为保守和稳定。研究表明密集的自蒸馏会在参数空间和响应空间中引入更大漂移，并通过自我强化循环放大格式化伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究对在线策略自蒸馏在持续学习中的效果持乐观态度，认为其能缓解灾难性遗忘。本文旨在对这一乐观观点进行系统性质疑和重新检验，揭示在线策略数据本身在持续学习中的局限性。

Method: 作者提出自蒸馏策略优化(SDPO)框架，通过实验对比SDPO与GRPO等在线策略强化学习方法在持续后训练场景中的表现。分析维度包括：域内专业化速度、分布外泛化能力、遗忘程度、参数空间漂移、响应空间漂移，以及自蒸馏密度对格式化伪影的放大效应。

Result: SDPO在教师信号稳定且对齐良好时可加速域内专业化，但在分布外场景中泛化困难；持续后训练中SDPO遗忘更严重甚至崩溃，而GRPO更保守地保留先验能力；更密集的自蒸馏导致参数空间和响应空间的更大漂移，并通过自我强化的师生循环放大高频格式化伪影。

Conclusion: 在线策略数据本身不足以支撑持续学习。密集自蒸馏可在教师目标稳定且token级监督可靠时加速专业化，但不应被当作持续后训练的默认稳定器使用。

Abstract: Continual post-training enables foundation models to acquire new knowledge while preserving existing capabilities. Recent work suggests that on-policy learning can mitigate forgetting, with on-policy self-distillation emerging as a particularly attractive approach. In this work, we revisit this optimistic view through self-distillation policy optimization (SDPO). Our experiments show that SDPO can accelerate in-domain specialization when teacher signals are stable and well aligned, but it struggles to generalize to out-of-distribution scenarios. In continual post-training, SDPO exhibits stronger forgetting and can even collapse, whereas on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO adapt more conservatively and better preserve prior capabilities. Further analyses reveal that denser self-distillation induces larger drift in both parameter space and response space, and can amplify high-frequency formatting artifacts through a self-reinforcing teacher--student loop. These findings suggest that on-policy data alone is insufficient for continual learning. Dense self-distillation can accelerate specialization when teacher targets are stable and token-level supervision is reliable, but it should not be treated as a default stabilizer for continual post-training. Our code is available at https://github.com/Moenupa/SDPO-CL.

</details>


### [208] [Set Diffusion: Interpolating Token Orderings Between Autoregression and Diffusion for Fast and Flexible Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01775)
*Marianne Arriola,Volodymyr Kuleshov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出集合扩散（Set Diffusion）语言模型，通过将似然参数化分解到灵活位置、灵活长度的token集合上，并结合集合因果扩散架构支持KV缓存，实现任意顺序解码和更快的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型受限于固定长度生成且不支持KV缓存；块扩散虽部分弥合了扩散与自回归模型的差距，但其固定大小的顺序块限制了解码灵活性和并行性。需要一种更灵活的扩散范式。

Method: 提出集合扩散框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 对灵活位置、灵活长度的token集合进行因子分解的似然参数化；(2) 支持每步推理后更新KV缓存的集合因果扩散架构。通过按集合而非固定块进行因子分解，token可以按任意顺序的集合解码（如滑动窗口集合）。

Result: 在数学推理、摘要生成和无条件生成任务上，集合扩散相比先前扩散语言模型取得了更好的速度-质量权衡；在填充（infilling）性能上优于块扩散。

Conclusion: 集合扩散作为一类新的语言模型范式，通过灵活的集合因子分解和KV缓存支持，有效提升了解码灵活性与推理效率，在多个生成任务上表现优越。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have steadily improved in quality relative to autoregressive (AR) models. However, these models are normally constrained to fixed-length generation and do not support key-value (KV) caching. Block diffusion partially bridges diffusion and AR by generating token blocks left-to-right, but its fixed-size sequential blocks limit decoding flexibility and parallelism. Here, we present a new class of language models, set diffusion, comprised of (i) a likelihood parameterization that factorizes over flexible-position, flexible-length token sets and (ii) a set-causal diffusion architecture that supports KV cache updates after every inference step. By factorizing over token sets instead of fixed-size blocks, tokens can be decoded in arbitrarily-ordered sets, including sliding-window sets, enabling faster inference and support for any-order decoding. Set diffusion achieves better speed-quality tradeoffs on mathematical reasoning, summarization, and unconditional generation compared to prior diffusion language models while offering stronger infilling performance than block diffusion. We provide the code, along with the model weights and blog post on the project page: https://m-arriola.com/setdlms/

</details>
