Additionally, we also leverage DNG-Encoder to develop INR2JLS (Implicit Neural Representation to Joint Latent Space) for facilitate downstream applications, such as classifying Implicit Neural Representations (INRs). Our approach demonstrates significant improvements across multiple tasks, surpassing the state-of-the-art INR classification accuracy by approximately 10% on the CIFAR-100-INR.

</details>


### [244] [Privacy-Preserving and Verifiable Approximate Distributed Coded Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02187)
*Xavier Martínez-Luaña,Alba Gude-Santos,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的对抗鲁棒分布式学习框架，将隐私保护编码计算技术GPBACC与针对联邦学习和去中心化学习的特定防御机制相结合，统一应对隐私泄露和恶意攻击。通过攻击驱动的实证评估，证明该框架能显著降低隐私泄露并提升对主动攻击者的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式机器学习在联邦学习和去中心化学习两种范式下，面临隐私泄露与恶意操纵两类威胁。现有防御方案通常孤立地处理单一威胁，且与特定学习范式或模型架构强绑定，缺乏统一框架来同时应对两种范式下的不同攻击面。

Method: 提出模型无关的对抗鲁棒分布式学习框架：1）引入GPBACC——一种适用于任意机器学习模型的隐私增强编码计算技术；2）对联邦学习，集成鲁棒聚合策略以抑制恶意参与方影响；3）对去中心化学习，采用近似解码-比较和群组测试技术，实现轻量级验证与对手隔离，无需可信聚合器；4）通过实现代表性隐私攻击与恶意行为，进行攻击驱动的实证评估。

Result: GPBACC与鲁棒聚合及验证机制的结合，能够显著降低隐私泄露程度，并提升系统对主动攻击者的恢复能力。

Conclusion: 隐私增强编码计算技术与适当的对抗防御策略相结合，可为安全分布式机器学习提供实用且可部署的基础方案。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning enables collaborative model training without centralizing data, but it also exposes learning processes to privacy leakage and malicious manipulation. Existing defenses typically address these threats in isolation and are often tailored to specific learning paradigms or model architectures, limiting their applicability in realistic deployments. In particular, federated learning and decentralized learning exhibit distinct adversarial surfaces that are rarely addressed within a unified framework. In this paper, we present a model-agnostic framework for adversary-resistant distributed learning that jointly addresses privacy preservation and malicious behavior across both federated and decentralized settings. Our approach combines paradigm-specific defense mechanisms with GPBACC, a privacy-enhancing coded computing technique applicable to arbitrary machine learning models. For federated learning, we integrate robust aggregation strategies to mitigate the impact of malicious participants, while for decentralized learning we employ approximate decode-and-compare and group testing techniques to enable lightweight verification and adversary isolation without relying on a trusted aggregator. Crucially, we evaluate the proposed framework through an explicit, attack-driven analysis. We implement representative privacy attacks and malicious behaviors, and empirically demonstrate that the combination of GPBACC with robust aggregation and verification mechanisms significantly reduces privacy leakage and improves resilience against active adversaries. These results suggest that privacy-enhancing coded computing, when combined with appropriate adversary-resistance strategies, provides a practical and deployable foundation for secure distributed machine learning.

</details>


### [245] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSGNAR是一个基于双重草图高斯-牛顿法的二阶优化框架，通过精心控制正则化和步长来解决PINNs中的病态优化问题，在多个PDE基准上实现了前所未有的精度（双精度下相对误差低至3×10⁻¹⁶）和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs虽然在求解偏微分方程方面前景广阔，但其优化过程因损失景观严重病态而难以达到经典求解器的精度。现有方法在精度和速度上存在明显不足，亟需一种能够有效应对这种病态条件的优化框架。

Method: 提出DSGNAR框架，核心包括两个部分：(1) 双重草图高斯-牛顿模型（doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton），利用草图技术对二阶信息进行高效近似；(2) 自适应比例策略，同时精细控制正则化强度与步长。该框架对网络架构、算术精度和初始超参数具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维及Navier-Stokes等广泛问题上验证：(1) 双精度下相对ℓ₂误差低至3×10⁻¹⁶；(2) Burgers方程上比现有结果提升五个数量级；(3) 高维Poisson问题上提升八个数量级；(4) 单精度下Burgers方程在十秒内达到4.75×10⁻⁷的相对误差，接近舍入误差极限。

Conclusion: DSGNAR通过有效应对PINNs的病态优化问题，在精度和速度上均大幅超越现有方法，达到了接近机器精度的求解水平，为PINNs在高精度科学计算中的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [246] [Online Resource Allocation with Continuous Random Consumption: Regret under Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02196)
*Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在奖励和资源消耗均连续分布下的在线资源分配问题。核心发现是：加性遗憾由"价值-规模比接近活跃接受阈值的请求的规模加权质量"所决定，通过活跃加权质量指数 p 刻画。当 p > 1 时，遗憾下界为 T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}，样本路径边际策略可匹配该下界（至多对数因子）；当 p = 1 时，遗憾为 O((log T)^2)。该策略在无需流体非退化假设下实现 o(√T) 遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的在线资源分配问题通常假设奖励或资源消耗为离散分布，或依赖流体松弛的非退化假设以得到 √T 量级的遗憾界。然而，当奖励和消耗均为连续分布时，确定性流体松弛可能出现退化（即对偶解不唯一），此时标准的分析方法失效。本文旨在刻画这一更一般设定下的遗憾下界，并设计无需非退化假设即可实现 o(√T) 遗憾的在线策略。

Method: 首先引入"活跃加权质量指数"p，用于量化在最优对偶阈值附近、请求的价值-规模比密度以规模加权的形态。接着，通过构造信息论下界，证明当 p > 1 时任何在线策略的遗憾至少为 Ω(T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)})。然后提出"样本路径边际策略"(sample-path marginal policy)，该策略根据历史观测动态更新对偶阈值，在不依赖流体非退化假设的前提下，证明其遗憾上界为 Õ(T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)})（p > 1）或 O((log T)^2)（p = 1），从而匹配下界。分析的关键在于处理双重退化（原始退化与对偶非唯一性）。

Result: (1) 遗憾下界：对任意 p > 1，任何在线策略的遗憾至少为 Ω(T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)})。(2) 遗憾上界：样本路径边际策略在 p > 1 时实现 Õ(T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}) 遗憾，在 p = 1 时实现 O((log T)^2) 遗憾，均匹配相应下界。(3) 实例：若规模和价值-规模比独立且均匀分布，则 p = 1；若规模和奖励独立且均匀分布，则 p = 2。(4) 该策略在整个正则性类中实现 o(√T) 遗憾，且无需流体非退化假设。

Conclusion: 在线资源分配问题的难度本质上由最优对偶阈值附近的请求"质量"密度决定，通过指数 p 可精确刻画遗憾的阶。样本路径边际策略在不依赖流体非退化假设的情况下实现了紧的遗憾界，统一处理了原始退化和对偶非唯一性问题，显著拓展了在线资源分配理论在高维连续随机环境中的适用性。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation when both rewards and consumption sizes may be continuously distributed. Requests arrive sequentially and must be accepted or rejected irrevocably under fixed resource capacities. Each request belongs to one of finitely many observable types; conditional on an observable request type, both the reward and the scalar size are random, and the realized size scales a fixed type-specific resource-consumption vector. The model allows the deterministic fluid relaxation to be degenerate.
  We show that additive regret is governed by the size-weighted mass of requests whose value-to-size ratios lie near the active acceptance cutoffs. We formalize this quantity through an active weighted-mass exponent p. When p > 1, this cutoff mass is thin, and the problem is genuinely hard: every online policy must incur regret of order at least $T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}$, and this holds for every p > 1. A sample-path marginal policy matches this lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors; and when p = 1, so that the mass grows linearly near the cutoff, it attains $O((\log T)^2)$ regret. For example, if the size and the value-to-size ratio are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 1; if instead the size and the reward are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 2. Thus the policy achieves $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret throughout this regularity class without any fluid non-degeneracy assumption, allowing both primal degeneracy and dual non-uniqueness.

</details>


### [247] [Self-explainable Operator Learning for Discovering Spatial Patterns in Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02203)
*Mojgan Alishiri,Amirhossein Arzani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自解释的算子学习框架，将算子学习重新表述为广义泛函线性模型的线性组合，通过积分方程的可加分解性实现输入域子区域的贡献评估，使模型能够直接解释输入区域与输出模式之间的关联，无需依赖外部事后解释工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子学习方法基于神经网络，模型不透明，预测背后的推理过程不可见，这限制了其在科学应用中的可信度。亟需一种内建可解释性的算子学习框架，使模型能够自我解释其决策过程。

Method: 将算子学习重新表述为基于积分方程表达的广义泛函线性模型的线性组合。利用积分方程的可加分解性，将输入域划分为子域，计算各子域的局部积分，评估每个区域对最终预测的贡献。这种分解使模型能够直接将特定输入区域与相应输出模式联系起来，实现内建的可解释性。

Result: 在血流动力学和非定常空气动力学的函数到标量及函数到函数映射任务上验证了框架有效性。结果表明，算子通常优先关注具有强特征梯度的区域，提供了物理上有意义的洞见。与现有事后解释方法的对比显示定性一致，且本方法无需外部工具即可实现解释。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了数学透明、物理可解释的方式来揭示数据中的关系，通过将可解释性直接嵌入算子结构，促进了科学应用中机器学习模型的可信度，使基于数据驱动的物理系统分析更加可靠。

Abstract: Operator learning has emerged as a powerful tool for modeling complex physical systems in functional spaces. However, their neural network-based architectures make them opaque models, obscuring the reasoning behind their predictions. In this work, we introduce a self-explainable operator learning framework that overcomes this challenge by reformulating operator learning as a linear combination of generalized functional linear models expressed through integral equations. Exploiting the additive decomposability of these integral equations, we divide the input domain into subdomains and compute localized integrals to evaluate the contribution of each region to the final prediction. This decomposition enables direct interpretability where the model explains both inputs and outputs by linking specific input regions to corresponding output patterns, thereby revealing which spatial features drive predictions. We demonstrate the framework on function-to-scalar and function-to-function mappings in fluid flow problems involving blood flow and unsteady aerodynamics. The results show that the operator most often prioritizes regions with strong feature gradients, providing physically meaningful insight into the model's decision-making process. Comparisons with established post-hoc explainability methods demonstrate qualitative agreement while highlighting the key advantage of the proposed approach: explainability is embedded directly within the operator structure itself and does not require an external tool. Therefore, our framework provides a mathematically transparent and physically interpretable approach to uncover relationships within data, fostering trust in machine learning for scientific applications by enabling more informed data-driven analysis of physical systems.

</details>


### [248] [Generalization in offline RL: The structure is more important than the amount of pessimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02288)
*Max Weltevrede,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Wendelin Böhmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了离线强化学习中，过度悲观并不会阻碍最优泛化；关键在于悲观结构是否尊重最优解的底层对称性。对称但过度悲观的价值函数比非对称但轻度悲观的价值函数泛化更好，并提出通过策略提取阶段的一致性损失来施加数据增强，而非在训练集上直接增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为离线RL中的过度保守会阻碍泛化，但作者挑战这一直觉，提出悲观的结构（是否对称）而非悲观的程度才是决定泛化能力的关键因素。这一新视角为离线RL中的泛化问题提供了不同的理论理解。

Method: 在上下文MDP（CMDP）框架下，通过理论证明对比了轻度悲观但非对称的价值函数与过度悲观但对称的价值函数在泛化性能上的差异。进一步将悲观结构与数据集覆盖的结构联系起来，并提出在策略提取阶段使用一致性损失（consistency loss）来施加数据增强，而非传统的在增强数据集上进行离线训练。实验上使用IQL和CQL算法在旋转对称的reacher环境中验证。

Result: 理论上证明：对称的过度悲观价值函数可以优于非对称的轻度悲观价值函数。实验上验证了在策略提取阶段施加一致性损失的数据增强方式优于在增强数据集上直接训练的常规做法。

Conclusion: 离线RL中成功泛化的关键不是悲观的量，而是悲观结构是否与最优解的对称性一致。数据增强应通过策略提取时的一致性损失来施加，这比在增强数据集上训练更有效。

Abstract: While pessimism counteracts overestimation bias in offline reinforcement learning (RL), being overly conservative has been associated with hindering certain forms of generalization. However, in this paper we demonstrate that being overly pessimistic does not inherently prevent optimal generalization in contextual MDPs (CMDPs). Instead, we argue successful generalization depends not on the amount of pessimism, but whether the pessimistic structure respects the underlying symmetries of the optimal solution. We prove that a mildly pessimistic, non-symmetric value function can generalize worse than an overly pessimistic, symmetric one. In offline RL, the structure of the pessimism is determined by the structure of the dataset coverage. As such, enforcing a symmetric value function can be non-trivial, and might require techniques such as data augmentation (DA). Inspired by our theoretical results, we argue that DA can best be applied through a consistency loss during policy extraction, rather than the common practice of (regular) offline training on an augmented dataset. This is empirically validated using IQL and CQL on a rotationally symmetric reacher environment.

</details>


### [249] [Optimizing Visual Generative Models via Distribution-wise Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02291)
*Ruihang Li,Mengde Xu,Shuyang Gu,Leigang Qu,Fuli Feng,Han Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分布级奖励（distribution-wise reward）的强化学习微调框架，用于视觉生成模型，通过子集替换策略高效估计分布奖励，并结合后验模型融合系数优化，有效缓解奖励黑客（reward hacking）和模式坍塌问题，在SiT和EDM2等模型上显著提升FID-50K指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉生成的强化学习通常采用样本级奖励函数，容易导致奖励黑客现象：模型为最大化奖励而牺牲图像多样性，并引入视觉伪影。此外，样本独立优化会导致模式坍塌。因此，需要一种能够从分布层面评估生成质量、且计算上可行的奖励机制。

Method: 核心方法包括三部分：(1) 提出分布级奖励函数，以生成样本的整体数据分布而非单样本作为评估对象，缓解模式坍塌；(2) 引入子集替换策略（subset-replace strategy），仅更新生成参考集中的一小部分样本，大幅降低分布奖励的估计计算成本；(3) 使用强化学习优化后验模型融合系数（post-hoc model merging coefficients），缓解因引入随机微分方程（SDE）导致的训练-推理不一致问题。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个基座模型上显著改善FID-50K：SiT从8.30降至5.77，EDM2从3.74降至3.52。定性评估也证实该方法在保持样本多样性的同时提升了感知质量。

Conclusion: 分布级奖励配合高效子集替换策略，能有效克服样本级RL微调中的奖励黑客和模式坍塌问题，同时后验融合系数优化缓解了SDE引入的不一致性，在定量和定性指标上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Conventional reinforcement learning strategies for visual generation typically employ sample-wise reward functions, yet this practice frequently results in reward hacking that degrades image diversity and introduces visual anomalies. To address these limitations, we present a novel framework that finetunes generative models using distribution-wise rewards, ensuring better alignment with real-world data distributions. Unlike rewards that evaluate samples individually, distribution-wise reward accounts for the data distribution of the samples, mitigating the mode collapse problem that occurs when all samples optimize towards the same direction independently. To overcome the prohibitive computational cost of estimating these rewards, we introduce a subset-replace strategy that efficiently provides reward signals by updating only a small subset of a generated reference set. Additionally, we apply RL to optimize post-hoc model merging coefficients, potentially mitigating the train-inference inconsistency caused by introducing stochastic differential equation (SDE) in regular RL practices. Extensive experiments show our approach significantly improves FID-50K across various base models, from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and from 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2. Qualitative evaluation also confirms that our method enhances perceptual quality while preserving sample diversity.

</details>


### [250] [One More Time: Revisiting Neural Quantum States from a Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02292)
*Juan Agustín Duque,Sergio García Heredia,Vinicius Hernandes,Eliška Greplová,Thomas Spriggs,Aaron Courville,Anna Dawid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将变分能量最小化重新框架为优势策略梯度问题，提出Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO)——一种基于信任区域的NQS训练算法，通过裁剪振幅和相位通道的更新来稳定训练，避免了矩阵求逆，在多个自旋系统上优于Adam、minSR和SPRING，并首次将NQS优化扩展至15亿参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NQS优化方法存在两难困境：Adam等一阶方法可扩展但忽略函数空间几何结构；随机重配置(SR)有理论保证但需矩阵求逆，在大模型中计算昂贵且数值不稳定。自回归NQS的优化方法探索不足，亟需一种兼具可扩展性和理论保证的优化算法。

Method: 将变分能量最小化视为Born分布上的优势策略梯度问题，由此推导出信任区域优化框架。提出PWO算法：在振幅通道对概率比变化进行裁剪，在相位通道对相位增量进行裁剪，实现双通道信任区域约束。该方法无需显式矩阵求逆，支持多样本复用，将一阶优化的可扩展性与信任区域的理论保证相结合。

Result: 在Ising模型和阻挫J1-J2一维/二维自旋系统上，PWO在稳定性和实际收敛时间上均优于Adam、minSR和SPRING。最终在15亿参数的RWKV-7模型上进行微调，将NQS优化规模提升了三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: PWO成功桥接了大规模自回归NQS训练中可扩展性与理论严谨性之间的鸿沟，为将NQS推向百亿参数级别的大规模量子多体问题求解开辟了道路。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible and scalable framework for approximating quantum many-body wavefunctions. Among NQS parameterizations, autoregressive models are especially attractive because they enable exact, independent sampling from the Born distribution, avoiding the autocorrelation and mixing issues of Markov chain methods. Yet their optimization remains comparatively underexplored: Adam is a scalable method but ignores function space geometry, while stochastic reconfiguration is principled but costly and numerically fragile in large models. To address this gap, we show that variational energy minimization can be viewed as an advantage policy-gradient problem over the Born distribution, motivating trust-region optimization for NQS training. We introduce Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO), a principled trust-region algorithm that clips probability-ratio changes in the amplitude channel and phase increments in the phase channel. PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [251] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为自门控注意力（SGA）的即插即用注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵加输入依赖残差的方式替代传统的查询-键投影，将自注意力的时间和存储复杂度从二次降低到线性，在保持预测性能的同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头自注意力在时间序列预测中具有二次时间和内存复杂度（相对于回溯窗口长度），限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐场景下的部署。作者通过定性和定量分析发现，时间序列预测中的注意力图往往包含跨时间戳的冗余模式，这与真实世界时间序列中重复的时序模式和相对稳定的时序相关性有关。

Method: 提出自门控注意力（Self-Gating Attention, SGA），一种即插即用的注意力机制。SGA 将注意力分数表示为一个共享可学习矩阵与一个输入依赖的残差分量的叠加：共享矩阵捕获通用的注意力模式，残差分量捕获输入依赖的变化。该设计避免了标准注意力中的查询（Query）和键（Key）投影计算，从而将时间和注意力分数矩阵的内存复杂度降至线性。

Result: 在覆盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人体活动和气候记录的9个公开真实世界数据集上，将SGA集成到多种预测骨干网络中，与标准自注意力和轻量级注意力变体进行对比。结果表明，SGA在公开基准上提升了推理效率，同时保持了与最先进注意力机制相竞争的性能。

Conclusion: SGA 有效解决了标准自注意力在时间序列预测中的效率瓶颈，以线性复杂度实现了与二次复杂度注意力机制相媲美的预测性能，为实际部署提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [252] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DecompRL 提出将复杂问题分解为可独立求解的子模块，通过重新组合实现方案数量指数级增长（k^n），将瓶颈从 GPU 推理转移到廉价 CPU 评估，在 LiveCodeBench 和 CodeContests 上以约 50 倍 GPU token 成本削减超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 在面对几乎零概率产生正确解的问题时，重复采样（受 GPU 成本线性增长限制）和 RL with verifiable rewards（牺牲样本多样性）均无效。核心痛点是搜索空间过大，需要一种范式转换——不是更努力地采样，而是通过分解让任务本身变得更容易。

Method: 提出 DecompRL，一种强化学习算法，显式学习将问题分解为层级化代码结构（n 个子模块）。训练后，对每个子模块独立生成 k 个实现，再重新组合产生最多 k^n 个候选解。该过程将计算瓶颈从 GPU 推理（生成 token）转移到 CPU 评估（组合验证）。

Result: 在 LiveCodeBench 和 CodeContests 基准上（使用 Qwen 2.5 7B 和 Code World Model 32B），DecompRL 在每道题超过 10^5 token 的预算下，显著优于标准 RL 和多样性优化 RL 基线，解决了标准生成方法无法触及的问题，GPU token 成本降低约 50 倍。

Conclusion: 通过将问题分解与模块化重组结合 RL 训练，DecompRL 提供了一种突破 LLM 推理瓶颈的新路径：在基策略几乎无解时，不是增加采样次数，而是结构化地缩小搜索空间，实现以 CPU 算力替代 GPU 算力的大幅效率提升。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [253] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuFS，一种基于神经元感知的主动少样本学习框架，通过利用LLM内部神经元激活模式（而非传统输出级信号）来识别最具价值的未标注样本，实现高效标注和少样本演示选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFSL方法依赖输出级信号（预测熵、外部嵌入的语义相似度）来筛选样本，忽略了模型内部动态，无法精确定位LLM的特定知识缺口。需要一种能从模型内部状态出发、更精准地识别信息量大且易导致幻觉的样本的选择范式。

Method: NeuFS框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 利用神经元激活模式直接表征样本；(2) 双准则选择策略——基于神经元模式确保少样本多样性以扩大覆盖范围，同时通过量化“神经元共识”优先识别LLM易产生幻觉的信息性强且具挑战性的样本；(3) 以神经元内部激活替代外部嵌入作为选择信号。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务上均优于现有AFSL基线方法。消融研究进一步证明，内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供了更具原则性和有效性的选择信号。

Conclusion: NeuFS成功将主动少样本学习的样本选择范式从输出级代理信号转向模型内部神经元动态，验证了神经元激活模式作为选择信号的优势，为AFSL提供了一种更本质且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [254] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QFedAgent 提出一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，利用变分量子电路融合模块建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，在非独立同分布多模态传感器流下实现97.7%的平均测试准确率，同时参数量仅为经典MLP融合的约1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统产生的异构、非独立同分布多模态传感器流会显著降低传统联邦学习算法性能，且经典多模态融合模块（如MLP）引入大量参数和通信开销。亟需一种参数高效的多模态融合方案来支撑隐私敏感的机器人感知场景。

Method: 提出 QFedAgent 框架，核心创新为变分量子电路（VQC）融合模块：通过量子态编码和纠缠门建模加速度计与陀螺仪信号间的交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数；结合个性化联邦学习策略，在本地客户端进行量子融合与模型训练，仅共享经典参数。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上基于受试者非IID划分进行实验：达到97.7%平均测试准确率；量子融合模块仅72参数 vs 经典MLP融合的33K参数，实现约10倍总参数量缩减，且性能与经典联邦基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合方案在联邦多智能体活动识别任务中可与经典方法相媲美，验证了量子-经典混合计算在资源受限、隐私敏感的分布式感知场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [255] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Exformer 是一种面向极端事件的 Transformer 预测框架，通过引入局部、步长和极端三分支稀疏注意力机制，在高度偏态的水文径流数据上显著提升了3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 模型在时间序列预测中平等对待所有时间点，导致对稀有但关键的极端事件（如洪水峰值）建模不足，而水文径流数据通常呈高度偏态分布，极端事件对洪水监测和水资源管理至关重要。

Method: 提出 Extreme-Adaptive Transformer（Exformer），核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏组件：（1）Local 组件捕获短期时序依赖；（2）Stride 组件捕获周期性依赖；（3）Extreme 组件选择性地建模正常与极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖。

Result: 在四个真实世界水文径流数据集上的实验表明，Exformer 在3天预测任务上均优于现有最先进基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力能够有效提升 Transformer 模型在存在稀有但重要事件的不平衡时间序列上的预测能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [256] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格的理论分析，证明掩码图像建模（MIM）天然比对比学习（CL）对数据异质性更鲁棒，且网络平均连通性越高鲁棒性越强。基于理论，提出MAR损失，并通过实验验证了理论洞见和MAR损失的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习面临数据异质性的关键挑战，但不同D-SSL框架在此挑战下的理论理解十分有限，缺乏对框架鲁棒性的严格理论分析。

Method: 对D-SSL框架在非IID设置下的鲁棒性进行严格理论分析，比较MIM与CL两种预训练范式；提出MAR损失，即在MIM目标基础上加入局部到全局的对齐正则化。

Result: 理论证明：(1) MIM预训练天生比CL对异质数据更鲁棒；(2) D-SSL鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提升，联邦学习（FL）鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。实验在多种模型架构和分布式设置下验证了理论发现及MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论指导，MAR损失作为理论应用实例表现出良好效果，建议未来D-SSL算法优先考虑MIM范式并增强网络连通性。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [257] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Neuron-OPSD 是一种无需标注的自蒸馏框架，通过内部神经元激活来指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，并采用 on-policy 蒸馏方式，在提升特定领域任务性能的同时保持跨域泛化能力，并缓解校准崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家标注成本高昂的专业领域中，无需真实交互反馈或人工监督对大语言模型进行后训练极具挑战。现有无标注自进化方法（如基于 SFT/GRPO 或基于奖励的 on-policy RL）存在域外性能退化或校准误差膨胀等问题，亟需一种新的范式来解决这些缺陷。

Method: 提出 Neuron-OPSD（Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation），一种以数据为中心的无标注自蒸馏框架。核心思路包括：1）利用模型内部神经元激活信号来指导训练数据的选择；2）基于神经元激活构建教师上下文（teacher context）；3）通过 on-policy 蒸馏，从教师分布中训练模型，全程不需要任何真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准测试中，Neuron-OPSD 相较于先前的无标注基线方法，提升了领域内任务性能，同时保持了跨领域泛化能力，并有效缓解了校准崩溃（calibration collapse）问题。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD 为在线交互或外部监督成本高昂甚至不可行的场景提供了一种有效的自蒸馏方案，与依赖已记录、带奖励标签轨迹的离线 RL 方法在概念上有本质区别。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [258] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）在训练MLIP（NequIP和Allegro）中的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于默认的Adam，尤其在部分力监督场景下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP社区在架构和数据集方面已投入大量研究，但优化器的选择一直被忽视，默认使用Adam及其变体。作者旨在探索矩阵结构优化器是否能突破这一惯性，提升训练效率和模型精度。

Method: 以NequIP和Allegro两种等变MLIP模型为测试平台，系统对比Adam与三种矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）在不同训练设定（包括部分力监督）下的收敛速度和最终精度。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam，表现稳健且一致；Muon仅带来部分提升。在部分力监督条件下，改进尤为突出。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，矩阵结构优化器（尤其是SOAP/SOAP-Muon）有潜力成为Adam的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [259] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD提出了一种选择性采纳教师指导的在线自蒸馏框架，通过构建教师与学生分布的逆向KL barycenter目标，根据分布差异自适应控制逐token的混合比例，从理论和实验上证明了该方法能有效缓解特权信息泄露并保持探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）存在两个核心问题：(1) 教师模型基于特权信息的密集token级监督会导致学生对域内模式过拟合，抑制探索，损害跨域泛化；(2) 更根本的"特权信息泄露"问题，学生会编码依赖答案的捷径，这些捷径在测试时不可用。因此需要一种能选择性采纳教师指导的框架来解决上述问题。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是选择性采纳教师指导。具体而言：(1) 不让学生拟合完整的教师分布，而是引导其逼近一个逆向KL barycenter目标——即教师分布与学生分布的加权几何混合；(2) 通过测量两个分布之间的差异，自适应地控制每个token位置的混合权重；(3) 从理论上证明了该方法具有泄露衰减（特权信息泄露的有效缓解）和探索保持（密集token级蒸馏下保持探索能力）两个性质。

Result: 在SciKnowEval的四个科学领域上进行了广泛实验，结果表明DemoPSD同时优于GRPO和SDPO，同时保持更高的训练熵，并能在分布外GPQA基准测试上实现稳健泛化。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过逆向KL barycenter目标和自适应混合机制，有效解决了OPSD中特权信息泄露和探索能力丧失的问题，在科学推理任务上验证了其有效性和分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [J- and MJ-Type Tests for Non-Nested Parametric Survival Models with a Cure Fraction: A Score Test Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01379)
*Cynthia A. V. Tojeiro,Francisco Cribari-Neto,Tatiene C. Souza,Tarciana L. Pereira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于区分具有治愈分数的非嵌套参数生存模型的规范检验方法。该方法通过将竞争模型信息增强到原模型的零似然中，并应用得分检验来评估增强信息的冗余性。对于两个竞争模型，其得分统计量简化为个体对数似然差异样本均值的二次型，其带符号平方根与Vuong检验统计量一致。该方法扩展至M≥2个模型，并结合参数自助法估计Kullback-Leibler偏差，形成MJ统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中，具有治愈分数（cure fraction）的参数生存模型常被用于分析存在长期幸存者的时间-事件数据。当多个非嵌套模型仅基线分布不同时，研究者需要一种有效的模型判别方法。现有方法（如Vuong检验）存在局限：仅适用于两两比较、使用带符号统计量，且难以自然扩展至多个模型。因此，需要一种能够同时检验多个模型设定正确性、并提供模型选择准则的新框架。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于得分检验（score test）的框架：首先，将竞争模型的对数似然信息作为增强项加入原模型（待检验模型）的零对数似然中；然后，仅在原模型下进行受限最大似然估计（无需拟合增强模型），构建得分统计量检验增强信息是否冗余。对于两个模型的情形，统计量简化为个体对数似然差异样本均值的二次型。该框架采用无符号统计量，自然扩展至M≥2个竞争模型，并通过参数自助法（parametric bootstrap）估计Kullback-Leibler偏差，最终构建MJ统计量，同时实现全局零假设检验（至少一个候选模型正确设定）与模型选择。

Result: 在两模型情形下，得分统计量的带符号平方根与Vuong检验统计量一致，验证了该方法的合理性。MJ统计量能够有效检验全局零假设（即至少有一个候选模型是正确设定的数据生成过程），并同时提供模型选择准则。参数自助法成功估计了Kullback-Leibler偏差，避免了依赖渐近近似的局限。

Conclusion: 所提出的规范检验框架为具有治愈分数的非嵌套参数生存模型提供了一种灵活且可扩展的判别工具。与Vuong检验相比，该方法具有三个优势：检验特定的零假设（某一模型为真实数据生成过程）、使用无符号统计量自然扩展至多个模型、通过参数自助法估计KL偏差。MJ统计量同时实现了全局设定检验与模型选择，为应用研究者提供了实用的模型判别手段。

Abstract: We propose specification tests for discriminating among non-nested parametric survival models with a cure fraction, focusing on models that differ only in their baseline distributions. The proposed approach augments the null log-likelihood with information from competing models and applies a score test to assess whether the additional information is redundant. Because the test relies only on restricted maximum likelihood estimates, it avoids fitting augmented models. For two competing models, the score statistic reduces to a quadratic form in the sample mean of the individual log-likelihood differences. We show that its signed square root coincides with Vuong's test statistic, although our framework differs in three important respects: it tests the specific null hypothesis that a given model is the true data-generating process, it uses an unsigned statistic that extends naturally to $M \ge 2$ competing models, and it estimates the Kullback-Leibler bias by parametric bootstrap. The resulting MJ statistic combines the individual J tests to assess the global null hypothesis that at least one candidate model is correctly specified, while also providing a model-selection criterion.

</details>


### [261] [Continuous-Time Bayesian Networks with Structured Shrinkage Priors for Modelling Multimorbidity Trajectories in Large-Scale Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01549)
*Oyebayo R. Olaniran,Soumya S. Paria,Mizanur Khondoker,Alexander J. MacGregor,Alexandra Lewin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化贝叶斯连续时间贝叶斯网络框架，用于从纵向电子健康记录中学习疾病依赖关系网络。通过引入阶数依赖收缩先验控制高阶交互参数增长，并在模拟研究中比较了四种稀疏先验，其中spike-and-slab先验表现最优。在UK Biobank数据中，模型识别出以糖尿病为核心的心脏代谢簇和连接呼吸系统与过敏性疾病的炎症簇两大疾病模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重长期病症通过疾病间复杂的、时变的交互作用发展而来，但现有方法难以同时对疾病进展、多病共患网络和高维风险因素进行联合建模。需要一种能够从纵向EHR数据中学习有向疾病依赖网络、且能有效控制参数组合爆炸问题的方法。

Method: 提出结构化贝叶斯连续时间贝叶斯网络(CTBN)框架：(1)允许疾病转移强度依赖于已有疾病状态、成对疾病交互和外源性协变量；(2)引入阶数依赖的收缩先验，对高阶交互效应施加递增惩罚，同时保留临床可解释的主效应；(3)比较四种稀疏诱导先验——spike-and-slab、结构化正态、贝叶斯LASSO和正则化马蹄先验，通过广泛模拟研究评估网络恢复、变量选择和假发现控制能力。

Result: 模拟研究中，spike-and-slab先验在网络恢复、变量选择准确性和假发现控制方面均表现最佳，连续收缩先验在硬变量选择任务中效果较差。在UK Biobank的33,558名参与者数据中，spike-and-slab模型识别出两个主导疾病模块：以糖尿病为核心的心脏代谢簇，以及连接呼吸系统与特应性疾病的炎症簇。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化贝叶斯CTBN框架能够有效从纵向EHR数据中推断疾病依赖网络，spike-and-slab先验在变量选择方面优于连续收缩先验。该方法揭示了多重长期病症中具有临床意义的疾病模块化结构，为理解多病共患的进展路径提供了新工具。

Abstract: Multiple long-term conditions (MLTCs) arise through complex, time-dependent interactions among diseases, yet existing methods often struggle to jointly model disease progression, multimorbidity networks, and high-dimensional risk factors. We propose a structured Bayesian continuous-time Bayesian network (CTBN) framework for learning directed disease-dependency networks from longitudinal electronic health records. The model allows disease transition intensities to depend on existing conditions, pairwise disease interactions, and exogenous covariates. To control the combinatorial growth of interaction parameters, we introduce order-dependent shrinkage priors that increasingly penalise higher-order effects while preserving clinically interpretable main effects. We compare four sparsity-inducing priors, spike-and-slab, structured normal, Bayesian LASSO, and regularised horseshoe through extensive simulation studies. Across multiple data-generating scenarios, the spike-and-slab prior achieved the best network recovery, variable-selection accuracy, and false-discovery control, while continuous shrinkage priors were less effective for hard variable selection. The proposed framework was applied to UK Biobank primary care records, focusing on data from 33,558 participants who were free of the ten selected most prevalent conditions at age 40 and who subsequently developed at least one of these conditions during the follow-up period. The selected spike-and-slab model identified two dominant disease modules: a cardiometabolic cluster centred on diabetes and an inflammatory cluster linking respiratory and atopic conditions.

</details>


### [262] [Lancaster copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01558)
*Angelo Efoevi Koudou,Yves I. Ngounou Bakam,Denys Pommeret*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出Lancaster Copula——一种基于正交展开的新型Copula族，推导其无穷级数表示，研究截断性质，并通过数值实验证明低阶截断即可实现高精度近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引入基于连续Lancaster概率的正交展开构建的新型Copula族，以解决传统Copula在灵活建模和高维扩展方面的局限性。

Method: 基于连续Lancaster概率的正交展开构建Lancaster Copula；推导Copula及其密度的无穷级数表示；研究截断效应；通过数值实验验证低阶截断的近似精度。

Result: 推导出Lancaster Copula及其密度的无穷级数表示；截断效应研究显示低阶截断即可提供精确近似，数值实验验证了该结论。

Conclusion: Lancaster Copula作为一种基于正交展开的新型Copula族，低阶截断即可实现高精度近似，具有良好的实用性和计算效率。

Abstract: We introduce a new copula class, called Lancaster copulas, built from orthogonal expansions of continuous Lancaster probabilities. We derive infinite-series representations for the copula and its density, study truncation effects, and show in numerical experiments that low-order truncations already provide accurate approximation.

</details>


### [263] [From Subgroups to Population Composition: A Transportability Approach to Effect Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01717)
*Michael Cheung,Candus Shi,Kara E Rudolph,Valérie Garès,Caroline A Thompson,Tarik Benmarhnia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的异构性分析概念框架，通过将人群层面的暴露效应建模为效应修饰因子分布的函数，来识别不同人群中效应修饰因子构成如何影响暴露效应。方法包括三步：选择混杂因子和效应修饰因子、使用可移植性方法估计不同效应修饰因子流行率下的假设人群效应、以及将估计效应作为流行率的函数进行建模。该方法可量化效应修饰因子流行率变化对人群效应的影响，并对不同人群特征进行排序，以识别与差异化脆弱性最相关的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异质性分析方法依赖参数回归和预设亚组，难以捕捉复杂的效应修饰模式；而近期数据自适应方法虽能改善高维异质性效应预测，但增加了方法学复杂性，且对异质性的关键驱动因素洞察有限。因此需要一种新的概念性方法，能够在保持可解释性的同时，系统刻画人群组成差异如何影响暴露效应。

Method: 提出三步框架：(1) 基于先验知识选择混杂因子和效应修饰因子（也可使用数据自适应方法学习效应修饰因子）；(2) 利用可移植性（transportability）方法，估计具有不同效应修饰因子流行率的假设人群中的暴露效应；(3) 将估计的效应作为流行率的函数进行建模，绘制人群层面的效应曲面。输出包括：效应修饰因子流行率增加所导致的人群暴露效应变化量，以及跨多个效应修饰因子和流行率的效应估计排序。

Result: 利用人口与健康调查（DHS）数据，将该方法应用于干旱对儿童生长迟缓的异质性效应分析。开发了Shiny交互式应用，支持在任意场景中实现该方法。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种直观、可解释的框架来理解人群层面暴露效应的异质性来源，通过对效应修饰因子分布的建模，能够识别对差异化脆弱性贡献最大的群体特征，为干预优先级设定、成本效益分析和可移植性应用提供决策依据。

Abstract: Identifying heterogeneous populations across which exposure effects vary is essential for transportability applications, cost-benefit analyses, and intervention prioritization. Traditional methods for heterogeneity analyses rely on parametric regression with prespecified subgroups, which may fail to capture complex patterns of effect modification. While recent data-adaptive methods improve high-dimensional heterogeneous effect prediction, they add methodological complexity to analyses and may offer limited insight into key drivers of heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose a novel, conceptual approach for heterogeneity analyses that considers how exposure effects would differ in populations with different compositions by modeling the population-level effect surface as a function of the distribution of effect modifiers. The approach consists of three steps: i) selecting confounders and effect modifiers based on prior knowledge (or alternatively using data-adaptive methods to learn effect modifiers), ii) estimating exposure effects in hypothetical populations with different effect modifier prevalences using transportability methods, and iii) modeling the estimated effects as a function of prevalence values. This approach provides two types of outputs: estimation of the change in the population-level exposure effects attributable to increases in effect modifier prevalence and ranking of effect estimates across multiple effect modifiers and prevalences to identify population characteristics most strongly associated with differential vulnerability. We demonstrate the approach using Demographic and Health Surveys data to examine heterogeneous effects of drought on child stunting and provide a Shiny application to implement this approach in any setting.

</details>


### [264] [Pattern-Calibrated Multimodal Prediction under Blockwise Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01821)
*Junhan Yu,Kejian Zhang,Doudou Zhou,Guojun Zhu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MOSAIC 是一种针对多模态数据中分块缺失的模式校准框架，通过学习共享与模态特定表示，并利用与目标模式重叠的可用表示进行预测，再通过目标模式数据估计校准间隙，从而在不同缺失模式间实现信息借用的同时避免预测规则的塌缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态缺失处理方法（如插补、零填充、单一池化预测器）在不同缺失模式间借用信息时，容易混合模式特定的预测规则，导致预测性能下降。本文关注预定义观测模态模式下的预测问题，希望在借用跨模式信息的同时保持各模式预测规则的独立性。

Method: 提出 MOSAIC 框架：(1) 学习共享表示和模态特定表示；(2) 利用与目标模式重叠的可用表示训练第一阶段预测器；(3) 利用目标模式数据估计并校正校准间隙。理论分析建立非渐近误差界，将误差分解为重叠有效样本量、校准间隙和表示学习误差三项。

Result: 理论界明确了跨模式借用在何种条件下优于局部拟合，以及规则不匹配或表示学习误差如何控制改进幅度。模拟实验验证了表示恢复和目标模式校正效果；在 ICU 死亡率预测、情绪识别和青光眼分类三个应用中，MOSAIC 在目标模式样本有限或模式特定规则差异较大时均取得增益。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 在不塌缩模式特定预测规则的前提下实现了跨缺失模式的有效信息借用，理论和实验均表明其在有限目标样本和规则差异场景下的优越性。

Abstract: Blockwise missingness in multimodal data is usually treated as an incomplete-input problem. We instead focus on prediction for a prespecified observed-modality pattern, where the observed modality set determines the information on which the prediction rule can condition. A procedure that imputes missing modalities, zero-fills unobserved modalities, or trains a single pooled predictor may borrow information across patterns, but it can also mix pattern-specific prediction rules. We propose Multimodal Overlap-aware Shared-specific Alignment and Inter-pattern Calibration (MOSAIC), a pattern-calibrated framework for borrowing across missingness patterns without collapsing their prediction rules. MOSAIC learns shared and modality-specific representations, uses the available representations that overlap with the target pattern to fit a first-stage predictor, and then estimates the calibration gap from target-pattern data. We establish non-asymptotic bounds that decompose the error into overlap effective sample size, calibration gap, and representation-learning error, clarifying when cross-pattern borrowing improves over local fitting and when the improvement is controlled by rule mismatch or representation-learning error. Simulations examine representation recovery and target-pattern correction, and applications to ICU mortality prediction, emotion recognition, and glaucoma classification show gains when target-pattern samples are limited or pattern-specific rules differ.

</details>


### [265] [Beyond Laplace: Closed-form wrapped Gaussian posterior approximations on statistical manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01909)
*Marcelo Hartmann,Luu Hoang Phuc Hau,Anton Mallasto,Albert Kjøller Jacobsen,Georgios Arvanitidis,Søren Hauberg,Håvard Rue,Mark Girolami*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出利用对比函数（contrast functions）理论，在配备Fisher-Rao度量和先验分布几何的统计流形上，推导对数映射和指数映射的可计算近似。该方法无需计算Christoffel符号、曲率张量等几何量，也无需数值求解器，在保持对复杂后验几何（偏态、厚尾、狭窄高概率区域）建模能力的同时，计算速度比现有wrapped Gaussian方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉普拉斯近似虽计算高效，但其高斯形式局限于椭圆形状，无法捕捉偏态、厚尾、狭窄高概率区域等后验特征。现有wrapped Gaussian方法通过黎曼几何将切空间高斯分布推前至流形，虽具灵活性，但采样需通过指数映射求解测地线方程，密度评估依赖对数映射和Jacobi场，涉及昂贵的微分方程求解器及逆矩阵、Christoffel符号、曲率张量等几何量计算，构成严重计算瓶颈。

Method: 基于对比函数理论，在统计流形上构造指数映射和对数映射的易处理近似，该流形配备Fisher-Rao度量和先验分布几何。通过对比函数导出的 divergence 及其相关几何结构，绕过了显式计算Christoffel符号、曲率张量以及数值求解测地线方程的需求，从而大幅降低wrapped Gaussian逼近的计算代价。

Result: 在多种模型上的实证结果表明，所提出的近似方法能够捕捉复杂的后验几何结构（包括偏态、厚尾和非椭圆形态），同时计算速度比当前最先进的wrapped Gaussian逼近方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 基于对比函数的近似方法成功消除了wrapped Gaussian方法的主要计算瓶颈，在保持对复杂后验几何建模能力的同时实现了大幅加速，为贝叶斯推断中后验逼近提供了一种兼具灵活性与计算效率的新方案。

Abstract: In Bayesian statistics, the Laplace approximation provides a computationally efficient approximation to posterior distributions. However, its Gaussian form restricts it to elliptical shapes, limiting its ability to capture important posterior features such as skewness, heavy tails, and narrow high-probability regions. Recent work has addressed this limitation by exploiting Riemannian geometry to push forward Gaussian distributions from the tangent space to the manifold, referred to wrapped Gaussians. While offering greater flexibility, they introduce substantial computational challenges. Sampling requires solving geodesic equations through the exponential map and density evaluation additionally depends on the logarithmic map and Jacobi fields, involving costly differential equation solvers and geometric quantities such as inverse matrices, Christoffel symbols and curvature tensors. To overcome these limitations, we employ the theory of contrast functions to derive tractable approximations of the logarithmic and exponential maps on statistical manifolds endowed with the Fisher--Rao metric and the prior distribution geometry. The resulting methodology bypass the need to compute these geometric quantities and numerical solvers thereby removing the principal computational bottlenecks of existing wrapped Gaussian approaches. Empirical results across a range of models demonstrate that the proposed approximation captures complex posterior geometries while remaining orders of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art approximation.

</details>


### [266] [Moment-Based Selection of Multiresponse Linear Mixed-Effects Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01971)
*Yifan Chen,Yuedong Wang,Guo Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MOMENT 是一个基于矩的逐步框架，利用二阶交叉矩恒等式对混合效应模型中的随机效应协方差矩阵和固定效应系数进行选择与估计。其核心思想是通过在正半定约束下对协方差矩阵的对角线施加稀疏性，将随机效应选择转化为光滑约束凸优化问题，并用投影梯度下降高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多变量纵向数据分析中，现有的混合效应模型方法在处理随机效应协方差矩阵的选择时面临挑战：如何在保持协方差矩阵正半定性约束的同时实现稀疏选择，以获得可解释的模型结构。此外，当响应变量存在相关性时，独立单变量分析会损失效率。

Method: 提出 MOMENT 框架：(1) 利用二阶交叉矩恒等式构建目标函数；(2) 在正半定约束下对随机效应协方差矩阵的对角线施加稀疏诱导惩罚，将问题转化为光滑约束凸优化；(3) 采用投影梯度下降算法进行高效求解；(4) 在联合 sub-Weibull 误差假设下建立有限样本理论保证，包括随机效应和固定效应的选择一致性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，MOMENT 在整体上具有竞争力的表现，并且在响应变量相关时显著优于独立单变量分析。在血液透析数据集的应用中，该方法为多变量纵向数据提供了可解释且灵活的分析途径。

Conclusion: MOMENT 为混合效应模型中的随机效应协方差结构选择与固定效应估计提供了一个理论上完备、计算上高效的统一框架，尤其适用于响应相关的多变量纵向数据场景。

Abstract: We propose MOMENT (\textbf{MO}ment-Based \textbf{M}ixed-\textbf{E}ffects Selectio\textbf{N} and Es\textbf{T}imation), a stage-wise moment-based framework that exploits second-order cross-moment identities to select and estimate the random-effects covariance matrix and fixed-effects coefficients. By inducing sparsity through its diagonal under a positive semidefinite constraint, the random-effects selection problem reduces to a smooth constrained convex optimization problem that can be solved efficiently by projected gradient descent. We further establish finite-sample theoretical guarantees for the proposed procedure, including random-effects selection consistency and fixed-effects selection consistency under joint sub-Weibull errors. Simulation studies show that MOMENT performs competitively overall and can substantially outperform separate univariate analyses when responses are correlated. An application to the hemodialysis dataset demonstrates that the proposed method yields an interpretable and flexible approach for multivariate longitudinal data.

</details>


### [267] [Plausibility: Exact inference in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01976)
*Stefan Böhringer,Jesse Swen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍了R包plausibility，该包基于Plausibility理论框架，可在广义参数族中进行精确推断，支持多种回归模型及惩罚回归模型（如glmnet），并具备良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统计推断方法在处理一般参数族时往往依赖渐近近似，缺乏精确推断的工具。作者旨在将Plausibility理论框架实现为易用的R包，填补这一空白，使研究者能够在广泛的回归模型中进行精确推断。

Method: 基于Plausibility理论框架，通过面向对象的类机制设计R包架构，实现对多种回归模型的支持。该包能够与glmnet等惩罚回归模型对接，利用plausibility方法进行精确假设检验和置信区间构建。

Result: 通过多个R内置数据集进行实例演示，展示了plausibility包在实际数据分析中的应用效果。同时讨论了实现中的计算问题及其对实际数据分析的影响，验证了包的实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: plausibility包成功将Plausibility理论框架落地为实用工具，为回归模型的精确推断提供了统一、可扩展的解决方案，且能兼容惩罚回归方法，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Plausbility is a theoretical framework that allows to conduct exact inference in general parametric families. We introduce R-packages {\em plausibility} that implements this framework for a wide class of regression models. Plausibility can also be used to test penalized regression models such as estimated by package {\em glmnet}. We illustrate the package using a number of R data sets Through a class-based mechanism, the package can be easily extended. We illustrate and discuss computation aspects of the implementation and their impact on real-data analysis.

</details>


### [268] [Grouped Orthogonal Arrays from Orthogonal Arrays and Difference Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02027)
*Meixin Liu,Chunyan Wang,Guangzhou Chen,C. Devon Lin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了多种分组正交阵列的构造方法，大幅扩展了现有目录，生成了具有更多组数和更大组规模的灵活高效设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分组正交阵列源于计算机实验（含分组输入）和物理实验中忽略不同组间因子交互作用的设计需求。现有分组正交阵列的目录有限，无法满足对更灵活、更高效设计的日益增长需求。

Method: 本文开发了若干新的构造方法，通过这些构造方法系统性地生成新的分组正交阵列，重点在于实现更大的组数和组规模。

Result: 所提出的构造方法成功提供了大量新的分组正交阵列，显著扩展了现有目录，在组数和组规模方面均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该研究有效回应了分组因子实验设计中对灵活性和效率的需求，所构造的大规模分组正交阵列将为相关领域的实验设计提供更丰富的选择。

Abstract: Grouped orthogonal arrays were introduced to address experimental design problems arising in computer experiments with grouped inputs, as well as in physical experiments where interactions between factors from different groups are assumed to be negligible. Motivated by the growing need for flexible and efficient designs under such settings, this article develops several constructions to expand the existing catalogs of grouped orthogonal arrays. The proposed constructions provide a large collection of new grouped orthogonal arrays with significantly larger numbers of groups and group sizes.

</details>


### [269] [Sequential Structure-Sensitive Residual Diagnostics for PDE Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02101)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于e-process的结构敏感序贯诊断方法，用于检测反问题中模型误设。该方法通过组合多个空间残差模式专家，以财富积累的方式序贯更新，能在仅使用部分观测数据时即检测出传统残差范数检验遗漏的模型偏差，并在三个反问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平滑型反问题中，结构化的模型误差可能在观测空间中被衰减，导致残差范数低于预设阈值但残差呈现显著相干模式，从而使传统Morozov偏差准则等基于残差范数的诊断方法接受存在偏差的模型，最终导致参数估计和预测量出现实质性错误。

Method: 作者提出一种基于e-process（e-过程）的结构敏感序贯诊断框架：(1)构建一个由多个空间残差模式专家组成的投资组合，每个专家对应字典中的一种残差模式；(2)基于似然比财富（likelihood-ratio wealth）对观测进行序贯更新；(3)当聚合财富超过预设阈值时拒绝拟合模型，提供anytime-valid的I类错误控制。拒绝后，专家财富可归因至特定残差模式，为探索性模型修正提供依据。

Result: 在三个反问题（椭圆扩散、二维Stokes流、基于icepack社区有限元模型的冰川冰流反演）上，标准偏差检验接受了产生实质性错误量估计的误设拟合；结构敏感的批量检验利用全数据集能检测到这些失败；而e-process方法仅需部分观测即可更早检测到模型误设。

Conclusion: 结构敏感的诊断方法比传统残差范数检验更能有效检测平滑型反问题中的模型误设。所提出的e-process序贯诊断方法不仅能早期检测模型失败，还能通过专家财富归因识别残差模式，为模型修正提供方向性指导。

Abstract: Computational models in science and engineering are often assessed by checking whether the residual norm is consistent with the assumed noise level. This can be misleading in smoothing inverse problems: structured model errors may be attenuated in observation space, leaving residual magnitudes below practitioner discrepancy thresholds while coherent residual patterns remain. As a result, residual-norm diagnostics can accept fitted models that still give biased parameters, predictions, or quantities of interest. We propose a structure-sensitive sequential diagnostic based on e-processes. The method uses a portfolio of spatial residual-pattern experts, updates their likelihood-ratio wealth as observations are processed, and rejects the fitted model when the aggregate wealth crosses a prescribed threshold, giving anytime-valid type-I error control for a fixed fitted model. We compare the method with Morozov discrepancy checks, fixed-sample residual tests, and batch projection tests. Across three inverse problems (elliptic diffusion, two-dimensional Stokes flow, and a glaciological ice-stream inversion implemented in the community finite-element model icepack) we demonstrate how standard discrepancy checks accept misspecified fits that produce materially wrong quantities of interest. Structure-sensitive batch tests detect these failures using the full dataset, while the e-process detects them earlier from a fraction of the observations. After rejection, the expert wealth attributes the evidence to residual patterns in the chosen dictionary and provides a basis for exploratory model correction.

</details>


### [270] [Conformal Bayes for Two-Sided Censored Gaussian Regression under Label Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02173)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对双侧截尾高斯模型下标签偏移的共形贝叶斯方法，通过后验预测倾斜与加权共形校准相结合，在混合空间（边界原子+连续内部密度）中构建预测集，解决了截尾响应下标签偏移导致的非标准预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当响应变量被双侧截尾（低于L记为L，高于U记为U）时，观测尺度的预测分布呈现混合结构（边界原子+连续密度），传统的标签偏移校正方法不再适用，因为潜在空间的密度比无法直接迁移到截尾观测空间。

Method: 采用双侧Tobit高斯贝叶斯预测头，结合拉普拉斯后验近似。核心创新包括：(1) 潜在指数倾斜在截尾边界诱导出尾部平均的原子权重，内部区间保持密度比，形成包含两个原子比和一个内部密度比的三项混合观测空间校准权重；(2) 通过后验预测倾斜获得目标自适应的混合最高密度区域(HDR)，其归一化因子具有三项闭式解。

Result: 合成实验表明，加权倾斜共形贝叶斯能够恢复边际覆盖率，且预测集小于加权源得分校准方法；同时揭示了边际覆盖率与各分量（边界原子、内部观测）行为之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 通过组合后验预测倾斜与加权共形校准，该方法有效处理了截尾响应下的标签偏移问题，混合HDR预测集能根据截尾强度自适应退化为纯原子集，实现了覆盖率和效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Prediction under label shift becomes nonstandard when responses are censored. In a two-sided censored Gaussian model, latent values below $L$ and above $U$ are recorded at the boundary values, so the observed predictive distribution is mixed, with atoms at $L$ and $U$ and a continuous density on $(L,U)$. In this paper we develop conformal Bayes for this mixed-space setting by combining posterior predictive tilting with weighted conformal calibration. Under a two-sided Tobit Gaussian Bayesian prediction head with a Laplace posterior approximation, the tilted predictive distribution has left-atom, interior, and right-atom components, with a three-term closed-form normalizer. The resulting prediction set is a mixed highest density region that can combine boundary atoms with an interior interval and can reduce to atom-only sets under strong censoring. The main technical issue is that latent label shift does not directly give an ordinary density ratio on the observed censored scale. A latent exponential tilt induces tail-averaged atom weights at the censored boundaries, while the interior ratio remains density based. This yields a mixed observed-space calibration weight with two atom ratios and one interior density ratio. The weight corrects the calibration measure, while predictive tilting gives target-adapted mixed-HDR geometry. Synthetic experiments show that weighted tilted conformal Bayes restores marginal coverage with smaller sets than weighted source-score calibration, while revealing a trade-off between marginal coverage and component-wise behavior across atoms and interior observations.

</details>


### [271] [Goodness of Fit Tests Based on Joint Densities of Multiple Sample Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02285)
*Roman Guchenko*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出两类基于模拟置信集的拟合优度检验方法：第一类使用主成分顺序统计量的超矩形置信集，第二类使用基于k近邻的最高密度区域构造任意形状置信集。仿真表明这些方法比现有方法更具检验力，并讨论了双样本扩展和不同变换策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟合优度检验方法在高维统计量向量或非标准备择假设下检验力不足。作者旨在开发基于多个样本统计量联合分布的置信集检验框架，以提升对广泛备择假设的检验效能。

Method: （1）第一类：基于主成分分析的顺序统计量及相关统计量向量，构造超矩形置信集进行检验，并与经典检验、Zhang检验等进行比较。（2）第二类：采用k近邻方法检测最高密度区域（HDR），构造任意形状置信集，适用于更高维统计量向量，涵盖顺序统计量、经验分布函数值、矩及经典统计量组合。（3）扩展：通过置换检验实现双样本扩展，并与能量距离置换检验比较；探讨概率积分变换以外的其他变换（如映射至标准正态分布）。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提两类检验程序与现有方法相比具有竞争力，且通常检验力更高。在正态零分布下，第一主成分对应样本均值，第二主成分与方差的线性类比相关。基于k近邻的HDR方法在更高维统计量向量场景下优于传统核密度方法。

Conclusion: 基于模拟置信集的拟合优度检验框架灵活且强大，主成分方法和HDR方法各有优势，可适应不同维度和备择假设场景。将数据变换至存在强检验方法的分布（如标准正态）是一种有效策略。该方法可自然扩展至双样本问题。

Abstract: We propose goodness-of-fit tests based on simulated confidence sets for joint distributions of multiple sample statistics, focusing on absolutely continuous null distributions with known parameters. One class of tests uses hyperrectangular confidence sets for principal components of order statistics and related statistic vectors. Extending earlier work on horizontal and vertical confidence bands for cumulative distribution functions, these tests are compared with some classical, Zhang, and related graphical tests. Simulations show that the proposed procedures are competitive with, and often more powerful than, existing methods. We also study the geometry of principal-component-based statistics; under a normal null distribution, the first principal component corresponds to the sample mean, while the second is related to a linear analogue of variance.
  A second class of tests uses confidence sets of arbitrary shape constructed through highest density regions. Unlike earlier kernel-density-based approaches, we use a k-nearest-neighbor method for detecting highest density regions, which is better suited to higher-dimensional statistic vectors. We study tests based on order statistics, empirical distribution function values, moments, and combinations of classical goodness-of-fit statistics. The resulting procedures are powerful against a wide range of alternatives.
  We also outline a two-sample extension via permutation tests based on joint distributions of several statistics and compare moment-based versions with energy-distance permutation tests. Finally, we discuss transformations other than the probability integral transform, showing that mapping data to another target distribution, such as the standard normal, can be advantageous when powerful tests are available for that distribution.

</details>


### [272] [MATCH: Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses in Non-Euclidean Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02295)
*Leheng Cai,Xu Guo,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出MATCH方法，利用样本分割与独立随机乘子，在Fréchet回归框架下对非欧几里得数据进行全局显著性、部分显著性及模型充分性检验，解决了原假设下损失差一阶退化的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非欧几里得数据（如分布、SPD矩阵、球面数据）的Fréchet回归缺乏统一的显著性检验与模型设定检验框架。核心困难在于：原假设下无条件与条件Fréchet均值重合，导致留出损失差一阶退化，直接使用插件统计量会被冗余参数估计误差主导。

Method: 提出MATCH（乘子辅助条件假设检验）：(1) 样本分割后，在留出样本上构造损失差；(2) 引入独立随机乘子，将退化统计量提升为非退化高斯过程，无需残差或切空间坐标；(3) 扩展至交叉拟合检验与重复交叉拟合的p值合并以提升数据利用率和稳定性。

Result: 理论证明了渐近零分布有效性、固定备择下的一致性及局部功效。模拟实验涵盖分布响应、SPD矩阵响应和球面响应，验证了理论结果；实际应用分析了县级家庭收入分布和北大西洋热带气旋位置数据。

Conclusion: MATCH为Fréchet回归提供了统一、灵活的假设检验框架，解决了退化统计量带来的推断难题，在模拟与真实数据中均表现良好。

Abstract: We propose a new procedure MATCH (Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses) to test whether the non-Euclidean data match the target model, which is a general framework for significance and specification testing in Fréchet regression. MATCH covers global significance, partial significance, and the adequacy of global Fréchet regression, providing a unified way to compare unrestricted conditional Fréchet means with restricted alternatives. One of the key challenges is that the ordinary held-out loss difference is first-order degenerate under the null: the oracle losses coincide, and plug-in statistics is dominated by nuisance estimation error. MATCH uses sample splitting and independent random multipliers on held-out losses to create a nondegenerate Gaussian leading term without residuals or tangent-space coordinates. To improve data use and stability, we further develop cross-fitted tests and repeated cross-fitting with p-value merging. We establish asymptotic null validity, consistency under fixed alternatives, and local power guarantees. Simulations for distributional, symmetric positive-definite (SPD) matrix-valued, and spherical responses support the theoretical findings, and applications to county-level household income distributions and North Atlantic tropical-cyclone locations demonstrate the practical use of the proposed tests.

</details>


### [273] [Cross-Audit Projection for Model Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02328)
*Yijian Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文揭示了K折交叉验证在二分类模型风险预测中的反直觉缺陷——其估计类特定风险时甚至不如经验估计量，并提出了结合重采样与渐进理论校正的交叉审计投影（CAP）方法，实现一阶等价于经验风险且二阶渐进无偏的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K折交叉验证被广泛认为能缓解经验风险的高估乐观性，但作者通过数值实验发现在二分类经验风险最小化场景下，K折CV估计类特定风险时表现可能比经验估计量更差。这一反直觉现象缺乏理论解释，需要深入分析其渐进性质并提出更可靠的模型风险预测方法。

Method: 首先进行高阶渐进分析，揭示K折CV收敛速率更慢的原因，以及经验估计量存在二阶渐进偏差（导致过度乐观）。基于此提出两步法CAP：第一步"交叉审计"采用与K折CV相同的重采样方案在子样本中估计过度乐观程度；第二步"投影"利用渐进理论对经验风险进行样本量缩减的偏差校正。同时构建了相应的推断程序。

Result: CAP估计量一阶渐进等价于经验风险，同时实现了二阶渐进无偏性。模拟研究验证了理论优势并展示了良好的有限样本表现，乳腺癌检测的实际应用进一步说明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: K折CV并非总是可靠的模型风险预测工具，尤其在二分类的类特定风险估计中存在收敛速率慢的问题。CAP方法通过交叉审计与渐进投影的结合，在保持一阶效率的同时消除了二阶偏差，为基于训练数据的模型风险预测提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: For training-data-based model risk prediction, $K$-fold cross-validation~(CV) is widely used to mitigate the well-known over-optimism of the empirical risk and is often regarded as reliable. However, for binary classification via empirical risk minimization, our numerical studies reveal a surprising phenomenon: $K$-fold CV may perform poorly in estimating class-specific risks, even worse than the empirical estimator. We perform a higher-order asymptotic analysis showing that $K$-fold CV may converge at a slower rate, whereas the empirical estimator exhibits a second-order asymptotic bias that explains its over-optimism. These findings motivate a novel two-step procedure for model risk prediction, termed cross-audit projection (CAP). The cross-audit step adopts the same resampling scheme as $K$-fold CV to estimate over-optimism in subsamples, while the asymptotic-theory-informed projection step adjusts for the reduced sample size in bias correction of the empirical risk. The resulting CAP estimator is first-order asymptotically equivalent to the empirical risk while achieving second-order asymptotic unbiasedness. An accompanying inference procedure is also developed. Simulation studies support theoretical advantages of CAP and demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance. An application to breast cancer detection further illustrates the proposed method.

</details>


### [274] [Merging of Bayes and quasi-Bayes empirical Bayes procedures for Poisson compound decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02340)
*Stefano Favaro,Sandra Fortini*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究泊松复合决策问题中的g-modeling方法，比较了基于Dirichlet过程后验的贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略与基于Newton算法的准贝叶斯策略。作者建立了两种估计下边际概率质量函数的浓度速率，并将其转化为遗憾（regret）的衰减速率，证明了两种策略的频率学派合并结果。数值实验表明准贝叶斯策略在精度上可与贝叶斯策略媲美，但计算成本显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松复合决策问题是统计学中的经典问题，常用经验贝叶斯方法在混合模型下估计泊松均值。基于Newton算法的准贝叶斯策略虽然计算上很有吸引力，但其与基于Dirichlet过程后验的贝叶斯策略之间的关系缺乏理论论证，需要填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在带有真实（oracle）混合分布的泊松混合模型下：1) 建立贝叶斯和准贝叶斯估计所诱导的边际概率质量函数的浓度速率（concentration rates）；2) 将这些速率转化为对应遗憾（regret，即超额贝叶斯风险）的衰减速率；3) 利用上述结果证明贝叶斯与准贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略之间的频率学派合并（frequentist merging）结果；4) 将分析扩展至多维泊松复合决策问题；5) 通过合成数据的数值实验进行验证。

Result: 理论上证明了贝叶斯与准贝叶斯策略之间的频率学派合并结果，给出了浓度速率和遗憾衰减速率的具体刻画。数值实验表明，准贝叶斯策略在达到与贝叶斯策略可比精度的同时，所需计算资源大幅减少，在多维情形下优势尤为突出。

Conclusion: 准贝叶斯策略（基于Newton算法）是贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略（基于Dirichlet过程后验）的一种计算高效且理论上有保障的替代方案，两者在频率学派意义下具有合并性质，且准贝叶斯方法在多维泊松复合决策问题中具有显著的计算优势。

Abstract: The Poisson compound decision problem is a long-standing problem in statistics, in which empirical Bayes methods are used to estimate Poisson means under a mixture model. We study this problem from the viewpoint of $g$-modeling, comparing two nonparametric strategies for estimating the unknown mixing distribution: a Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on the Dirichlet process posterior, and a quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on Newton's algorithm. The latter is computationally attractive, but its relationship with the Bayesian strategy requires theoretical justification. Under a Poisson mixture model with a ``true'', or oracle, mixing distribution, we establish concentration rates for the marginal probability mass functions induced by the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian estimates. These rates are then translated into rates of decay for the corresponding regrets, interpreted as excess Bayes risks, and used to prove a frequentist merging result between the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategies. We also extend the analysis to the multidimensional Poisson compound decision problem. Numerical experiments on synthetic data illustrate that the quasi-Bayesian strategy achieves accuracy comparable to the Bayesian strategy, while requiring substantially fewer computational resources, especially in the multidimensional setting.

</details>


### [275] [Inference for Group Interaction Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02385)
*Jiawei Fu,Cyrus Samii,Ye Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为"群体互动实验"（个体随机分配到组后在不同组层面处理条件下互动）建立了基于设计的推断框架。作者在稀疏抽样渐近机制下，刻画了不同场景（固定/随机分组、是否存在干扰）中的因果目标量和适配的推断策略，并利用一种新颖的耦合策略推导了常用估计量的渐近分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的实验设计涉及个体随机分组后在组内互动并接受组层面处理，但此类"群体互动实验"的基于设计的推断理论尚不完善。现有方法未能系统处理不同场景（固定组/随机组、有无干扰效应）下的因果识别与推断问题。

Method: 作者在稀疏抽样渐近框架下，针对固定分组和随机分组、潜在结果是否受他人分组影响（即是否存在干扰）四种场景，分别刻画设计所瞄准的因果目标量及适配推断策略。文中提出一种新颖的耦合策略（coupling strategy），用于推导常用估计量的渐近分布。

Result: 当存在干扰时，聚类稳健推断保持一致且能处理多种来源的依赖性，对边际化暴露效应给出有效推断；当不存在干扰且随机分组时，设计退化为个体随机实验，个体层面的异方差稳健推断足以估计平均处理效应。

Conclusion: 群体互动实验的推断应根据分组方式与干扰存在与否选择相应策略。所提出的耦合策略为其他复杂实验的基于设计推断提供了方法论工具。

Abstract: A common experimental research design is one in which individuals are randomly allocated into groups that then interact under different group-level treatment conditions. We develop design-based inference for such "group interaction" experiments, covering scenarios in which groups are either fixed or randomly formed and in which potential outcomes are either fixed relative to others' group assignments or subject to interference. For each scenario, we characterize the causal estimand that the design targets and the inferential strategy appropriate to it. Working in a sparse-sampling asymptotic regime, we show that cluster-robust inference remains consistent and accounts for dependencies from various sources when interference is present, delivering valid inference on marginalized exposure effects. When interference is absent and groups are formed randomly, the design reduces to an individually randomized experiment, and individual-level heteroskedasticity-robust inference suffices for the average treatment effect. Our results on the asymptotic distribution of commonly used estimators rely on a novel coupling strategy that may be useful for design-based inference in other complex experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一个模块化的神经符号框架，通过在反事实解释生成过程中引入符号推理层来施加领域约束，从而产生既有效又符合现实可行性的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽然能生成改变预测的替代方案，但因缺乏纳入领域知识和干预约束的显式机制，常产生不切实际或不可行的建议。需要一种能将数据驱动预测与可解释的符号推理相结合的方法。

Method: 提出PACE框架，将预测与推理分离为两个组件：用于分类的神经预测模型（多层感知机），以及使用Answer Set Programming（ASP）规则编码可行修改的符号推理层。ASP规则用于约束教育、职业、工时等可修改属性，同时保护不可变属性。该方法是模型无关的。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，引入符号约束可在反事实有效性与合理性之间取得更好的权衡，生成的反事实解释更好地满足领域特定的可行性要求。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在可解释AI中具有潜力，能够生成透明且可行性感知的反事实解释，为需要现实决策支持的领域提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [277] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个受约束的编码代理工作流，用于联邦学习(FL)算法配方的自动化搜索。在五个医疗保健跨中心FLamby任务和六个LEAF数据集上评估，结果表明部分增益源于FL配方改进，而其他增益来自固定表面调参或单次运行伪影。该工作贡献在于展示了如何将代理生成的候选方案分离为可复现的FL机制、固定表面调参效应和选择性单次运行伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FL研究涉及大量微小但关键的算法选择（优化器变体、服务器聚合规则、本地训练调度、归一化、正则化、模型架构等），手动探索代价高昂，且当候选变更会改变训练或评估路径时难以公平比较。因此需要一种自动化搜索方法来解决这一组合爆炸问题。

Method: 提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个受约束的编码代理工作流。代理可以提出并实现候选训练算法（包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标函数和注册模型变体），同时由任务配置文件(profile)固定变异表面、计算预算、通信契约和最终模型评估。每次搜索活动记录候选分数、运行时间、编辑文件、产物和失败状态。

Result: 在五个医疗保健跨中心FLamby任务和五个固定LEAF数据集加LEAF合成任务的分组客户端配置上进行了五种子重复评估。AFR在四个FLamby任务和六个LEAF配置中的五个上取得增益，同时也暴露了种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。等预算对照实验表明，若干增益对应于FL配方变更，而其他改进可通过固定表面标量控制复现，或在重复/保留评估下失败。

Conclusion: 混合结果是本工作的核心贡献：展示了如何将代理生成的候选方案分离为可复现的FL机制、固定表面调参效应和选择性单次运行伪影三类，为FL算法的自动化搜索提供了重要的方法论框架和分析视角。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [278] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一个全新的小型语言模型架构，独立于现有模型家族，提出了五种创新组件：螺旋旋转位置编码(SRPE)、门控跨层注意力(GCLA)、自适应令牌合并(ATM)、双流前馈网络(DSFF)和改进的 RMS 归一化(WiolaRMSNorm)，发布了四种规模并兼容 HuggingFace 生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种完全原创、与现有模型家族（GPT、LLaMA、Mistral、Falcon 等）无结构亲缘关系的小型语言模型架构，从第一性原理出发探索新的设计空间，避免对现有架构的增量式改进。

Method: 提出五项独立创新：(1) SRPE 将位置编码嵌入三维螺旋流形，融合绝对、相对和层级位置信号；(2) GCLA 让每层解码器对前两层的压缩摘要进行软交叉注意力访问；(3) ATM 在网络中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻 token，降低注意力复杂度；(4) DSFF 用双并行流替代传统 MLP，通过可学习的逐维门控融合；(5) WiolaRMSNorm 引入逐维可学习偏移向量防止表征塌缩。发布了 120M、360M、700M、1.5B 四种参数规模。

Result: 提供了完整的数学推导、架构框图、复杂度分析，并与 GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral 进行了系统性对比。22 项架构单元测试全部通过，完全兼容 HuggingFace Transformers 生态。

Conclusion: Wiola 证明可以从第一性原理出发构建完全原创的 SLM 架构，且五个独立创新组件协同工作良好，具备实用性和生态兼容性。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon.
