Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [279] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，通过自底向上的方式对大型代码库进行摘要，利用摘要智能体、关键词提取智能体、质量保证智能体协同工作，在语义一致性和关键词覆盖率上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方案通常仅依赖单一语言模型，将源代码视为扁平文本，未能充分利用代码库中的层次结构和丰富的依赖关系，尤其在面对混淆结构和文档不完整的复杂代码库时效果不佳。

Method: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，采用自底向上的方式工作：摘要智能体负责生成稳健摘要，关键词提取智能体主动从子文件夹中识别关键信息，质量保证智能体迭代优化输出以提高可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上评估，Agent4cs在所有文件夹层级的语义一致性上平均提升8%；在真实世界数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: 多智能体协同的自底向上摘要策略能有效利用代码库的层次结构信息，显著提升大型代码库摘要的质量和完整性。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [280] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规服务会话保持在低成本路径，而将存在操作耦合的会话升级至包含冲突感知通信和写触发反思的高阶工作流，从而在保持常规服务效率的同时，针对涉及后端写入的复杂请求集中施加控制与安全措施。在零售和航空领域的评测中，该方法在操作冲突场景下持续提升可靠性，且增益并非来自无差别的交互扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服智能体正从对话界面转向操作执行角色，需要执行退款、取消、订单修改等后端写入操作。这带来了服务控制难题：企业必须在保持常规服务快速低摩擦的同时，防止客户指令、策略约束、企业记录与后端写入交互时产生操作错误。现有方案往往对所有会话统一施加额外控制，缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出"难度路由服务控制架构"（difficulty-routed service-control architecture）。核心组件包括：（1）轻量级路由器，判断会话是否包含操作耦合，将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径；（2）升级路径采用冲突感知通信（conflict-aware communication）和写触发反思（write-triggered reconsideration）机制，在关键后端写入前集中进行审慎推理和安全保障，而非在所有服务会话上统一施加额外控制。

Result: 在τ²-bench的零售和航空任务上进行了人工验证评估。零售场景中，该方法在存在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升可靠性；路由证据表明更强的控制被导向冲突请求而非全面施加于常规请求；对话和工具使用分析显示增益并非来自无差别交互扩展或更宽的工具链，而是来自证据收集、写入分离和写前反思。航空场景的结果将相同的服务控制逻辑成功扩展到预订操作领域。

Conclusion: 该架构通过选择性路由机制，在保持常规服务效率的同时，有效降低了操作耦合场景下的后端写入错误风险，为自主客服智能体从对话角色向操作角色的安全转型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [281] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityNeuro 是一种无需数据的对比权重操控方法，通过在权重空间而非激活空间进行操控，显著提升大语言模型的发散思维能力，并在多项测试中降低了模式坍缩现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在开放性问题中倾向于生成相似回复，即"人工蜂巢效应"，严重制约了其发散思维能力。现有方法通常依赖行为数据或微调，缺乏一种无需数据、可泛化至未知任务的轻量级增强方案。

Method: 提出 CreativityNeuro 方法，采用对比权重操控（contrastive weight steering），在权重空间直接操作模型参数以增强发散思维，无需行为数据、重新训练或基于梯度的微调。同时对比了激活操控（activation steering）方法。

Result: 在发散联想任务（DAT）上，人类百分位提升高达14个点；在替代用途测试（AUT）和 Task Task 的大规模人类评估（N=720）中，原创性、惊喜度和创造力均显著提升，且三项任务均表现出模式坍缩的显著降低。激活操控在 DAT 上表现相当，但无法迁移至 AUT 和 Task Task，凸显了权重空间操控的泛化优势。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro 能有效增强 LLM 的发散思维能力并缓解模式坍缩，且具备无需数据、无需微调、易于部署的特点，为提升模型在创意领域的表现提供了简洁高效的路径。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [282] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将混合专家（MoE）扩散语言模型 DiffusionGemma-26B 适配到医学视觉问答任务，在与同规模自回归模型 Gemma-4-26B 的对比中，扩散模型在各项指标上匹配或超越自回归模型，解码速度快3.5-4.4倍，且具备自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，非常适合放射报告等真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学基础模型几乎全部采用自回归架构，而扩散语言模型在通用文本生成中已展现竞争力。作者旨在探索扩散语言模型在医学领域的潜力，验证其是否能匹配甚至超越自回归模型，同时利用扩散模型双向去噪带来的独特"任意顺序填充"能力，解决放射报告因临床医生和机构差异导致的简略或不一致问题。

Method: 采用混合专家扩散语言模型 DiffusionGemma-26B，以相同的 LoRA 微调配方在其同规模自回归对照模型 Gemma-4-26B 上进行微调。在医学视觉问答数据集上评估，使用对冗长偏差鲁棒的 LLM 评判器进行打分。同时展示了扩散模型特有的任意顺序填充功能：放射科医生可修正报告片段，模型自动填充中间文本。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学 VQA 数据集上匹配或超越自回归模型；微调后的模型（激活参数 3.8B）性能可与前沿视觉语言模型竞争；解码速度比自回归模型快 3.5-4.4 倍；具备自回归所缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，适用于真实放射报告场景。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学领域不仅性能与自回归模型持平甚至更优，解码更快，还天然支持任意顺序文本填充。这为构建更灵活、更适合临床工作流的医学基础模型开辟了新方向，挑战了医学 AI 领域对自回归架构的惯性依赖。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [283] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了强化学习与可验证奖励(RLVR)在缩小大语言模型通用训练目标与企业SaaS API精确调用需求之间差距的效果。通过在模拟Jira和Confluence API的五个合成环境中进行GRPO训练，研究发现RL策略将平均奖励从0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00，但同时也指出人工设计可验证奖励难以规模化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型以预测下一个token为训练目标，而非在特定API内部执行操作。在企业SaaS工作流（如Jira、Confluence）中，成功意味着以正确顺序、使用正确嵌套参数调用正确端点。这种目标错位表现为静默失败：遗漏必填字段、幻觉生成工具、单次读取后过早终止。作者旨在探究直接应用于目标环境的RLVR是否能弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建五个模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API的合成环境，以schema级别的保真度进行仿真。奖励完全从工具调用轨迹计算得出，无需实时API、学习型评判模型或人工标注。使用这些奖励检查器对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B进行评分提示，并应用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行强化学习训练。

Result: 在四个奖励非退化的场景中，RL训练策略将平均奖励从4B基线的0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00。最大单次增益出现在Confluence页面创建任务上（从0.35提升至1.00）。然而，第五个场景（ticket-transition）的奖励函数呈饱和形态，提示版4B模型已取得满分。

Conclusion: 该研究定位为面向垂直领域企业API的结果优化小模型的初步探索。作者强调了两项局限：其一，手工设计可验证奖励无法规模化至超出本文报告的少量端点；其二，其中一个场景的奖励饱和问题使得提升空间不存在。该工作为后续在特定企业API场景中应用RLVR提供了概念验证。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [284] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedAgentBench-v3修复了前两版中41.7%的"静默完成天花板"问题，将其降至8.9%。在Qwen3-8B上训练揭示了两个结构性障碍：能力天花板（20种任务类型中10种基础性能为0%）和格式知识障碍（3种类型需要探索无法发现的精确临床编码）。纯RL仅达18.2% pass@1，而基于规则的SFT达34.1%，差距完全归因于这两类障碍。论文提出决策/格式知识/查找三分法来预测RL可学习性并指导修复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务天然适合通过真实世界反馈进行强化学习，因为临床专家可将决策逻辑编码为验证器，无需逐轮标注即可对无限rollout进行评分。然而，现有基准存在严重缺陷（静默完成天花板），且RL在该领域的适用性缺乏系统分析。

Method: 首先审计MedAgentBench v1/v2发现静默完成天花板问题；然后构建MAB-v3（508个任务，天花板降至8.9%）；在Qwen3-8B上训练揭示能力天花板和格式知识障碍；提出决策/格式知识/查找三分法分类体系预测RL可学习性。

Result: MAB-v3将静默完成天花板从41.7%降至8.9%。纯RL仅达18.2% pass@1，基于规则的SFT达34.1%，差距15.9个百分点。10/20任务类型基础性能为0%（能力天花板），3/20类型需要精确临床编码且无法通过探索发现（格式知识障碍）。

Conclusion: RL在临床协议执行中的15.9 pp性能差距完全归因于能力天花板和格式知识障碍。解决策略为：用SFT注入不可发现的编码知识，再用RL学习条件决策逻辑。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [285] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PMD将跨episode的程序性知识提炼为可复用的记忆，并在训练中蒸馏进策略权重，实现推理时无需记忆的模型，在科学知识和代码基准上显著优于SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法（如SDPO）仅利用episode级别的验证信号更新策略，丢弃了rollout中丰富的程序性信息。跨episode的重复模式（哪些策略持续通过验证、哪些失败模式反复出现）无法被episode-local更新所捕获，造成学习效率低下。

Method: 提出PMD方法，在三个抽象层次组织程序性记忆：（1）原始轨迹；（2）自我反思的策略与经验教训；（3）跨问题反复出现的高层行为模式。这些均从模型自身轨迹中在线提取。核心机制是"记忆条件化的自我教师"——利用累积经验监督学生自身的rollout，使学生逐步将程序性知识内化至参数中。设计原则是协同进化：策略生成rollout更新记忆，记忆塑造监督信号更新策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相比SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL上提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH上提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验表明，冻结记忆或冻结策略均导致PMD在SCIKNOWEVAL各领域下降超10%，验证了协同进化的关键作用。

Conclusion: PMD通过跨episode程序性记忆的构建与蒸馏，有效捕获并内化了RLVR中被忽视的重复模式信号，协同进化机制是其性能增益的核心驱动力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [286] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示AI智能体可通过赋予不同"人设"重现人类研究者的分析变异，系统性地产生与信念一致的分歧结论。作者提出m值（多元宇宙值）和"智能体自助法"（Agentic Bootstrap），将可辩护分析路径的分布作为科学可信度的评判标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究中，同一数据因分析选择不同可得出截然相反的结论，但这些"分叉路径"难以被直接观测。传统同行评审难以识别这类选择性报告问题，因为每项单独分析在方法论上都可辩护。作者旨在将这一隐性过程显性化，并量化分析选择带来的不确定性。

Method: （1）跨四个高 stakes 领域，为AI智能体分配不同"人设"（personas），令其从相同数据和问题出发进行分析，观察结论分歧；（2）在42个人类团队分析同一移民数据的研究中，用AI智能体复现人类意识形态导致的分析差距（再现率72%）；（3）对AI报告进行AI评审和人类专家评审（通过率分别为86%和78%）；（4）提出m值——分析路径产生至少同等极端结论的概率；（5）提出Agentic Bootstrap方法，利用AI智能体采样合理分析路径以估计m值。

Result: （1）仅改变AI人设即可产生系统性对立结论，且结论与人设信念一致；（2）AI智能体重现了72%的人类意识形态差距；（3）尽管结论对立，86%的AI报告通过AI评审，78%通过多数人类专家评审；（4）在人类移民研究中，13.5%的已发表分析落在分析空间最极端的5%区域（m<0.05）。

Conclusion: 核心挑战往往并非分析"有缺陷"，而是从庞大方法论可辩护空间中进行的"选择性探索与报告"。AI智能体可能因低成本、可规模化加剧这一问题。科学证据不应仅由单一报告分析评判，还应考察其在可合理报告的分析分布中所处位置。Agentic Bootstrap使该分布可观测，并将其转化为科学可信度的评判标准。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [287] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus 是一个用于设计和评估用户参与式智能体权限管理的游乐场系统，包含模块化智能体核心和自动化评估框架。通过实现六种权限助手并在多场景下评估，发现用户输入对安全隐私至关重要、AI增强决策可降低认知负担，且没有单一设计在所有场景下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主执行工具调用的AI智能体引发了权限管理的紧迫问题：用户应当扮演什么角色、能够扮演什么角色。尽管已有许多方案被提出，但用户在智能体权限管理中的角色仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 Janus 系统，包含两个组件：(1) Janus-Core，一个模块化智能体系统，支持多种权限管理设计；(2) Janus-Harness，一个自动化评估框架。基于识别用户参与关键设计轴的概念模型，在设计空间中实现了六种权限助手，并在三种场景和三种合成响应者下进行评估。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 用户输入至关重要，能显著增强隐私和安全性；(2) AI增强用户决策有助于降低认知负荷；(3) 系统设计必须考虑包括权限疲劳在内的真实用户行为。没有任何单一设计在所有场景下均表现最优。

Conclusion: 需要在智能体系统中采用更具原则性、上下文感知的方法来部署权限助手。Janus 已公开发布，以支持未来对此方向的研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [288] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Semi-CoT（半监督思维链学习）框架，利用无标注问题生成伪推理链，通过语义熵筛选高质量伪思维链，将其作为半监督信号训练模型。在多个数学推理数据集上的初步实验表明，熵门控能筛选出高精度伪链（91%-100%），但在不同数据集上迁移效果不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链方法主要将推理链用作推理时的提示，生成的大量推理轨迹很少被复用为半监督学习信号。作者希望将 CoT 从仅用于推理时精炼的自训练视角，扩展到半监督伪监督学习，从而更充分地利用无标注数据。

Method: 提出 Semi-CoT 框架：(1) 对每个无标注问题采样多条伪思维链；(2) 估计答案级别的语义熵（semantic entropy）；(3) 通过熵门控筛选低熵（即高置信度、高一致性）的推理链作为可靠的伪 CoT 示范；(4) 将筛选后的伪推理链作为半监督信号用于学生模型训练。

Result: 在 AQuA、SVAMP、GSM8K 和 MultiArith 四个数学推理数据集上的初步实验表明：熵门控筛选的伪答案精度在 91.36% 到 100% 之间。Semi-CoT 在 SVAMP 和 GSM8K 上带来小幅提升，AQuA 出现负迁移，MultiArith 达到性能天花板。

Conclusion: 无标注问题确实可以提供可靠的伪推理信号，但有效利用这些信号仍需更强的示范选择策略或更优的学生训练方法。半监督思维链学习是一个有前景但尚需进一步研究的方向。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling. These results suggest that unlabeled questions can provide reliable pseudo reasoning signals, but their effective use still requires stronger demonstration selection or student training.

</details>


### [289] [OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01531)
*David Courtis,Wenhao Li,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPINE-World 是一个通过在线交互学习对象中心化程序化世界模型的LLM智能体。它耦合两个协作代理（环境执行与代码合成），利用重放验证、基于模型的规划以及本体论误差驱动的探索，在ARC-AGI-3基准上无需逐游戏训练即可解决25个游戏中的20个，动作效率达78.4（对比人类基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型存在两难：深度网络世界模型灵活但数据饥渴且分布外泛化差；程序合成世界模型数据高效且可复用，但仅限于结构化状态世界且需预定义对象词汇，无法扩展到需要灵活假设对象结构的像素渲染环境。本文旨在弥合这一差距，实现在像素环境中在线、数据高效地学习可重用的程序化世界模型。

Method: OPINE-World 采用双代理协同的"假设-检验"循环架构：（1）一个代理在环境中执行动作；（2）另一个代理通过代码合成构建世界模型，并利用重放验证和基于模型的规划进行迭代精化。探索策略由一种称为"本体论误差"（ontology error）的贝叶斯度量驱动，用于评估对象类型假设的充分性，从而引导智能体高效探索未知对象结构。整体框架将LLM代码生成与反例引导归纳合成（CEGIS）相结合。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3基准（该基准隐藏了对象词汇、目标和动作语义，用于评估技能习得效率）上，OPINE-World 在25个游戏中解决了20个，且无需逐游戏训练；动作效率得分达到78.4（人类基线为100）。

Conclusion: OPINE-World 成功展示了在像素渲染环境中，通过耦合环境交互与程序合成，可以高效地在线学习对象中心化的可重用世界模型。本体论误差驱动的探索机制有效引导了对未知对象结构的发现，使得智能体在无需大量训练数据的情况下，具备了接近人类的技能习得效率。

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>


### [290] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski,Sam Adam-Day,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了SOLiD（基于谎言检测器的可扩展监督）方法，在更大规模模型和更多样化的偏好学习场景中进行评估。结果表明，随着模型规模增大，未被检测到的欺骗行为显著下降（从1B模型的34%降至405B模型的14%），且可以在微调阶段完全移除昂贵的人工标注者而不显著增加欺骗。但该方法对检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据之间的分布偏移高度敏感，可能导致假阳性率升至不可接受的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中的欺骗行为监控和预防成本高昂，现有方法依赖昂贵的人工标注者来审查可疑输出。作者旨在验证SOLiD方法能否在更大规模模型上有效运行，并在更真实、更多样化的偏好学习场景中保持鲁棒性，以探索降低甚至消除对人工标注者依赖的可行性。

Method: 将SOLiD框架扩展到更大参数规模（1B至405B）的模型，在多种偏好学习设置下进行评估。SOLiD的核心思路是训练谎言检测器（lie detector）来识别模型输出中的欺骗行为，仅将检测器标记的可疑响应交由高成本标注者审查，从而实现可扩展监督。实验还测试了完全移除微调阶段人工标注者的方案，并分析了分布偏移对检测器性能的影响。

Result: （1）规模扩展表现良好：在检测器真阳性率99%的条件下，未被发现的欺骗行为从1B模型的34%降至405B模型的14%；（2）可在微调阶段完全移除人工标注者，欺骗行为无统计显著增加；（3）SOLiD对检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据之间的分布偏移高度敏感，分布偏移会导致检测器假阳性率达到不可实际使用的水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD方法在大规模模型上展现出良好的扩展性和实用潜力，甚至可完全省去微调阶段的人工标注。但分布偏移问题构成了该方法在实际部署中的关键瓶颈，需要进一步研究以提升检测器的跨分布鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception. However, SOLiD is sensitive to distribution shift between detector training and preference-training data, which can drive detector false positive rates to impractical levels.

</details>


### [291] [Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02510)
*Mona Schirmer,Metod Jazbec,Alexander Timans,Christian Naesseth,Maja Waldron,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于外部验证器信号加阈值校准的实时安全监控方法，用于检测LLM部署时的不安全输出。该方法在数学推理和红队测试数据集上表现与基于序贯假设检验的高级监控器相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管经过对齐训练，LLM在部署时仍可能生成不安全输出。因此，需要在线监控输出并在安全性无法保证时及时报警，确保实际应用中的安全可控。

Method: 设计了一个简单的实时监控器：利用外部模型的验证器信号，通过设定阈值将其转化为报警决策，其中阈值通过风险控制（risk control）方法进行校准。

Result: 在数学推理和红队测试数据集上的实验表明，该简单设计与基于序贯假设检验的更复杂监控方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单的阈值化实时监控器配合风险控制校准，即可达到复杂序贯检测方法的效果，为LLM部署安全监控提供了一种轻量高效的设计方案。

Abstract: Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [292] [EO-Agents: A Three-Agent LLM Pipeline for Earth Observation Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01584)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Amin Tabrizian,Armin Mehrabian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于NASA地球观测知识图谱的科学假设生成管线：利用异构图神经网络对历史数据集共现关系进行排序，再通过三智能体LLM流水线筛选、生成并评估结构化研究假设，在1475个NASA数据集上产生了160个跨领域假设，并揭示了单评判LLM评估的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的科学假设生成大多依赖非结构化文献和自由形式的文本主张，缺乏对结构化知识图谱的利用。作者旨在将假设生成直接建立在NASA地球观测知识图谱之上，从而产生更具可验证性和领域针对性的假设。

Method: 系统包含两个阶段：(1) 在NASA地球观测知识图谱的历史共现关系上训练异构图神经网络（HGNN），对候选数据集配对进行排序；(2) 使用三智能体LLM流水线（筛选、生成、评估）对排序后的配对进行结构化研究假设的过滤、生成和评分。最后通过2×2×2析因实验（GPT-5.2 vs Claude Sonnet 4.6 交叉评判）评估排序稳定性和评分一致性。

Result: 系统在1475个NASA数据集上生成了160个跨领域假设，涵盖生态水文学、冰川学、气溶胶-云相互作用、植被物候学和平流层化学等。模型预测的新颖数据集配对的合理性评分接近真实文献中的共现配对。析因实验表明假设排名在不同模型间保持稳定，但绝对分数高度依赖于评判模型的身份，暴露了单评判LLM评估的局限性。

Conclusion: 将知识图谱与LLM相结合的假设生成管线能够有效挖掘科学上连贯但尚未被探索的数据集组合，但评估环节需警惕单一LLM评判带来的偏差，多评判者设计或更可靠。

Abstract: Large language models have recently been explored for scientific hypothesis generation, but most prior work relies on unstructured literature and free-form textual claims. We present a pipeline for Earth observation that grounds hypothesis generation directly in the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph. A heterogeneous graph neural network trained on historical co-usage relations ranks candidate dataset pairings, and a three-agent LLM pipeline filters, generates, and evaluates structured research hypotheses. Applied to 1,475 NASA datasets, the system produces 160 hypotheses spanning multiple Earth-science domains, including ecohydrology, glaciology, aerosol--cloud interactions, vegetation phenology, and stratospheric chemistry. Model-predicted novel dataset pairings are rated nearly as plausible as held-out real co-usages from the literature, indicating that the pipeline surfaces scientifically coherent yet unexplored combinations. A 2*2*2 factorial experiment across GPT-5.2 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows that hypothesis rankings remain stable, while absolute scores depend strongly on judge identity, highlighting limitations of single-judge LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [293] [Hawk: Harnessing Hardware-Aware Knowledge for High-Performance NPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01590)
*Junyi Wen,Ruiyan Zhuang,Yongjia Xu,Pengtu Li,Rui Zou,Hongyi Chen,Chingman Wan,Puxu Yang,Wuhui Chen,Yanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hawk 是一个无需训练即可利用硬件感知知识生成高质量 NPU 内核的框架，通过运行时知识合成、瓶颈感知检索和效果驱动蒸馏三个模块，将生成准确率从 49.4% 提升至 80.0%，并实现最高 2.2 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前为 NPU 开发高性能内核是产业瓶颈，需要开发者手动处理隐式硬件约束和严格的内存层次。大语言模型虽有自动化潜力，但因缺乏硬件特定先验知识，直接移植类似内核代码片段虽能通过编译，却会因违反底层硬件约束而导致运行时崩溃和性能退化。

Method: 提出 Hawk 框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 运行时知识合成模块，采用三元组可执行知识表示（Triple-Part Executable Knowledge Representation），将错误上下文与可执行语义内在地耦合；(2) 瓶颈感知知识检索模块，实现二维检索范式（2D-Retrieval），将查询投影到正交的语法空间和硬件对齐的语义空间；(3) 效果驱动知识蒸馏模块，利用 LLM 驱动的语义仲裁，基于实际执行反馈持续剪枝错误并合并冗余知识。

Result: 在真实 NPU 工作负载上，Hawk 将内核生成准确率从 49.4% 提升至 80.0%，相比现有最优基线方法实现最高 2.2 倍执行加速。

Conclusion: Hawk 证明通过训练无关的硬件感知知识管理框架，可以有效弥补 LLM 在 NPU 内核生成中的硬件先验缺失问题，显著提升生成准确率和执行性能。

Abstract: Developing high-performance kernels for Neural Processing Units (NPUs) is a critical industry bottleneck, requiring developers to manually navigate implicit hardware constraints and strict memory hierarchies. While large language models offer immense automation potential, they fail catastrophically on NPUs due to a fundamental lack of hardware-specific priors. Naively transplanting code snippets from similar NPU kernels may pass the compiler, but it consistently triggers runtime crashes and performance degradation by blindly violating underlying hardware constraints. To overcome this, we introduce Hawk, a training-free framework that harnesses hardware-aware knowledge through three core modules: (1) Run-Time Knowledge Synthesis Module, which employs a Triple-Part Executable Knowledge Representation to inherently couple the error context with executable semantics; (2) Bottleneck-Aware Knowledge Retrieval Module, which implements a 2D-Retrieval paradigm to project queries into orthogonal syntactic and hardware-aligned semantic spaces; and (3) Effect-Driven Knowledge Distillation Module, which leverages LLM-driven semantic arbitration to continuously distill the knowledge by pruning errors and consolidating redundancies based on the empirical execution feedback. Extensive evaluations on real-world NPU workloads demonstrate that Hawk elevates generation accuracy from 49.4% to 80.0%, while achieving up to a 2.2x execution speedup over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [294] [Safe and Adaptive Cloud Healing: Verifying LLM-Generated Recovery Plans with a Neural-Symbolic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01595)
*Junyan Tan,Haoran Lin,Siyuan Guo,Yichen Fang,Xinyue Luo,Tianyu Shen,Zeyu Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PASE 框架将云故障自愈重新定义为神经符号程序合成任务，通过 LLM 规划生成、神经符号世界模型验证和 DRL 训练的元提示优化器形成紧耦合闭环，在真实云故障数据集上将恢复时间降低 40% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云 AI 系统故障自愈方案多采用顺序松耦合架构，将 LLM 语义理解与 DRL 策略优化分离，未能充分利用 LLM 的生成与推理能力，且恢复策略局限于预定义动作空间，缺乏动态上下文感知的自适应能力。

Method: 提出 PASE 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) LLM 作为计划合成引擎，从语义原语库生成结构化恢复计划；(2) 神经符号世界模型通过仿真验证计划可行性；(3) 基于 DRL 训练的元提示优化器，学习生成最优提示以引导 LLM 规划过程，形成「推理-规划-验证-适配」紧耦合闭环。

Result: 在真实云故障注入数据集上，PASE 显著优于现有最优方法：平均系统恢复时间降低超过 40%，且在未知故障场景下故障检测准确率提升明显。

Conclusion: PASE 通过统一 LLM 推理、模型辅助验证与元学习引导，推动了自主系统管理的前沿发展，验证了神经符号融合方法在云故障自愈领域的有效性。

Abstract: As the scale and complexity of cloud-based AI systems continue to escalate, ensuring service reliability through rapid fault detection and adaptive recovery has become a critical challenge. While existing approaches integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic understanding and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for policy optimization, they often rely on sequential, loosely coupled architectures that underutilize the generative and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift with PASE, a Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine, a novel fault self-healing framework that reconceptualizes recovery as a neuro-symbolic program synthesis task. PASE employs an LLM as a core Plan Synthesis Engine to generate structured recovery plans from a library of semantic primitives. A Neural-Symbolic World Model verifies plan feasibility through simulation, while a Meta-Prompt Optimizer, trained via DRL, learns to generate optimal prompts that guide the LLM's planning process. This tight reason-plan-verify-adapt loop enables dynamic, context-aware recovery strategy generation beyond predefined action spaces. Experiments on a real-world cloud fault injection dataset demonstrate that PASE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing average system recovery time by over 40% and improving fault detection accuracy in unknown fault scenarios. Our framework advances autonomous system management by unifying LLM-based reasoning with model-assisted verification and meta-learned guidance.

</details>


### [295] [SemHash-LLM: A Multi-Granularity Semantic Hashing Framework for Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01601)
*Xinyi Fang,Kejian Tong,Jiabei Liu,Tao Ning,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemHash LLM 是一个多粒度文档去重框架，融合语义投影哈希、注意力加权 MinHash、对比边界学习与选择性 LLM 判定，通过级联过滤管道在低于 1% 的神经验证成本下实现高质量重复检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文档去重需在保持语义等价的同时处理海量语料，现有方法难以在效率与语义保真度之间取得平衡，且面临模板污染、短文本扰动、文本包含关系及病毒式片段等复杂场景的鲁棒性挑战。

Method: 提出 SemHash LLM 框架：(1) 语义投影哈希在蒸馏后的 LLM 嵌入空间中学习紧凑二值码；(2) 注意力加权 MinHash 抑制模板/无关内容，强化信息密集区域；(3) 门控融合机制整合字符级、词元级和文档级信号；(4) 级联过滤管道逐步缩减候选集；(5) 对比边界学习与不确定性估计构建自适应决策边界；(6) 选择性 LLM 判定对边界模糊样本进行最终裁决。

Result: 实验表明 SemHash LLM 实现了优异的重复检测质量，同时将神经验证成本控制在 1% 以下，在模板污染、短文本扰动、文本包含和病毒片段等多种复杂场景中均表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SemHash LLM 通过多粒度信号融合与级联过滤策略，成功在检测质量与计算效率之间取得平衡，为大规模语料去重提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large scale document deduplication must preserve semantic equivalence while remaining efficient over massive corpora. We present SemHash LLM, a multi granularity framework that unifies semantic projection hashing, attention weighted MinHash, contrastive boundary learning, and selective LLM based adjudication. The method combines character, token, and document level signals through gated fusion, then applies a cascaded filtering pipeline for efficient candidate reduction. Semantic projection hashing learns compact binary codes in distilled LLM embedding space, while attention weighted Min- Hash suppresses boilerplate and emphasizes informative content. Adaptive decision boundaries and uncertainty estimation further improve robustness across template pollution, short text perturbation, containment, and viral fragments. Experiments show that SemHash LLM achieves strong duplicate detection quality with less than one percent neural verification cost.

</details>


### [296] [Meta-Benchmarks for Financial-Services LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01740)
*Blair Hudson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个元基准测试框架，将452个公开基准映射到41项O*NET通用工作活动和38个BIAN银行业务领域，采用乘法加权（区分度×覆盖率×时效性）的Elo锦标赛评分机制，实现对金融服务业AI模型的跨基准可比较评估，避免传统排行榜忽略领域特定认知需求的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开LLM排行榜追求全局平均性能，无法捕捉金融服务业特定的认知需求（如文档合规推理、多轮客户交互），导致在通用榜单领先的模型在实际金融业务场景中表现不佳。需要一种面向金融领域工作活动的模型评估与选型框架。

Method: 构建元基准框架：(1)将452个公开基准映射至41项O*NET通用工作活动和38个BIAN银行业务领域；(2)设计乘法加权方案（区分度×覆盖率×时效性），在滚动模型窗口上计算，自动抑制饱和的遗留测试；(3)以权重缩放配对Elo锦标赛的K因子，生成跨基准可比的工作活动得分，业务领域得分为其下属工作活动Elo的加权平均。以2026年6月的时间快照（288个模型、25个组织）进行演示验证。

Result: 该框架能自动淘汰已饱和的过时基准，生成跨基准可比较的工作活动和业务领域得分，无需原始分数归一化。在2026年6月的快照数据上完成了288个模型、25个组织的评估演示，验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该元基准框架为金融机构提供了一种可复现的、面向实际工作活动的模型评估与治理方法论，弥补了通用排行榜在金融领域适用性上的不足，但其设计决策和局限性已被充分说明，便于其他机构适配。

Abstract: Public LLM leaderboards optimise for global average performance and do not capture the specific cognitive demands of financial-services work: a model that leads on MMLU-Pro may underperform on document-grounded compliance reasoning, and a coding leader may handle multi-turn customer interactions poorly. We present a meta-benchmarking framework that organises 452 publicly reported benchmarks into 41 O*NET Generalized Work Activities and aggregates those into 38 BIAN banking business domains spanning sales, operations, risk, and support work. A multiplicative weighting scheme (discrimination x coverage x recency), computed over a rolling model window, rewards benchmarks that still separate the best models, are widely reported, and remain in active use, suppressing saturated legacy tests automatically. These weights scale the K-factor in a pairwise Elo tournament, producing cross-benchmark-comparable work-activity scores without raw score normalisation; business-domain scores are weighted averages of the constituent work-activity Elos. We demonstrate the framework on a point-in-time public snapshot covering 288 models across 25 organisations as of June 2026, and describe the methodology, full taxonomy, design decisions, and limitations with the aim of making the approach reproducible for institutions facing similar selection and governance challenges.

</details>


### [297] [Path-level Hindsight Instructions for Semantic Exploration in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01754)
*Sung June Kim,Sangpil Kim,Honglak Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Phi-Nav 是一种统一的在线策略VLN框架，通过事后推理将语言指令与智能体的实际探索轨迹对齐，利用三阶段双监督循环将语义无标注的移动转化为密集训练信号，在仅需少量专家演示的情况下取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略探索虽然能让VLN智能体接触更广泛的状态分布，但会不可避免地产生偏离专家演示的轨迹，导致视觉流与原始语言指令之间出现语义不匹配。现有方法缺乏有效的机制来弥合这一语义监督鸿沟。

Method: 提出三阶段双监督循环框架：(1) Oracle引导的在线策略探索——智能体采样轨迹并从专家动作反馈中学习；(2) 事后说话者合成——基于收集到的视觉观察，生成路径级的事后指令；(3) 二次模仿——将合成的轨迹-指令对作为额外的专家演示，供智能体进行第二轮模仿学习。整个过程形成闭环，将无标注移动转化为稠密训练信号。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上，Phi-Nav仅使用当前基线方法所需专家演示的一小部分，就取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了语义探索在VLN中的必要性，Phi-Nav为在有限数据条件下训练具身智能体提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: On-policy exploration is a crucial component for training robust Vision-Language Navigation agents, as it exposes the policy to a broader state distribution. However, such exploration inevitably leads to trajectories that deviate from expert demonstrations, resulting in a semantic mismatch between the executed visual stream and the original language instruction. In this work, we address this challenge by introducing Phi-Nav, a unified on-policy framework that leverages hindsight reasoning to align instructions with the agent's actual exploratory journey. Specifically, Phi-Nav operates through a three-stage dual-supervision cycle: 1) the agent performs oracle-guided on-policy exploration, sampling a trajectory while learning from expert action feedback, 2) a hindsight speaker synthesizes a path-level hindsight instruction grounded in the collected visual observations, and 3) the agent conducts a second imitation pass, treating the synthesized trajectory-instruction pair as an additional expert demonstration. Through this process, Phi-Nav bridges the critical semantic supervision gap inherent in on-policy methods, transforming semantically unlabeled movement into dense training signals. Evaluations on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks show that Phi-Nav yields competitive performance while requiring only a fraction of the expert demonstrations used by current baselines. These results underscore the necessity of semantic exploration in VLN, positioning Phi-Nav as an effective solution for training embodied agents with limited data.

</details>


### [298] [Mastermind: Strategy-grounded Learning for Repository-Scale Vulnerability Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01764)
*Mingzhe Du,Luu Anh Tuan,Tianyi Wu,Renyang Liu,Zhijiang Guo,Dong Huang,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mastermind 双循环框架，将可迁移的策略学习与任务特定经验分离，通过学习高层次策略（而非完整动作轨迹）来提升 LLM 代理在仓库级漏洞复现任务上的表现。在 CyberGym 上，GPT-5.5 作为冻结执行器时达到 84.5% 通过率，且同一规划器可跨模型迁移提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理在仓库级漏洞复现任务中，虽能执行正确步骤，但常因选择错误的策略而失败。作者认为"策略"是更适合 SE 代理的学习单元：足够紧凑可优化、足够具体可指导执行、足够稳定可存储复用。因此需要将策略学习与具体执行轨迹解耦。

Method: 提出 Mastermind 双循环框架：(1) 可训练规划器（Planner）：通过监督微调（SFT）和基于里程碑的 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）学习可复用的漏洞复现策略；(2) 经验循环（Experience Loop）：维护任务本地策略记录，指导后续尝试。规划器独立于执行器训练，无需修改执行器的动作生成能力即可提升多个冻结执行器的表现。

Result: 在 CyberGym 数据集上（260 个训练任务，200 个留出评估任务），以 GPT-5.5 为冻结执行器时，Mastermind 达到 84.5% 通过率，显著优于开卷 PoC 上下文（60.0%）、Best-of-8 采样（63.0%）和迭代改进（77.0%）。同一规划器将 GPT-5.4 mini 从 45.0% 提升至 60.0%，将 GLM 5.1 从 58.5% 提升至 71.0%。

Conclusion: 学习高层次策略是提升仓库级 SE 代理的有效且可迁移的机制。Mastermind 通过分离策略学习与任务执行，实现了跨模型的策略迁移，证明了策略作为学习单元的优越性。

Abstract: Repository-level vulnerability reproduction is a demanding software engineering (SE) task: an agent must inspect a codebase, infer the input grammar that reaches a vulnerable path, construct a proof-of-conceptv(PoC), and verify that the crash disappears on the patched build. Recent LLM agents can often execute these steps when the approach is correct, yet they still fail by choosing the wrong strategy. This paper argues that strategy, rather than the full action trajectory, is the right learning unit for such SE agents: it is compact enough to optimize, concrete enough to guide execution, and stable enough to store and reuse across attempts. We present Mastermind, a dual-loop framework that separates transferable strategy learning from task-specific experience. A trainable planner learns reusable vulnerability-reproduction strategies through SFT and milestone-based GRPO, while an experience loop maintains task-local strategy records that guide subsequent attempts. The planner is trained independently of the executor, allowing strategy learning to improve multiple frozen executors without modifying their action-generation capability. We evaluate Mastermind on CyberGym using 260 training tasks and 200 held-out evaluation tasks. With GPT-5.5 as the frozen executor, Mastermind achieves an 84.5% pass rate, outperforming open-book PoC context (60.0%), Best-of-8 sampling (63.0%), and iterative improvement (77.0%). The same planner also improves GPT-5.4 mini and GLM~5.1 from 45.0% and 58.5% to 60.0% and 71.0%. These results demonstrate that learning high-level strategies is an effective and transferable mechanism for improving repository-scale SE agents.

</details>


### [299] [SimWorlds: A Multi-Agent System for Dynamic 3D Scene Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01766)
*Chunjiang Liu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Haoyu Chen,Yizhou Zhao,Ming-Hsuan Yang,László A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimWorlds是一个多智能体框架，能够从文本生成动态、可编辑的4D场景（3D+时间），通过规划-编码-审查工作流、分层场景协议和运行时状态检查工具，解决了空间布局、物理求解器、时序、相机和照明的协同问题，并提出了4DBuildBench基准来评估视觉保真度和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统在文本到3D场景转换中仅关注静态输出，而动态4D场景（涉及流体、粒子、刚体、铰接机构等物理现象）虽具有作为可编辑内容和视频生成/具身AI物理训练数据的价值，却未被充分探索。动态场景面临两大独特挑战：(1) 需协同空间布局、多物理求解器、时序编排、相机与照明；(2) 从渲染视频验证运动正确性远比单张图像判断困难。

Method: 提出SimWorlds多智能体框架：(1) 包含Blender特定程序化知识；(2) 采用规划器-编码器-审查者(planner-coder-reviewer)工作流驱动固定顺序的构建阶段；(3) 由确定性验证器强制执行分层场景协议；(4) 运行时状态检查工具套件，可捕获渲染图像无法揭示的机制故障。同时引入4DBuildBench基准。

Result: 实验表明SimWorlds在动态Blender生成任务上优于现有基线方法。4DBuildBench基准可同时评估生成场景的视觉保真度和物理一致性。

Conclusion: SimWorlds通过多智能体协作、分层协议和运行时验证，有效解决了文本到动态4D场景生成的挑战，为可编辑物理仿真内容创作和下游训练数据生成开辟了新路径。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used to translate natural language into 3D scenes in a procedural way, but existing systems focus on static output. Dynamic 4D scenes from text alone, in which liquids flow, particles emit, rigid bodies cascade, and articulated mechanisms move, remain largely unexplored despite their value as editable content and as physics-grounded training data for video generation and embodied AI. Two challenges set the dynamic case apart from static text-to-scene work: an agent must jointly coordinate spatial layout, multiple physics solvers, temporal sequencing, camera, and lighting in a single coherent scene, and verifying motion correctness from rendered video is fundamentally harder than judging a single image. We present SimWorlds: a multi-agent framework that produces dynamic, editable 4D scenes from text, with Blender-specific procedural knowledge, a planner-coder-reviewer workflow driving a fixed ordered sequence of construction stages, a layered scene protocol enforced by a deterministic verifier, and a runtime-state inspection tool suite that catches mechanism failures the rendered image cannot reveal. We also introduce 4DBuildBench, a benchmark for assessing both visual fidelity and physical consistency of the procedural dynamic 3D scenes generated from text prompts. Experiments show that SimWorlds outperforms prior dynamic Blender generation baselines.

</details>


### [300] [Repair the Amplifier, Not the Symptom: Stable World-Model Correction for Agent Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01767)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究大规模规划图中的故障修复问题,提出WM-SAR方法,通过子图放大反向追溯因果错误子图,仅将精简的因果子图发送给LLM修复,相比传统扫描式工程修正器,在有限token预算下显著提升了修复效果和规划图稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agent规划从短工具链发展到包含数千甚至数万步的持久工作流,故障会出现在大型规划图内部而非孤立预测中。全图重规划面临三大瓶颈:(1)计算开销巨大;(2)消耗大量上下文预算;(3)LLM暴露于大量无关症状,可能降低长上下文检索质量。因此需要一种能够原位修复失败规划图的"世界模型修正器"。

Method: 论文比较了两类修正器:(1)工程修正器——扫描节点和边,选择可疑局部区域,交由LLM修复;(2)WM-SAR(World-Model Subgraph Amplification Repair)——不从可见症状扫描入手,而是从子图放大反向追溯,识别持续重新放大错误的节点和边,仅将因果子图发送给LLM进行精准修复。

Result: 在图模拟和LLM修复实验中,WM-SAR在现实token预算下大幅优于工程修正器;能以紧凑区域实现接近全图的稳定性;为LLM提供了更清晰的修复目标。

Conclusion: WM-SAR通过因果子图识别与精准修复,解决了大规模规划图中高效故障修复的关键问题,为持久化Agent工作流的可靠性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: As agent planning moves from short tool chains toward persistent workflows with thousands or tens of thousands of steps, failures will occur inside large planning graphs rather than in isolated predictions. Replanning the entire graph after every mistake is neither computationally realistic nor desirable: full-graph replay consumes large context budgets, exposes the LLM to many irrelevant symptoms, and can degrade long-context retrieval. This paper studies the missing component in such systems: a world-model corrector that repairs the failed planning graph in place. We compare two families of correctors. The first is the common engineering approach: scan nodes and edges, choose a suspicious local region, and ask an LLM to repair it. We implement strong engineering LLM correctors and find that they can help, especially when given very large contexts. The second family is our approach, WM-SAR (World-Model Subgraph Amplification Repair): instead of scanning for visible symptoms, it works backward from subgraph amplification, identifies the nodes and edges that keep re-amplifying error, and sends only that causal subgraph to the LLM. Across graph simulations and LLM repair experiments, WM-SAR substantially outperforms engineering correctors under realistic token budgets, achieves near-whole-graph stabilization with a compact region, and gives the LLM a cleaner repair target.

</details>


### [301] [Verifiable Knowledge Expansion through Retrieval-Grounded Formal Concept Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01773)
*Yujin Yang,Heejung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个检索增强的小语言模型框架，将形式概念分析（FCA）作为符号验证循环，用于本体构建中的知识扩展。该框架从种子属性出发，由FCA在增长的形式上下文中提出蕴含，再通过检索增强的SLM神谕验证每条蕴含或返回反例，实现了可检验的知识获取过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可从文本中提出本体结构（对象、属性、结构关系），但其输出可能缺乏支撑或不一致。因此需要一种将符号推理与语言模型结合的机制，以验证和约束知识扩展的可靠性。

Method: 提出检索增强小语言模型（SLM）框架，以形式概念分析（FCA）为符号验证循环。流程为：(1) 从种子属性出发，FCA在增长的形式上下文中生成属性蕴含；(2) 检索增强的SLM神谕对每条蕴含进行验证，或返回反例；(3) 神谕同时支持关联判断、一致性检查和新属性提议，使得所有被接受的蕴含、反例、矛盾及修正均可审查。实验基于Orphadata资源构建的罕见共济失调数据集。

Result: 在10种子运行中，检索增强方案的关系F1为0.29-0.52，基于闭包的蕴含F1为0.22-0.30。更大的种子集会增加评估的蕴含数量，且通常提升蕴含F1。较低的蕴含得分反映了对导出蕴含的更严格评估——遗漏或多余的关系会影响多条蕴含的判断。消融实验表明，在固定对象-属性设置中的关联判断可提升基于闭包的蕴含得分，但即使候选对象和属性已固定，识别正例对象-属性对仍然困难。

Conclusion: FCA作为符号验证循环能有效与检索增强SLM结合，实现可审查、可追溯的本体知识扩展。该方法在罕见病领域展示了可行性，但精确识别对象-属性关联仍是核心挑战。

Abstract: Ontology construction requires deciding which objects, attributes, and structural relations should be accepted as valid knowledge. Language models can propose such structures from text, but their outputs can still be unsupported or inconsistent. This paper proposes a retrieval-augmented small language model (SLM) framework that uses formal concept analysis (FCA) as a symbolic verification loop for knowledge expansion. Starting from seed attributes, FCA proposes implications over a growing formal context. A retrieval-grounded SLM oracle then validates each implication or returns a counterexample. The oracle also supports incidence judgments, consistency checks, and attribute proposals, making accepted implications, counterexamples, contradictions, and corrections inspectable. In a rare ataxia setting constructed from Orphadata resources, retrieval-grounded 10-seed runs obtain relation F1 of 0.29-0.52 and closure-based implication F1 of 0.22-0.30. Larger seed sets increase the number of evaluated implications and often improve implication F1. The lower implication scores reflect a stricter evaluation of derived implications, where one missed or extra relation can affect several implication judgments. Ablations show that incidence judgments in a fixed object-attribute setting can improve closure-based implication scores. However, identifying positive object-attribute pairs remains difficult even when the candidate objects and attributes are fixed.

</details>


### [302] [Subliminal Clocks: Latent Time Modelling in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01774)
*Maximo Rulli,Thomas Fontanari,Simone Petruzzi,Federico Alvetreti,Giorgio Strano,Donato Crisostomi,Giorgos Nikolaou,Tommaso Mencattini,Andrea Santilli,Emanuele Rodolà,Simone Scardapane,Alessio Devoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散语言模型（DLM）在残差流中隐式编码了与扩散时间步相关的潜在表示，该信号可通过探针可靠提取，并且操控该低维子空间可系统性调节模型的去噪进程感知，进而影响置信度和熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLM不像标准扩散模型那样显式依赖时间步条件，因此核心问题是：这些模型是否在内部表征了去噪进度，以及该信息如何被下游使用。

Method: 作者采用探针（probes）方法跨层从残差流激活中解码时间步信号；进一步通过沿推断时间步相关的低维子空间进行模型引导（steering），操控模型的去噪进程感知；最后对表征空间的几何结构进行分析。

Result: DLM确实在其内部激活中编码了可解码的时间步信号；引导该子空间可导致模型置信度和熵发生可预测的变化；该表征在激活空间中呈现出结构化和可解释的几何特性。

Conclusion: DLM在无显式时间步条件下仍能内在地形成去噪进度的结构化表征，该表征可通过探针提取和操控，揭示了此类模型处理去噪信号的内部机制。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models. Unlike standard diffusion-based approaches, DLMs are not explicitly conditioned on a timestep, raising a natural question: do these models internally represent denoising progress, and how is such information used downstream? In this work, we show that DLMs do in fact encode a latent representation related to the diffusion timestep within their residual streams. We find that this signal can be reliably extracted using probes across layers, indicating that denoising progress is decodable from internal activations. We further demonstrate that steering the model along a low-dimensional subspace associated with the inferred timestep allows us to systematically modulate its notion of denoising progress, leading to predictable changes in model confidence and entropy. Finally, we analyse the geometry of the identified representation, showing that it exhibits structured and interpretable properties in activation space, and shedding light on how such a signal is processed by these models.

</details>


### [303] [Safety Testing LLM Agents at Scale: From Risk Discovery to Evidence-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01793)
*Yunhao Feng,Ruixiao Lin,Ming Wen,Qinqin He,Yanming Guo,Yifan Ding,Yutao Wu,Jialuo Chen,Yunhao Chen,Xiaohu Du,Jianan Ma,Zixing Chen,Zhuoer Xu,Xingjun Ma,Xinhao Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vera 是一个端到端的自动化安全测试框架，通过三阶段自强化流水线（文献驱动的风险探索、组合式安全用例生成、自适应沙箱执行与证据验证），系统性地发现并验证 LLM Agent 在真实工具使用中的安全漏洞。在四个生产级 Agent 框架上平均攻击成功率达 93.9%，并发布了包含 1600 个可执行安全用例的 Vera-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 安全测试存在两大痛点：一是测试目标依赖专家手工设计的安全违规场景，难以跟随 Agent 快速演进；二是测试结果评估使用硬编码规则，扩展成本高、灵活性差。需要一种自动化、可扩展、能适应非确定性 Agent 行为的系统化安全测试方案。

Method: 提出三阶段自强化流水线：(1) 文献驱动探索：持续从文献中挖掘新兴风险，构建安全风险、攻击方法和工具执行环境三个维度的分类体系；(2) 组合式安全用例生成：跨分类维度进行组合，生成包含具体安全目标、编程构造的初始状态和基于可观测产物的确定性验证谓词的可执行安全用例；(3) 自适应执行：在隔离沙箱中运行异构 Agent，由控制 Agent 基于运行时观测引导多轮交互，验证器依据环境状态和工具调用证据（而非模型自述）进行结果判定。

Result: 在 OpenClaw、Hermes、Codex、Claude Code 四个生产级 Agent 框架上评估，多通道攻击下平均攻击成功率达 93.9%；发布了 Vera-Bench 基准，包含 1600 个可执行安全用例，覆盖 124 个风险类别、三种执行环境设置。

Conclusion: 模块化、可执行的安全测试基础设施对于大规模、快速演进的 Agent 系统实现严格且可维护的安全评估至关重要。Vera 展示了自动化安全测试在发现高成功率攻击方面的有效性，为 Agent 安全领域提供了方法论和基准支持。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly perform autonomous actions through external tools, leading to complex and evolving safety risks. However, existing safety testing targets expert-designed safety violations, and the corresponding outcomes are evaluated by hard-coded rules, making them costly to extend as agents evolve. To this end, we present Vera, an end-to-end automated safety testing framework that instantiates software engineering testing principles for non-deterministic agents through a three-stage, self-reinforcing pipeline. First, a literature-driven exploration continuously discovers and structures emerging risks into taxonomies of safety risks, attack methods, and tool execution environments. Second, combinatorial composition across taxonomy dimensions produces executable safety cases, each specifying a concrete safety goal, a programmatically constructed initial state, and a deterministic verification predicate grounded in observable artifacts. Third, adaptive execution runs heterogeneous agents in isolated sandboxes where a control agent steers multi-turn interaction based on runtime observations, while evidence-grounded verifiers judge outcomes from environment state and tool-call evidence rather than model self-report. We evaluate Vera on four production agent frameworks (OpenClaw, Hermes, Codex, Claude Code), revealing substantial safety weaknesses, with average attack success rates reaching 93.9\% under multi-channel attacks; we also release Vera-Bench, comprising 1600 executable safety cases spanning 124 risk categories across three execution settings. These results indicate that modular, executable testing infrastructure is essential for rigorous and maintainable safety evaluation of rapidly evolving agentic systems at scale. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Yunhao-Feng/Vera.

</details>


### [304] [MMIR-TCM: Memory-Integrated Multimodal Inference and Retrieval for TCM Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01814)
*Lihui Luo,Joongwon Chae,Ziyan Chen,Yang Liu,Siyi Cheng,Weihan Gao,Zelin Zeng,Xiaoming Yin,Samaneh Beheshti Kashi,Dongmei Yu,Lian Zhang,Jing Sui,Zeming Liang,Jiansong Ji,Peter E. Lobie,Peiwu Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMIR-TCM框架，通过将多模态大语言模型与记忆增强分割、检索增强生成相结合，模拟中医专家诊断流程，解决中医舌诊中视觉特征与文本推理之间的语义鸿沟问题，并引入MedTCM数据集和TDEU评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中医舌诊面临主观性强、可重复性差的问题，多模态AI在中医辨证论治中的应用受限于视觉舌象特征与文本推理之间的语义鸿沟，以及缺乏大规模标准化数据集。

Method: 提出MMIR-TCM三阶段框架：(1) 训练无关的Memory-SAM模块用于鲁棒舌象分割提取；(2) 微调的Qwen3-VL模型生成结构化舌诊描述；(3) 基于Qwen3的RAG组件生成有证据支撑的临床决策建议。同时引入新的大规模多模态数据集MedTCM，以及领域专用的TDEU评估指标（融合语义理解与诊断重要性）。

Result: 综合实验表明，MMIR-TCM在临床准确性上显著优于GPT-4o和Gemini 2.5 Flash等领先模型。

Conclusion: MMIR-TCM通过三阶段架构有效弥合了舌诊中多模态语义鸿沟，MedTCM数据集和TDEU评估指标为中医AI研究提供了标准化基础，框架在临床决策支持任务上达到领先水平。

Abstract: Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) diagnosis, particularly through tongue inspection, faces persistent challenges in subjectivity and reproducibility. The application of multimodal artificial intelligence to TCM clinical tasks, such as syndrome differentiation and prescription generation, is significantly hampered by the semantic gap between visual tongue features and textual reasoning, as well as the lack of large-scale, standardized datasets. To address these challenges, we introduce MMIR-TCM, a novel framework that emulates the diagnostic process of TCM experts by integrating multimodal large language model(MLLM) with memory-augmented segmentation and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Employing a three-stage architecture, MMIR-TCM integrates a training-free Memory-SAM module for robust tongue extraction, a fine-tuned Qwen3-VL model for structured tongue diagnosis generation, and a Qwen3-based RAG component for evidence-grounded clinical decision support generation. The framework was developed and validated using MedTCM, a new large-scale multimodal dataset that we introduce specifically for advanced TCM research. To properly evaluate our framework's clinical accuracy, which existing metrics fail to capture, we also developed TDEU, a domain-specific evaluation metric incorporating semantic understanding and diagnostic importance. Our comprehensive experiments demonstrate that MMIR-TCM significantly outperforms leading models, including GPT-4o and Gemini 2.5 Flash.

</details>


### [305] [Pre-Flight: A Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models on Aviation Operational Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01829)
*Alex Brooker,Tim Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Pre-Flight基准，包含300道航空领域多选题，覆盖国际标准与地面运营知识。评测显示，即使最强模型也仅达82.7%准确率，与专家参考水平（~95%）存在显著差距，强调领域专项评测是航空业负责任部署生成式AI的必要前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用基准无法衡量LLM在航空特定运营知识上的安全性与正确性推理能力，而航空领域的高风险、强监管特性使这一缺陷后果严重，亟需领域专用评测基准。

Method: 构建Pre-Flight基准：300道多选题，由空管、地面运营和商业飞行从业者编写与审核，覆盖国际机场地面运营、ICAO/FAA法规、航空常识及复杂运营场景。使用Inspect评测框架，按标准多选题准确率评分，并持续更新排行榜。

Result: 专家参考水平约95%（来自航空专业人士会议小样本测试）。最强模型（2026年发布）仅达82.7%，较2025年初的~75%提升缓慢。与专家可靠性之间仍存在显著且持续的差距。

Conclusion: 领域专项评测是航空非安全关键运营中负责任部署生成式AI的必要前提。当前LLM距专家级可靠性仍有实质差距，需要持续关注与改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for aviation business operations, from documentation and training generation to customer facing assistants. General purpose benchmarks do not measure whether a model reasons safely and correctly about aviation specific operational knowledge, and the high stakes, regulated nature of the domain makes that gap consequential. We present Pre-Flight, an open source benchmark of 300 multiple choice questions drawn from international standards and airport ground operations material, covering international airport ground operations, ICAO and US FAA regulations, aviation general knowledge and complex operational scenarios. Questions were authored and reviewed by practitioners with experience in air traffic management, ground operations and commercial flying. We evaluate a range of contemporary commercial and open weight models using the Inspect evaluation framework, scoring by accuracy under a standard multiple choice protocol, and we maintain the leaderboard on a rolling basis as new models are released. Against an informal expert reference of around 95%, obtained from a low sample quiz of aviation professionals at a conference, even the strongest model evaluated (released in 2026) reaches 82.7%, having improved only gradually from roughly 75% in early 2025. A substantial and persistent gap below expert level reliability therefore remains. We release the dataset, the evaluation harness and the results, and the benchmark is available within the community evaluations package distributed with inspect_evals. We argue that domain specific evaluation of this kind is a necessary precondition for responsible deployment of generative AI in non safety critical aviation operations.

</details>


### [306] [Actual causality in fault trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01840)
*Georgiana Caltais,Milan Lopuhaä-Zwakenberg,Mariëlle Stoelinga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从Halpern & Pearl的实际因果关系理论视角研究故障树，将故障树从"什么可能出错"的工具扩展为回答"为什么出错了"的诊断工具，并给出了不同实际因果概念在故障树图结构与逻辑结构下的完整分类，证明了最小割集如何产生实际原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障树分析传统上用于回答"什么可能出错"（通过最小割集分析进行风险评估），但缺乏对"为什么已经出错了"这一故障诊断核心问题的形式化回答能力。作者旨在将实际因果关系理论与故障树结合，填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Halpern & Pearl的实际因果关系理论框架，对故障树进行形式化分析：1) 将故障树的图结构（节点、门）映射到因果模型的变量与结构方程；2) 在该框架下对不同实际因果概念（如充分原因、必要原因、实际原因等）进行分类；3) 分析最小割集与实际原因之间的对应关系。

Result: 1) 给出了故障树中各类实际因果概念的完整分类，建立了图结构、逻辑结构与因果概念之间的精确对应；2) 证明了最小割集可以直接产生实际原因，从而在故障树与实际因果关系之间架起桥梁。

Conclusion: 故障树不仅可以用于前瞻性风险分析（什么可能出错），还可以通过实际因果关系理论实现回溯性故障诊断（为什么出错了），最小割集在因果归因中具有核心作用。

Abstract: Fault trees are a widely used as effective risk models for complex systems, answering the question "what can go wrong?", especially through minimal cut set analysis. We study fault trees from the perspective of Halpern & Pearl's theory of actual causality. This allows us to use fault trees to answer the question "why has it gone wrong?", which is fundamental to failure diagnostics. We give a complete classification of each of the different notions of actual causality in terms of the fault tree's graph structure and logical structure, and show how minimal cut sets give rise to actual causes.

</details>


### [307] [CLAP: Closed-Loop Training, Evaluation, and Release Control for Domain Agent Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01846)
*Fangfei Li,Chenyang Zhao,Long Wang,Feng Tian,Zhiyue Zheng,Lv Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLAP（闭环智能体后训练方法），将业务数据转化为结构化样本并建立数据-训练-评估-发布闭环，在制造场景中验证了 LoRA-SFT 的有限增益与 GRPO 的高 KL 风险，强调应通过集成化循环而非单次离线评分来管理领域智能体的后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域智能体面临噪声业务数据、不确定的后训练收益、离线/应用不匹配以及适配器发布风险等实际挑战，现有方法缺乏从数据到发布的系统性闭环管理。

Method: 提出 CLAP 闭环方法，包含五大组件：(1) 将业务数据转化为结构化 SFT 样本和决策偏好样本；(2) 构建留出集与风险诊断；(3) 数据验证与目标/证据归一化；(4) 奖励/KL 散度诊断与离线门控；(5) 应用链重放（application-chain replay），综合判断适配器是否适合目标应用链。实验中使用 QLoRA 风格的 LoRA-SFT 和 GRPO，在 3B 骨干模型上结合 RAG 进行验证。

Result: 在五个匿名化制造场景批次中，LoRA-SFT 平均收益有限：总体得分提升 0.0098，通过率提升 0.0240，证据准确率提升 0.0280，幻觉和错误事实有所减少。但仅 3/5 批次有正向提升，部分批次出现退化；GRPO 暴露出高 KL 风险。应用链重放进一步表明 RAG 对事实提取不可或缺；应用-RAG 导向的 LoRA-SFT 适配器在价值、核心字段及答案-证据文档/页码匹配上优于基础+RAG，但延迟增加。

Conclusion: 领域智能体的后训练应通过集成化的数据-训练-评估-发布闭环进行管理，而非仅依赖训练完成状态或单一离线评分。CLAP 为这一范式提供了可操作的框架。

Abstract: Domain agents often face noisy business data, uncertain post-training gains, offline/application mismatch, and adapter-release risk. This paper presents CLAP (Closed-Loop Agent Post-training), a closed-loop method that converts business data into structured SFT samples, decision-preference samples, holdout sets, risk diagnostics, and release-gate records. CLAP combines data validation, target/evidence normalization, reward/KL diagnosis, offline gates, and application-chain replay to decide whether an adapter is suitable for the target application chain. On five anonymized manufacturing-scenario batches, QLoRA-style LoRA-SFT yields modest average gains: overall score increases by 0.0098, pass rate by 0.0240, and evidence accuracy by 0.0280, while hallucination and wrong facts decrease. Yet only 3 of 5 batches improve, some batches regress, and GRPO exposes high KL risks. Application-chain replay further shows that RAG is necessary for factual extraction; under the same 3B backbone and 100 replay cases, an application-RAG-oriented LoRA-SFT adapter improves value, core fields, and answer-evidence doc/page matching over base+RAG, but increases latency. These results support managing domain-agent post-training through an integrated data-training-evaluation-release loop rather than relying on training completion or a single offline score.

</details>


### [308] [Safety Targeted Embedding Exploit via Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01859)
*Joshua Adrian Cahyono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型安全训练以英语为主，导致在低资源语言和代码混合输入中存在认知鸿沟，模型会自信地生成有害回复；作者提出 STEER 攻击方法，通过梯度引导识别拒绝关键词并迭代翻译为低资源语言，攻击成功率高达 96.7%，且可迁移至 GPT-4o-mini。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 安全对齐训练主要以英语进行，其在低资源语言和混合语言（code-switching）场景下的泛化能力尚不明确。作者旨在揭示这一"认知鸿沟"（epistemic gap），即模型在面对分布外多语言输入时会自信地绕过安全机制生成有害内容。

Method: 提出 STEER（Safety Targeted Embedding Exploit via Refinement），一种梯度引导攻击方法：1）利用模型梯度识别对拒绝行为贡献最大的关键 token；2）迭代地将这些 token 翻译为低资源语言，在保留有害意图的同时抑制模型的拒绝响应。在 6 个开源 8B 模型上进行评估，并与随机代码切换和 GCG 攻击对比，同时测试向 GPT-4o-mini 的迁移攻击。

Result: STEER 在 JailbreakBench 上攻击成功率高达 93.0%，在 AdvBench 上高达 96.7%，显著优于随机代码切换和 GCG 基线。生成的攻击提示可迁移至 GPT-4o-mini，在无目标模型访问权限下仍达 35.5% 的攻击成功率，表明该漏洞非单一架构所独有。

Conclusion: 仅基于英语的安全对齐机制不能假定在多语言输入上泛化。提升多语言安全性需要在安全对齐阶段覆盖更广泛的语言分布，并引入能显式检测和拒绝对分布外输入进行响应的机制。

Abstract: Safety training for large language models (LLMs) is conducted predominantly in English, leaving uncertain how well safety mechanisms generalize to low-resource languages and mixed-language code-switching. We show that this creates an epistemic gap in which models confidently generate harmful responses for inputs that fall outside the distribution of their safety training. To study this phenomenon, we introduce STEER (Safety Targeted Embedding Exploit via Refinement), a gradient-guided attack that identifies words contributing most strongly to the model's refusal behavior and iteratively translates them into low-resource languages to suppress refusal while preserving harmful intent. Across six open-source 8B-parameter models, STEER achieves attack success rates of up to 93.0% on JailbreakBench and 96.7% on AdvBench, outperforming random code-switching and Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG). The resulting prompts also transfer to GPT-4o-mini, achieving a 35.5% attack success rate without requiring access to the target model, suggesting that the underlying weakness is not specific to a single architecture. These findings demonstrate that safety mechanisms aligned primarily on English cannot be assumed to generalize across multilingual inputs. We argue that improving multilingual safety requires broader coverage during alignment and mechanisms that explicitly detect and abstain on out-of-distribution inputs.

</details>


### [309] [CamoNAS: Neural Architecture Search for Enhanced Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01870)
*Dawei Ren,Yan Zhang,Hongying Tang,Qiaoling Zhou,Jianpo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CamoNAS 是一个面向伪装目标检测的神经架构搜索框架，通过自动搜索细胞级操作和网络级下采样路径，并引入频率感知的RGB-频域双流架构，在四个COD基准上达到了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统COD模型依赖手工设计的架构和直觉驱动的多尺度特征融合，缺乏系统性搜索，难以有效应对伪装目标边缘弱、边界模糊的挑战。因此需要一种自动化架构搜索方法来为COD任务定制最优网络结构。

Method: 提出CamoNAS框架：(1) 构建层次化搜索空间，同时搜索细胞级操作和网络级下采样路径；(2) 采用RGB-频域双流架构，利用可学习的小波变换提取频域信息，与RGB空间流互补；(3) 通过神经架构搜索自动发现适合COD任务的最优网络配置。

Result: 在四个COD基准数据集（CAMO、COD10K、NC4K、CHAMELEON）上均取得最先进性能，验证了NAS在伪装目标检测任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: CamoNAS证明了神经架构搜索可以替代手工设计，为COD任务自动发现更优的网络结构，频率双流设计进一步提升了伪装目标的检测能力。

Abstract: Camouflaged Object Detection (COD) aims to locate and segment objects that blend into their surroundings, presenting challenges due to weak edge cues and ill-defined boundaries. Traditional COD models rely on hand-designed architectures and multi-scale feature fusion, which are often guided by intuition rather than systematic search. This paper introduces CamoNAS, a frequency-aware multi-resolution Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for COD. CamoNAS automatically searches both cell-level operations and network-level downsampling paths, forming a hierarchical search space tailored to detect camouflaged objects. Additionally, it adopts an RGB frequency dual-stream architecture, where a learnable wavelet transform complements the RGB spatial stream. CamoNAS achieves state-of-the-art performance on four COD benchmarks (CAMO, COD10K, NC4K, CHAMELEON), highlighting the effectiveness of NAS for COD. Our code is available at https://github.com/rendaweiSIMIT/CamoNAS.

</details>


### [310] [SkillCoach: Self-Evolving Rubrics for Evaluating and Enhancing Agentic Skill-Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01874)
*Jiayin Zhu,Kelong Mao,Yudong Guo,Dengbo He,Sulong Xu,Simiu Gu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillCoach 是一个自演化的评分框架，通过从真实轨迹中提取技能基元过程量规，沿四个维度（技能选择、技能遵循、技能组合、技能基元反思）评估智能体技能使用，并将过程质量与外部验证结果解耦，从而区分偶然成功与真正的技能掌握。演化后的量规进一步作为过程监督信号，用于筛选高质量训练轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实技能库中存在大量重叠技能，导致智能体难以可靠地选择和使用技能。仅依赖最终验证器（outcome-only signal）作为评估和训练信号过于粗糙——智能体可能通过试错侥幸成功，同时存在选择干扰技能、跳过必要步骤、错误组合工作流或遗漏最终检查等问题，但这些过程性缺陷被最终成功率掩盖。

Method: 提出 SkillCoach 框架：1）从真实 rollout 轨迹中自动推导技能基元过程量规（skill-grounded process rubrics）；2）沿四个维度评估轨迹：技能选择、技能遵循、技能组合、技能基元反思；3）将外部验证器作为独立的结果信号保留，使过程质量与偶然任务成功得以区分；4）演化后的量规作为过程监督，用于筛选高质量训练轨迹进行策略优化。

Result: 实验表明，演化后的量规显著提升了评估质量，能暴露被最终准确率掩盖的失败模式，并且相比仅使用结果过滤的方法，能提供更强的监督信号来增强智能体的技能使用能力。

Conclusion: SkillCoach 通过过程量规实现了细粒度的技能使用评估与训练，证明过程监督信号优于仅依赖结果的信号，为构建更可靠的 LLM 智能体技能使用机制提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Skills are becoming a reusable operational layer for LLM agents, encoding SOPs, domain rules, tool workflows, scripts, and validation routines. In realistic skill repositories, overlapping skills make reliable skill-use difficult. Final verifier success is too coarse for both evaluation and training, since an agent may pass through trial and error while selecting distractor skills, skipping required steps, composing workflows incorrectly or omitting final checks. We introduce SkillCoach, a self-evolving rubric framework for evaluating and enhancing agentic skill-use. SkillCoach derives skill-grounded process rubrics from real rollouts and evaluates trajectories along four dimensions: skill selection, skill following, skill composition, and skill-grounded reflection. It keeps the external verifier as a separate outcome signal, allowing process quality to be distinguished from accidental task success. The evolved rubrics further serve as process supervision for selecting high-quality training trajectories. Experiments show that evolved rubrics substantially improve evaluation quality, expose failures hidden by final accuracy, and provide stronger supervision signals than outcome-only filtering for enhancing agentic skill-use.

</details>


### [311] [Spec-AUF: Accept-Until-Fail Training under Train-Inference Misalignment for Masked Block Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01893)
*Tianjian Yang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUF（Accepted Prefix-Under Focus）是一种针对块草稿器（block drafter）的单一损失函数改进策略：将全块交叉熵的支持集截断至草稿器首次预测失败的位置，从而使监督信号集中在对推理实际有用的已接受前缀上，无需辅助目标、验证器展开或推理流程变更。在 Qwen3-8B 上，DFlash 的平均发射长度 τ 从 2.40 提升至 2.61，Domino 从 2.56 提升至 2.68。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块草稿器（如 DLM 风格）训练时使用全块交叉熵，对每个位置都用 gold token 监督，但推理时首个被拒绝 token 之后的所有预测都会被丢弃，造成训练-推理不一致。已有接受感知目标通过重加权缓解此问题，但本文以教师强制学习为动机，提出监督应更根本地集中在被接受的前缀上。

Method: AUF 方法针对仅使用掩码输入的块草稿器（无 gold 前缀条件输入的通道），在损失侧近似前缀敏感监督：将交叉熵支持集截断至草稿器自身第一个预测失败的位置（即第一个与 gold 不一致的 token），只保留该位置之前的 token 参与损失计算。这是一个对 CE 支持集的单一、独立修改，不引入辅助目标、不需要验证器展开、不改变推理流程和精确性契约。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上固定草稿器骨干和 serving 设置：(1) DFlash 草稿器在六个基准上平均发射长度 τ 从 2.40 提升至 2.61，每个基准均有提升；(2) 迁移至 Domino 双分支头，τ 从 2.56 提升至 2.68；(3) 仅使用衰减加权的基线在共享块掩码上达到更高 token 准确率但解码效果更差；(4) 一旦 AUF 截断支持集后，标准指数位置衰减加权在经验上变得无效。

Conclusion: AUF 表明，对块草稿器损失支持集的简单截断——将监督集中在推理时实际被保留的前缀上——是一种有效且低侵入性的改进策略。更高的 token 准确率不一定转化为更好的解码效率，这揭示了训练指标与推理性能之间的关键差异。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by drafting a block of tokens that the target model verifies left-to-right, committing only the longest accepted prefix. Block (DLM-style) drafters predict the whole block in parallel, which is fast but trained with a full-block cross-entropy that supervises every position against the gold continuation -- even though inference discards every token after the first rejection. Recent acceptance-aware objectives patch this by reweighting the full-block loss; we instead use teacher-forced learning as a motivation for how supervision should concentrate on the accepted prefix. A mask-only block drafter has no input-side channel for gold-prefix conditioning, so AUF approximates that prefix-sensitive supervision on the loss side by keeping the cross-entropy support only through the drafter's first predicted failure. AUF is a single, detached change to the CE support -- no auxiliary objective, no verifier rollouts, and no change to the inference pipeline or the exactness contract. Within fixed drafter backbones and serving settings on Qwen3-8B, AUF raises the DFlash drafter's average emitted length $τ$, averaged over six benchmarks, from 2.40 to 2.61, with a gain on every benchmark, and transfers to Domino's two-branch head (2.56 to 2.68). Two findings sharpen the picture: the decay-only baseline reaches higher token accuracy on the shared block mask yet decodes worse, and on DFlash, once AUF truncates the support, the standard exponential position-decay weighting becomes empirically inert.

</details>


### [312] [Rethinking Complexity Metrics for LLM-Integrated Applications: Beyond Source Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01903)
*Zihao Xu,Yuekang Li,Gelei Deng,Yi Liu,Zhenchang Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HECATE是首个同时评估LLM集成应用中提示层和代码层复杂性的工具。基于Hoare逻辑启发的"提示即规约"形式化方法，该工具从25个复杂性维度生成52个候选指标，经实证筛选后仅10个指标通过检验。其中7个新引入的"结构广度"指标（如LLM调用点、内存属性、提示模板等）在预测维护工作量方面优于传统的RFC、Halstead N和V指标，且提示层指标即使在控制代码层指标后仍具显著性，表明提示复杂性是一个独立维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复杂性度量工具仅作用于代码层，完全忽略了LLM集成应用中提示层所承载的大量运行时行为逻辑，导致无法准确评估此类应用的真实复杂性。

Method: 提出Prompt-as-Specification形式化方法（受Hoare逻辑启发），基于已发表的分类法识别25个复杂性维度并生成52个候选指标。从18个开源仓库收集118个组件，以版本历史中的维护活动作为复杂性的实证代理，通过控制代码规模后的统计显著性检验筛选指标，最终在6个留出仓库的20个组件上进行泛化验证。

Result: 52个候选指标中仅10个通过检验。7个为新引入的"结构广度"指标（计量LLM调用点、内存属性、提示模板等结构上不同的元素数量），3个为传统指标（RFC、Halstead N和V）。新指标性能超越传统指标；提示层指标在添加最强代码层指标作为协变量后仍保持显著性，证明提示复杂性是独立维度。留出验证证实两个最优指标仍能预测维护工作量。

Conclusion: 提示复杂性是LLM集成应用中一个独立且重要的复杂性维度，传统的代码层指标无法充分捕捉。HECATE引入的"结构广度"类指标比传统的"规模"类指标更能有效预测维护工作量，为LLM集成应用的复杂性评估提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: LLM-integrated applications blend natural language prompts with program code, and much of their runtime behavior originates in the prompt layer rather than in the code itself. Existing complexity metrics, however, operate solely at the code level and therefore overlook this behavioral logic entirely. We present HECATE, the first tool designed to assess complexity in both the prompt and code layers of such applications. Central to HECATE is Prompt-as-Specification, a Hoare-logic-inspired formalism that interprets every prompt as a specification of intended behavior. Grounded in 25 complexity dimensions identified across published taxonomies, the tool generates 52 candidate metrics. We assess each metric against 118 components collected from 18 open-source repositories, relying on maintenance activity derived from version history as an empirical proxy for complexity, and discard any metric that loses significance once code size is accounted for. Only ten metrics withstand this test. Seven belong to our newly introduced set; rather than measuring sheer volume, each tallies structurally distinct elements, such as LLM call sites, memory attributes, and prompt templates, an attribute we call structural breadth. Of the three surviving conventional metrics, RFC exhibits a similar breadth-oriented character, while Halstead N and V survive only as a residual effect of size; our top-performing metrics exceed all three. Crucially, the prompt-layer metrics retain significance even when the strongest code-level metric is added as a covariate, establishing prompt complexity as a dimension in its own right. A final validation on 20 components spanning six held-out repositories shows that the two best-performing metrics continue to predict maintenance effort, supporting their generalizability beyond the training set.

</details>


### [313] [ContextSniper: AntTrail's Token-Efficient Code Memory for Repository-Level Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01916)
*Chiwang Luk,Matin Mohammad Najafi,Zhifeng Jia,Wei Yang,Xiuchang Li,Jinwei Zhu,Yang Ren,Lei Chen,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.AI
